#pragma once

#define MAX_BONES        256 // スキニングのボーン数の上限
#define MAX_POINT_LIGHTS 64  // ポイントライトの数の上限

#define SHADOW_CASCADE_COUNT 3   // カスケードシャドウの段数


===== ShadowMap.cpp =====
Path: C:\Users\offri\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Engine\Rendering\ShadowMap.cpp
#include "ShadowMap.h"
#include <cassert>

// 初期化
void ShadowMap::Initialize(ID3D11Device* device)
{
	HRESULT hr;

	// 本体：段数ぶんの配列テクスチャ
	{
		D3D11_TEXTURE2D_DESC desc{};
		desc.Width = RESOLUTION;
		desc.Height = RESOLUTION;
		desc.MipLevels = 1;
		desc.ArraySize = CASCADE_COUNT;
		desc.Format = DXGI_FORMAT_R32_TYPELESS;
		desc.SampleDesc.Count = 1;
		desc.Usage = D3D11_USAGE_DEFAULT;
		desc.BindFlags = D3D11_BIND_DEPTH_STENCIL | D3D11_BIND_SHADER_RESOURCE;
		hr = device->CreateTexture2D(&desc, nullptr, texture.GetAddressOf());
		assert(SUCCEEDED(hr));
	}

	// 書くとき：段ごとに DSV を作る
	for (UINT i = 0; i < CASCADE_COUNT; i++)
	{
		D3D11_DEPTH_STENCIL_VIEW_DESC desc{};
		desc.Format = DXGI_FORMAT_D32_FLOAT;
		desc.ViewDimension = D3D11_DSV_DIMENSION_TEXTURE2DARRAY;
		desc.Texture2DArray.MipSlice = 0;
		desc.Texture2DArray.FirstArraySlice = i;
		desc.Texture2DArray.ArraySize = 1;
		hr = device->CreateDepthStencilView(texture.Get(), &desc, dsvs[i].GetAddressOf());
		assert(SUCCEEDED(hr));
	}

	// 読むとき：全段まとめて1つの SRV
	{
		D3D11_SHADER_RESOURCE_VIEW_DESC desc{};
		desc.Format = DXGI_FORMAT_R32_FLOAT;
		desc.ViewDimension = D3D11_SRV_DIMENSION_TEXTURE2DARRAY;
		desc.Texture2DArray.MostDetailedMip = 0;
		desc.Texture2DArray.MipLevels = 1;
		desc.Texture2DArray.FirstArraySlice = 0;
		desc.Texture2DArray.ArraySize = CASCADE_COUNT;
		hr = device->CreateShaderResourceView(texture.Get(), &desc, srv.GetAddressOf());
		assert(SUCCEEDED(hr));
	}

	// 比較サンプラー：「読んだ深度 と 自分の深度」の比較を GPU にやらせる
	{
		D3D11_SAMPLER_DESC desc{};
		desc.Filter = D3D11_FILTER_COMPARISON_MIN_MAG_LINEAR_MIP_POINT;
		desc.AddressU = D3D11_TEXTURE_ADDRESS_BORDER;
		desc.AddressV = D3D11_TEXTURE_ADDRESS_BORDER;
		desc.AddressW = D3D11_TEXTURE_ADDRESS_BORDER;
		desc.ComparisonFunc = D3D11_COMPARISON_LESS_EQUAL;
		desc.BorderColor[0] = 1.0f;
		desc.BorderColor[1] = 1.0f;
		desc.BorderColor[2] = 1.0f;
		desc.BorderColor[3] = 1.0f;
		desc.MinLOD = 0;
		desc.MaxLOD = D3D11_FLOAT32_MAX;
		hr = device->CreateSamplerState(&desc, comparisonSampler.GetAddressOf());
		assert(SUCCEEDED(hr));
	}

	// 影を描くときのラスタライザー
	{
		D3D11_RASTERIZER_DESC desc{};
		desc.FillMode = D3D11_FILL_SOLID;
		desc.CullMode = D3D11_CULL_BACK;
		desc.FrontCounterClockwise = FALSE;
		desc.DepthBias = 0;                // ステップ2で調整
		desc.SlopeScaledDepthBias = 0.0f;  // ステップ2で調整
		desc.DepthBiasClamp = 0.0f;
		desc.DepthClipEnable = FALSE;      // ライトの手前にはみ出た物も潰して描く
		hr = device->CreateRasterizerState(&desc, rasterizerState.GetAddressOf());
		assert(SUCCEEDED(hr));
	}
}

// 指定した段を描画先にする
void ShadowMap::BeginCascade(ID3D11DeviceContext* dc, UINT cascadeIndex)
{
	assert(cascadeIndex < CASCADE_COUNT);

	// 深度だけ
	dc->OMSetRenderTargets(0, nullptr, dsvs[cascadeIndex].Get());
	dc->ClearDepthStencilView(dsvs[cascadeIndex].Get(), D3D11_CLEAR_DEPTH, 1.0f, 0);

	D3D11_VIEWPORT vp{};
	vp.Width = static_cast<float>(RESOLUTION);
	vp.Height = static_cast<float>(RESOLUTION);
	vp.MinDepth = 0.0f;
	vp.MaxDepth = 1.0f;
	dc->RSSetViewports(1, &vp);
}

// 描画先を外す
void ShadowMap::End(ID3D11DeviceContext* dc)
{
	dc->OMSetRenderTargets(0, nullptr, nullptr);
}



===== ShadowMap.h =====
Path: C:\Users\offri\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Engine\Rendering\ShadowMap.h
#pragma once
#include <d3d11.h>
#include <wrl.h>
#include "Engine/Rendering/ShaderLimits.h"

// カスケードシャドウ用の影マップ
class ShadowMap
{
public:
	static constexpr UINT CASCADE_COUNT = SHADOW_CASCADE_COUNT; // カスケードの段数
	static constexpr UINT RESOLUTION = 2048;  // 1段あたりの縦横ピクセル数

	// 初期化
	void Initialize(ID3D11Device* device);

	// 指定した段を描画先にする
	void BeginCascade(ID3D11DeviceContext* dc, UINT cascadeIndex);

	// 描画先を外す
	void End(ID3D11DeviceContext* dc);

	ID3D11ShaderResourceView* GetSRV() const { return srv.Get(); }
	ID3D11SamplerState* GetComparisonSampler() const { return comparisonSampler.Get(); }
	ID3D11RasterizerState* GetRasterizerState() const { return rasterizerState.Get(); }

private:
	Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<ID3D11Texture2D>          texture;             // 本体（配列テクスチャ）
	Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<ID3D11DepthStencilView>   dsvs[CASCADE_COUNT]; // 書くとき：段ごとに1枚
	Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<ID3D11ShaderResourceView> srv;                 // 読むとき：全段まとめて1つ
	Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<ID3D11SamplerState>       comparisonSampler;   // 影判定用
	Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<ID3D11RasterizerState>    rasterizerState;     // 影を描くとき用
};


===== ShapeRenderer.cpp =====
Path: C:\Users\offri\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Engine\Rendering\ShapeRenderer.cpp
#include "ShapeRenderer.h"
#include "Engine/Resource/GpuResourceUtils.h"
#include <algorithm>

static const float PI = DirectX::XM_PI;
static const int   SUBDIVISIONS = 16;

ShapeRenderer::ShapeRenderer(ID3D11Device* device)
{
	// Wiredシェーダー
	{
		D3D11_INPUT_ELEMENT_DESC layout[] =
		{
			{ "POSITION", 0, DXGI_FORMAT_R32G32B32_FLOAT,    0, 0,  D3D11_INPUT_PER_VERTEX_DATA,   0 },
			{ "WORLD",    0, DXGI_FORMAT_R32G32B32A32_FLOAT, 1, 0,  D3D11_INPUT_PER_INSTANCE_DATA, 1 },
			{ "WORLD",    1, DXGI_FORMAT_R32G32B32A32_FLOAT, 1, 16, D3D11_INPUT_PER_INSTANCE_DATA, 1 },
			{ "WORLD",    2, DXGI_FORMAT_R32G32B32A32_FLOAT, 1, 32, D3D11_INPUT_PER_INSTANCE_DATA, 1 },
			{ "WORLD",    3, DXGI_FORMAT_R32G32B32A32_FLOAT, 1, 48, D3D11_INPUT_PER_INSTANCE_DATA, 1 },
			{ "COLOR",    1, DXGI_FORMAT_R32G32B32A32_FLOAT, 1, 64, D3D11_INPUT_PER_INSTANCE_DATA, 1 },
		};

		GpuResourceUtils::LoadVertexShader(device, "Data/Shader/WiredShape_VS.cso", layout, _countof(layout), wiredInputLayout, wiredVertexShader);
		GpuResourceUtils::LoadPixelShader(device, "Data/Shader/WiredShape_PS.cso", wiredPixelShader);
		GpuResourceUtils::CreateConstantBuffer(device, sizeof(CbWiredMesh), wiredConstantBuffer);
	}

	// Solidシェーダー
	{
		D3D11_INPUT_ELEMENT_DESC layout[] =
		{
			{ "POSITION", 0, DXGI_FORMAT_R32G32B32_FLOAT,    0, 0,                            D3D11_INPUT_PER_VERTEX_DATA,   0 },
			{ "NORMAL",   0, DXGI_FORMAT_R32G32B32_FLOAT,    0, D3D11_APPEND_ALIGNED_ELEMENT, D3D11_INPUT_PER_VERTEX_DATA,   0 },
			{ "WORLD",    0, DXGI_FORMAT_R32G32B32A32_FLOAT, 1, 0,                            D3D11_INPUT_PER_INSTANCE_DATA, 1 },
			{ "WORLD",    1, DXGI_FORMAT_R32G32B32A32_FLOAT, 1, 16,                           D3D11_INPUT_PER_INSTANCE_DATA, 1 },
			{ "WORLD",    2, DXGI_FORMAT_R32G32B32A32_FLOAT, 1, 32,                           D3D11_INPUT_PER_INSTANCE_DATA, 1 },
			{ "WORLD",    3, DXGI_FORMAT_R32G32B32A32_FLOAT, 1, 48,                           D3D11_INPUT_PER_INSTANCE_DATA, 1 },
			{ "COLOR",    1, DXGI_FORMAT_R32G32B32A32_FLOAT, 1, 64,                           D3D11_INPUT_PER_INSTANCE_DATA, 1 },
		};
		GpuResourceUtils::LoadVertexShader(device, "Data/Shader/SolidShape_VS.cso", layout, _countof(layout), solidInputLayout, solidVertexShader);
		GpuResourceUtils::LoadPixelShader(device, "Data/Shader/SolidShape_PS.cso", solidPixelShader);
		GpuResourceUtils::CreateConstantBuffer(device, sizeof(CbSolidMesh), solidConstantBuffer);
	}

	// 単位メッシュを事前生成
	CreateWiredBoxMesh(device);
	CreateSolidBoxMesh(device);
	CreateWiredSphereMesh(device, SUBDIVISIONS);
	CreateSolidSphereMesh(device, SUBDIVISIONS);
	CreateWiredHalfSphereMesh(device, SUBDIVISIONS);
	CreateSolidHalfSphereMesh(device, SUBDIVISIONS);
	CreateWiredCylinderMesh(device, SUBDIVISIONS);
	CreateSolidCylinderMesh(device, SUBDIVISIONS);

	// 不透明描画用ステート生成
	CreateRenderStates(device);
}

// 箱描画
void ShapeRenderer::DrawBox(
	const DirectX::XMFLOAT4X4& transform,
	const DirectX::XMFLOAT3& size,
	const DirectX::XMFLOAT4& color)
{
	DirectX::XMMATRIX S = DirectX::XMMatrixScaling(size.x, size.y, size.z);
	DirectX::XMMATRIX W = DirectX::XMLoadFloat4x4(&transform);

	Instance inst;
	inst.wiredMesh = &wiredBoxMesh;
	inst.solidMesh = &solidBoxMesh;
	DirectX::XMStoreFloat4x4(&inst.worldTransform, S * W);
	inst.color = color;
	instances.push_back(inst);
}
// 球描画
void ShapeRenderer::DrawSphere(
	const DirectX::XMFLOAT3& center,
	float radius,
	const DirectX::XMFLOAT4& color)
{
	DirectX::XMMATRIX S = DirectX::XMMatrixScaling(radius, radius, radius);
	DirectX::XMMATRIX T = DirectX::XMMatrixTranslation(center.x, center.y, center.z);

	Instance inst;
	inst.wiredMesh = &wiredSphereMesh;
	inst.solidMesh = &solidSphereMesh;
	DirectX::XMStoreFloat4x4(&inst.worldTransform, S * T);
	inst.color = color;
	instances.push_back(inst);
}
// カプセル描画
void ShapeRenderer::DrawCapsule(
	const DirectX::XMFLOAT4X4& transform,
	float radius,
	float halfHeight,
	const DirectX::XMFLOAT4& color)
{
	DirectX::XMMATRIX base = DirectX::XMLoadFloat4x4(&transform);
	DirectX::XMMATRIX S = DirectX::XMMatrixScaling(radius, radius, radius);
	DirectX::XMMATRIX flip = DirectX::XMMatrixRotationX(DirectX::XM_PI);

	// 上半球
	{
		DirectX::XMMATRIX T = DirectX::XMMatrixTranslation(0, halfHeight, 0);
		Instance inst;
		inst.wiredMesh = &wiredHalfSphereMesh;
		inst.solidMesh = &solidHalfSphereMesh;
		DirectX::XMStoreFloat4x4(&inst.worldTransform, S * T * base);
		inst.color = color;
		instances.push_back(inst);
	}
	// 下半球
	{
		DirectX::XMMATRIX T = DirectX::XMMatrixTranslation(0, -halfHeight, 0);
		Instance inst;
		inst.wiredMesh = &wiredHalfSphereMesh;
		inst.solidMesh = &solidHalfSphereMesh;
		DirectX::XMStoreFloat4x4(&inst.worldTransform, S * flip * T * base);
		inst.color = color;
		instances.push_back(inst);
	}
	// 円柱
	{
		DirectX::XMMATRIX cylS = DirectX::XMMatrixScaling(radius, halfHeight * 2.0f, radius);
		Instance inst;
		inst.wiredMesh = &wiredCylinderMesh;
		inst.solidMesh = &solidCylinderMesh;
		DirectX::XMStoreFloat4x4(&inst.worldTransform, cylS * base);
		inst.color = color;
		instances.push_back(inst);
	}
}

// 描画
void ShapeRenderer::Render(
	ID3D11DeviceContext* dc,
	const DirectX::XMFLOAT4X4& view,
	const DirectX::XMFLOAT4X4& projection,
	const DirectX::XMFLOAT3& lightDirection,
	bool drawWired) 
{
	if (instances.empty()) return;

	DirectX::XMMATRIX V = DirectX::XMLoadFloat4x4(&view);
	DirectX::XMMATRIX P = DirectX::XMLoadFloat4x4(&projection);
	DirectX::XMMATRIX VP = V * P;

	// メッシュの種類ごとにグループ化
	struct InstanceGroup
	{
		WiredMesh* wiredMesh;
		SolidMesh* solidMesh;
		UINT instanceOffset;
		UINT instanceCount;
	};

	std::sort(instances.begin(), instances.end(),
		[](const Instance& a, const Instance& b)
		{
			return a.wiredMesh < b.wiredMesh;
		});

	std::vector<InstanceGroup> groups;
	std::vector<InstanceData> instanceData;
	instanceData.reserve(instances.size());

	for (auto& inst : instances)
	{
		// 既存グループを探す
		InstanceGroup* group = nullptr;
		for (auto& g : groups)
		{
			if (g.wiredMesh == inst.wiredMesh)
			{
				group = &g;
				break;
			}
		}
		if (!group)
		{
			groups.push_back({ inst.wiredMesh, inst.solidMesh, static_cast<UINT>(instanceData.size()), 0 });
			group = &groups.back();
		}

		InstanceData data;
		data.world = inst.worldTransform;
		data.color = inst.color;
		instanceData.push_back(data);
		group->instanceCount++;
	}

	// インスタンスバッファへ一括転送
	Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<ID3D11Device> device;
	dc->GetDevice(device.GetAddressOf());
	EnsureInstanceBuffer(device.Get(), static_cast<UINT>(instanceData.size()));

	D3D11_MAPPED_SUBRESOURCE mapped;
	dc->Map(instanceBuffer.Get(), 0, D3D11_MAP_WRITE_DISCARD, 0, &mapped);
	memcpy(mapped.pData, instanceData.data(), sizeof(InstanceData)* instanceData.size());
	dc->Unmap(instanceBuffer.Get(), 0);

	// 描画前に不透明用のステートへ明示的に切り替える
	float blendFactor[4] = { 0, 0, 0, 0 };
	dc->OMSetBlendState(opaqueBlendState.Get(), blendFactor, 0xFFFFFFFF);
	dc->OMSetDepthStencilState(opaqueDepthStencilState.Get(), 0);

	if (drawWired)
	// Wiredパス
	{
		CbWiredMesh cb;
		DirectX::XMStoreFloat4x4(&cb.viewProjection, VP);
		dc->UpdateSubresource(wiredConstantBuffer.Get(), 0, nullptr, &cb, 0, 0);

		dc->VSSetShader(wiredVertexShader.Get(), nullptr, 0);
		dc->PSSetShader(wiredPixelShader.Get(), nullptr, 0);
		dc->IASetInputLayout(wiredInputLayout.Get());
		dc->VSSetConstantBuffers(0, 1, wiredConstantBuffer.GetAddressOf());
		dc->IASetPrimitiveTopology(D3D11_PRIMITIVE_TOPOLOGY_LINELIST);

		for (auto& g : groups)
		{
			ID3D11Buffer* buffers[2] = { g.wiredMesh->vertexBuffer.Get(), instanceBuffer.Get() };
			UINT strides[2] = { sizeof(DirectX::XMFLOAT3), sizeof(InstanceData) };
			UINT offsets[2] = { 0, g.instanceOffset * sizeof(InstanceData) };
			dc->IASetVertexBuffers(0, 2, buffers, strides, offsets);
			dc->DrawInstanced(g.wiredMesh->vertexCount, g.instanceCount, 0, 0);
		}
	}

	// Solidパス
	{
		CbSolidMesh cb;
		DirectX::XMStoreFloat4x4(&cb.viewProjection, VP);
		cb.lightDirection = { lightDirection.x, lightDirection.y, lightDirection.z, 0 };
		dc->UpdateSubresource(solidConstantBuffer.Get(), 0, nullptr, &cb, 0, 0);
		dc->VSSetConstantBuffers(0, 1, solidConstantBuffer.GetAddressOf());
		dc->IASetPrimitiveTopology(D3D11_PRIMITIVE_TOPOLOGY_TRIANGLELIST);

		dc->VSSetShader(solidVertexShader.Get(), nullptr, 0);
		dc->PSSetShader(solidPixelShader.Get(), nullptr, 0);
		dc->IASetInputLayout(solidInputLayout.Get());
		dc->OMSetBlendState(alphaBlendState.Get(), blendFactor, 0xFFFFFFFF);
		dc->OMSetDepthStencilState(opaqueDepthStencilState.Get(), 0);

		for (auto& g : groups)
		{
			ID3D11Buffer* buffers[2] = { g.solidMesh->vertexBuffer.Get(), instanceBuffer.Get() };
			UINT strides[2] = { sizeof(Vertex), sizeof(InstanceData) };
			UINT offsets[2] = { 0, g.instanceOffset * sizeof(InstanceData) };
			dc->IASetVertexBuffers(0, 2, buffers, strides, offsets);
			dc->IASetIndexBuffer(g.solidMesh->indexBuffer.Get(), DXGI_FORMAT_R16_UINT, 0);
			dc->DrawIndexedInstanced(g.solidMesh->indexCount, g.instanceCount, 0, 0, 0);
		}
	}

	instances.clear();
}

// インスタンスバッファの確保・拡張ヘルパー
void ShapeRenderer::EnsureInstanceBuffer(ID3D11Device* device, UINT requiredCount)
{
	// 既に十分な容量があれば何もしない
	if (requiredCount <= instanceBufferCapacity) return;

	// 少し余裕を持たせて確保する(毎フレーム微妙に増減するケースで頻繁に作り直しが起きないようにするため)
	UINT newCapacity = requiredCount + requiredCount / 2;

	D3D11_BUFFER_DESC desc = {};
	desc.ByteWidth = sizeof(InstanceData) * newCapacity;
	desc.Usage = D3D11_USAGE_DYNAMIC;          // CPUから毎フレーム書き込むため
	desc.BindFlags = D3D11_BIND_VERTEX_BUFFER; // インスタンスバッファも「頂点バッファ」として扱う
	desc.CPUAccessFlags = D3D11_CPU_ACCESS_WRITE;

	instanceBuffer.Reset();
	HRESULT hr = device->CreateBuffer(&desc, nullptr, instanceBuffer.GetAddressOf());
	_ASSERT_EXPR(SUCCEEDED(hr), L"ShapeRenderer: InstanceBuffer failed");

	instanceBufferCapacity = newCapacity;
}

// 不透明描画用のレンダーステート生成
void ShapeRenderer::CreateRenderStates(ID3D11Device* device)
{
	// ブレンドなし(不透明、Wired用)
	{
		D3D11_BLEND_DESC desc = {};
		desc.RenderTarget[0].BlendEnable = FALSE;
		desc.RenderTarget[0].RenderTargetWriteMask = D3D11_COLOR_WRITE_ENABLE_RED | D3D11_COLOR_WRITE_ENABLE_GREEN | D3D11_COLOR_WRITE_ENABLE_BLUE;
		HRESULT hr = device->CreateBlendState(&desc, opaqueBlendState.GetAddressOf());
		_ASSERT_EXPR(SUCCEEDED(hr), L"ShapeRenderer: BlendState failed");
	}

	// アルファブレンドあり(半透明、Solid用)
	{
		D3D11_BLEND_DESC desc = {};
		desc.RenderTarget[0].BlendEnable = TRUE;
		desc.RenderTarget[0].SrcBlend = D3D11_BLEND_SRC_ALPHA;
		desc.RenderTarget[0].DestBlend = D3D11_BLEND_INV_SRC_ALPHA;
		desc.RenderTarget[0].BlendOp = D3D11_BLEND_OP_ADD;
		desc.RenderTarget[0].SrcBlendAlpha = D3D11_BLEND_ONE;
		desc.RenderTarget[0].DestBlendAlpha = D3D11_BLEND_ZERO;
		desc.RenderTarget[0].BlendOpAlpha = D3D11_BLEND_OP_ADD;
		desc.RenderTarget[0].RenderTargetWriteMask = D3D11_COLOR_WRITE_ENABLE_RED | D3D11_COLOR_WRITE_ENABLE_GREEN | D3D11_COLOR_WRITE_ENABLE_BLUE;
		HRESULT hr = device->CreateBlendState(&desc, alphaBlendState.GetAddressOf());
		_ASSERT_EXPR(SUCCEEDED(hr), L"ShapeRenderer: AlphaBlendState failed");
	}

	// 深度テスト・書き込みあり
	{
		D3D11_DEPTH_STENCIL_DESC desc = {};
		desc.DepthEnable = TRUE;
		desc.DepthWriteMask = D3D11_DEPTH_WRITE_MASK_ALL;
		desc.DepthFunc = D3D11_COMPARISON_LESS_EQUAL;
		HRESULT hr = device->CreateDepthStencilState(&desc, opaqueDepthStencilState.GetAddressOf());
		_ASSERT_EXPR(SUCCEEDED(hr), L"ShapeRenderer: DepthStencilState failed");
	}
}

// バッファ生成ヘルパー
void ShapeRenderer::CreateWiredMesh(
	ID3D11Device* device,
	const std::vector<DirectX::XMFLOAT3>& positions,
	WiredMesh& mesh)
{
	D3D11_BUFFER_DESC desc = {};
	desc.ByteWidth = static_cast<UINT>(sizeof(DirectX::XMFLOAT3) * positions.size());
	desc.Usage = D3D11_USAGE_IMMUTABLE;
	desc.BindFlags = D3D11_BIND_VERTEX_BUFFER;

	D3D11_SUBRESOURCE_DATA init = {};
	init.pSysMem = positions.data();

	HRESULT hr = device->CreateBuffer(&desc, &init, mesh.vertexBuffer.GetAddressOf());
	_ASSERT_EXPR(SUCCEEDED(hr), L"ShapeRenderer: WiredMesh failed");
	mesh.vertexCount = static_cast<UINT>(positions.size());
}
void ShapeRenderer::CreateSolidMesh(
	ID3D11Device* device,
	const std::vector<Vertex>& vertices,
	const std::vector<uint16_t>& indices,
	SolidMesh& mesh)
{
	{
		D3D11_BUFFER_DESC desc = {};
		desc.ByteWidth = static_cast<UINT>(sizeof(Vertex) * vertices.size());
		desc.Usage = D3D11_USAGE_IMMUTABLE;
		desc.BindFlags = D3D11_BIND_VERTEX_BUFFER;

		D3D11_SUBRESOURCE_DATA init = {};
		init.pSysMem = vertices.data();

		HRESULT hr = device->CreateBuffer(&desc, &init, mesh.vertexBuffer.GetAddressOf());
		_ASSERT_EXPR(SUCCEEDED(hr), L"ShapeRenderer: SolidMesh vertex failed");
	}

	{
		D3D11_BUFFER_DESC desc = {};
		desc.ByteWidth = static_cast<UINT>(sizeof(uint16_t) * indices.size());
		desc.Usage = D3D11_USAGE_IMMUTABLE;
		desc.BindFlags = D3D11_BIND_INDEX_BUFFER;

		D3D11_SUBRESOURCE_DATA init = {};
		init.pSysMem = indices.data();

		HRESULT hr = device->CreateBuffer(&desc, &init, mesh.indexBuffer.GetAddressOf());
		_ASSERT_EXPR(SUCCEEDED(hr), L"ShapeRenderer: SolidMesh index failed");
	}
	mesh.indexCount = static_cast<UINT>(indices.size());
}

// 箱
void ShapeRenderer::CreateWiredBoxMesh(ID3D11Device* device)
{
	float h = 0.5f;
	std::vector<DirectX::XMFLOAT3> p =
	{
		// Bottom
		{-h,-h,-h},{h,-h,-h},  {h,-h,-h},{h,-h,h},
		{h,-h,h},{-h,-h,h},    {-h,-h,h},{-h,-h,-h},
		// Top
		{-h,h,-h},{h,h,-h},    {h,h,-h},{h,h,h},
		{h,h,h},{-h,h,h},      {-h,h,h},{-h,h,-h},
		// Verticals
		{-h,-h,-h},{-h,h,-h},  {h,-h,-h},{h,h,-h},
		{h,-h,h},{h,h,h},      {-h,-h,h},{-h,h,h},
	};
	CreateWiredMesh(device, p, wiredBoxMesh);
}
void ShapeRenderer::CreateSolidBoxMesh(ID3D11Device* device)
{
	float h = 0.5f;
	std::vector<Vertex> verts =
	{
		{{-h,-h,h},  {0,0,1}}, {{h,-h,h},  {0,0,1}},  {{h,h,h},  {0,0,1}},  {{-h,h,h}, {0,0,1}},  // +Z
		{{h,-h,-h},  {0,0,-1}},{{-h,-h,-h},{0,0,-1}}, {{-h,h,-h},{0,0,-1}}, {{h,h,-h}, {0,0,-1}}, // -Z
		{{-h,-h,-h}, {-1,0,0}},{{-h,-h,h}, {-1,0,0}}, {{-h,h,h}, {-1,0,0}}, {{-h,h,-h},{-1,0,0}}, // -X
		{{h,-h,h},   {1,0,0}}, {{h,-h,-h}, {1,0,0}},  {{h,h,-h}, {1,0,0}},  {{h,h,h},  {1,0,0}},  // +X
		{{-h,h,h},   {0,1,0}}, {{h,h,h},   {0,1,0}},  {{h,h,-h}, {0,1,0}},  {{-h,h,-h},{0,1,0}},  // +Y
		{{-h,-h,-h}, {0,-1,0}},{{h,-h,-h}, {0,-1,0}}, {{h,-h,h}, {0,-1,0}}, {{-h,-h,h},{0,-1,0}}, // -Y
	};
	std::vector<uint16_t> idxs;
	for (uint16_t f = 0; f < 6; f++)
	{
		uint16_t b = f * 4;
		idxs.push_back(b); idxs.push_back(b + 1); idxs.push_back(b + 2);
		idxs.push_back(b); idxs.push_back(b + 2); idxs.push_back(b + 3);
	}
	CreateSolidMesh(device, verts, idxs, solidBoxMesh);
}

// 球
void ShapeRenderer::CreateWiredSphereMesh(ID3D11Device* device, int subdivisions)
{
	std::vector<DirectX::XMFLOAT3> p;
	for (int i = 0; i < subdivisions; i++)
	{
		float a0 = 2.0f * PI * i / subdivisions;
		float a1 = 2.0f * PI * (i + 1) / subdivisions;
		// XY平面
		p.push_back({ cosf(a0), sinf(a0), 0 });
		p.push_back({ cosf(a1), sinf(a1), 0 });
		// XZ平面
		p.push_back({ cosf(a0), 0, sinf(a0) });
		p.push_back({ cosf(a1), 0, sinf(a1) });
		// YZ平面
		p.push_back({ 0, sinf(a0), cosf(a0) });
		p.push_back({ 0, sinf(a1), cosf(a1) });
	}
	CreateWiredMesh(device, p, wiredSphereMesh);
}
void ShapeRenderer::CreateSolidSphereMesh(ID3D11Device* device, int subdivisions)
{
	std::vector<Vertex> verts;
	std::vector<uint16_t> idxs;
	int rings = subdivisions / 2;
	int slices = subdivisions;

	for (int r = 0; r <= rings; r++)
	{
		float phi = PI * r / rings - PI * 0.5f;
		float y = sinf(phi);
		float xz = cosf(phi);
		for (int s = 0; s <= slices; s++)
		{
			float theta = 2.0f * PI * s / slices;
			float x = xz * cosf(theta);
			float z = xz * sinf(theta);
			verts.push_back({ {x,y,z},{x,y,z} });
		}
	}
	for (int r = 0; r < rings; r++)
	{
		for (int s = 0; s < slices; s++)
		{
			uint16_t a = (uint16_t)(r * (slices + 1) + s);
			uint16_t b = a + 1;
			uint16_t c = a + (uint16_t)(slices + 1);
			uint16_t d = c + 1;
			idxs.push_back(a); idxs.push_back(c); idxs.push_back(b);
			idxs.push_back(b); idxs.push_back(c); idxs.push_back(d);
		}
	}
	CreateSolidMesh(device, verts, idxs, solidSphereMesh);
}

// 半球
void ShapeRenderer::CreateWiredHalfSphereMesh(ID3D11Device* device, int subdivisions)
{
	std::vector<DirectX::XMFLOAT3> p;
	for (int i = 0; i < subdivisions; i++)
	{
		float a0 = PI * i / subdivisions;
		float a1 = PI * (i + 1) / subdivisions;
		p.push_back({ cosf(a0), sinf(a0), 0 });
		p.push_back({ cosf(a1), sinf(a1), 0 });
		p.push_back({ 0, sinf(a0), cosf(a0) });
		p.push_back({ 0, sinf(a1), cosf(a1) });
	}
	for (int i = 0; i < subdivisions; i++)
	{
		float a0 = 2.0f * PI * i / subdivisions;
		float a1 = 2.0f * PI * (i + 1) / subdivisions;
		p.push_back({ cosf(a0), 0, sinf(a0) });
		p.push_back({ cosf(a1), 0, sinf(a1) });
	}
	CreateWiredMesh(device, p, wiredHalfSphereMesh);
}
void ShapeRenderer::CreateSolidHalfSphereMesh(ID3D11Device* device, int subdivisions)
{
	std::vector<Vertex> verts;
	std::vector<uint16_t> idxs;
	int rings = subdivisions / 4;
	int slices = subdivisions;

	for (int r = 0; r <= rings; r++)
	{
		float phi = (PI * 0.5f) * r / rings;
		float y = sinf(phi);
		float xz = cosf(phi);
		for (int s = 0; s <= slices; s++)
		{
			float theta = 2.0f * PI * s / slices;
			float x = xz * cosf(theta);
			float z = xz * sinf(theta);
			verts.push_back({ {x,y,z},{x,y,z} });
		}
	}
	for (int r = 0; r < rings; r++)
	{
		for (int s = 0; s < slices; s++)
		{
			uint16_t a = (uint16_t)(r * (slices + 1) + s);
			uint16_t b = a + 1;
			uint16_t c = a + (uint16_t)(slices + 1);
			uint16_t d = c + 1;
			idxs.push_back(a); idxs.push_back(c); idxs.push_back(b);
			idxs.push_back(b); idxs.push_back(c); idxs.push_back(d);
		}
	}
	CreateSolidMesh(device, verts, idxs, solidHalfSphereMesh);
}

// 円柱
void ShapeRenderer::CreateWiredCylinderMesh(ID3D11Device* device, int subdivisions)
{
	std::vector<DirectX::XMFLOAT3> p;
	for (int i = 0; i < subdivisions; i++)
	{
		float a0 = 2.0f * PI * i / subdivisions;
		float a1 = 2.0f * PI * (i + 1) / subdivisions;
		// 上リング
		p.push_back({ cosf(a0),  0.5f, sinf(a0) });
		p.push_back({ cosf(a1),  0.5f, sinf(a1) });
		// 下リング
		p.push_back({ cosf(a0), -0.5f, sinf(a0) });
		p.push_back({ cosf(a1), -0.5f, sinf(a1) });
	}
	// 4本縦ライン
	for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++)
	{
		float a = 2.0f * PI * i / 4;
		float x = cosf(a), z = sinf(a);
		p.push_back({ x,  0.5f, z });
		p.push_back({ x, -0.5f, z });
	}
	CreateWiredMesh(device, p, wiredCylinderMesh);
}
void ShapeRenderer::CreateSolidCylinderMesh(ID3D11Device* device, int subdivisions)
{
	std::vector<Vertex> verts;
	std::vector<uint16_t> idxs;

	// 側面のみ
	for (int i = 0; i <= subdivisions; i++)
	{
		float theta = 2.0f * PI * i / subdivisions;
		float x = cosf(theta), z = sinf(theta);
		verts.push_back({ {x,  0.5f, z}, {x, 0, z} });
		verts.push_back({ {x, -0.5f, z}, {x, 0, z} });
	}
	for (int i = 0; i < subdivisions; i++)
	{
		uint16_t t0 = (uint16_t)(i * 2);
		uint16_t b0 = t0 + 1, t1 = t0 + 2, b1 = t0 + 3;
		idxs.push_back(t0); idxs.push_back(t1); idxs.push_back(b0);
		idxs.push_back(t1); idxs.push_back(b1); idxs.push_back(b0);
	}
	CreateSolidMesh(device, verts, idxs, solidCylinderMesh);
}



===== ShapeRenderer.h =====
Path: C:\Users\offri\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Engine\Rendering\ShapeRenderer.h
#pragma once
#include <vector>
#include <wrl.h>
#include <d3d11.h>
#include <DirectXMath.h>

class ShapeRenderer
{
public:
	ShapeRenderer() = default;
	ShapeRenderer(ID3D11Device* device);

	// 箱描画
	void DrawBox(
		const DirectX::XMFLOAT4X4& transform,
		const DirectX::XMFLOAT3& size,
		const DirectX::XMFLOAT4& color);

	// 球描画
	void DrawSphere(
		const DirectX::XMFLOAT3& center,
		float radius,
		const DirectX::XMFLOAT4& color);

	// カプセル描画
	void DrawCapsule(
		const DirectX::XMFLOAT4X4& transform,
		float radius,
		float halfHeight,
		const DirectX::XMFLOAT4& color);

	// 描画実行
	void Render(
		ID3D11DeviceContext* dc,
		const DirectX::XMFLOAT4X4& view,
		const DirectX::XMFLOAT4X4& projection,
		const DirectX::XMFLOAT3& lightDirection,
		bool drawWired = true);
private:
	struct Vertex
	{
		DirectX::XMFLOAT3 position;
		DirectX::XMFLOAT3 normal;
	};

	struct WiredMesh
	{
		Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<ID3D11Buffer> vertexBuffer;
		UINT vertexCount = 0;
	};

	struct SolidMesh
	{
		Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<ID3D11Buffer> vertexBuffer;
		Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<ID3D11Buffer> indexBuffer;
		UINT indexCount = 0;
	};

	struct Instance
	{
		WiredMesh* wiredMesh;
		SolidMesh* solidMesh;
		DirectX::XMFLOAT4X4    worldTransform;
		DirectX::XMFLOAT4      color;
	};

	struct CbWiredMesh
	{
		DirectX::XMFLOAT4X4 viewProjection;
	};

	struct CbSolidMesh
	{
		DirectX::XMFLOAT4X4 viewProjection;
		DirectX::XMFLOAT4   lightDirection;
	};

	// GPUインスタンシング用
	struct InstanceData
	{
		DirectX::XMFLOAT4X4 world;
		DirectX::XMFLOAT4   color;
	};

	// インスタンスバッファの確保・拡張ヘルパー
	void EnsureInstanceBuffer(ID3D11Device* device, UINT requiredCount);

	// 不透明描画用のレンダーステート生成
	void CreateRenderStates(ID3D11Device* device);

	// バッファ生成ヘルパー
	void CreateWiredMesh(
		ID3D11Device* device,
		const std::vector<DirectX::XMFLOAT3>& positions,
		WiredMesh& mesh);

	void CreateSolidMesh(
		ID3D11Device* device,
		const std::vector<Vertex>& vertices,
		const std::vector<uint16_t>& indices,
		SolidMesh& mesh);

	// 箱
	void CreateWiredBoxMesh(ID3D11Device* device);
	void CreateSolidBoxMesh(ID3D11Device* device);

	// 球
	void CreateWiredSphereMesh(ID3D11Device* device, int subdivisions);
	void CreateSolidSphereMesh(ID3D11Device* device, int subdivisions);

	// 半球
	void CreateWiredHalfSphereMesh(ID3D11Device* device, int subdivisions);
	void CreateSolidHalfSphereMesh(ID3D11Device* device, int subdivisions);

	// 円柱
	void CreateWiredCylinderMesh(ID3D11Device* device, int subdivisions);
	void CreateSolidCylinderMesh(ID3D11Device* device, int subdivisions);

private:
	// 単位サイズのメッシュ
	WiredMesh wiredBoxMesh;
	WiredMesh wiredSphereMesh;
	WiredMesh wiredHalfSphereMesh;
	WiredMesh wiredCylinderMesh;

	SolidMesh solidBoxMesh;
	SolidMesh solidSphereMesh;
	SolidMesh solidHalfSphereMesh;
	SolidMesh solidCylinderMesh;

	std::vector<Instance> instances;

	// GPUインスタンシング用バッファ
	Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<ID3D11Buffer> instanceBuffer;
	UINT instanceBufferCapacity = 0;

	// 不透明描画用ステート(Wired用)
	Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<ID3D11BlendState>        opaqueBlendState;
	Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<ID3D11DepthStencilState> opaqueDepthStencilState;

	// 半透明描画用ステート(Solid用)
	Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<ID3D11BlendState>        alphaBlendState;

	// Wiredシェーダー
	Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<ID3D11VertexShader> wiredVertexShader;
	Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<ID3D11PixelShader>  wiredPixelShader;
	Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<ID3D11InputLayout>  wiredInputLayout;
	Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<ID3D11Buffer>       wiredConstantBuffer;

	// Solidシェーダー
	Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<ID3D11VertexShader> solidVertexShader;
	Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<ID3D11PixelShader>  solidPixelShader;
	Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<ID3D11InputLayout>  solidInputLayout;
	Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<ID3D11Buffer>       solidConstantBuffer;
};


===== SkyBox.cpp =====
Path: C:\Users\offri\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Engine\Rendering\SkyBox.cpp
#include "SkyBox.h"
#include "Engine/Resource/GpuResourceUtils.h"
#include "Engine/Utility/misc.h"
#include <filesystem>

void SkyBox::Initialize(
	ID3D11Device* device,
	const std::string& folderPath) 
{
	// 末尾にスラッシュがなければ追加
	std::string folder = folderPath;
	if (!folder.empty() && folder.back() != '/' && folder.back() != '\\')
		folder += '/';

	// キューブマップ読み込み
	GpuResourceUtils::LoadDDSTexture(
		device, folder + "specular_pmrem.dds", skyTexture);

	GpuResourceUtils::LoadDDSTexture(
		device, folder + "diffuse_iem.dds", diffuseIem);

	GpuResourceUtils::LoadDDSTexture(
		device, folder + "lut_ggx.dds", lutGgx);

	// スカイボックス描画用（skybox.ddsがあれば使う、無かったらspecular_pmrem.ddsを使う）
	if (std::filesystem::exists(folder + "skybox.dds"))
	{
		GpuResourceUtils::LoadDDSTexture(
			device, folder + "skybox.dds", skyBoxTexture);
	}
	else
	{
		skyBoxTexture = skyTexture;
	}

	// 入力レイアウト（POSITIONのみ）
	const D3D11_INPUT_ELEMENT_DESC layout[] =
	{
		{ "POSITION", 0, DXGI_FORMAT_R32G32B32_FLOAT, 0, 0,
		  D3D11_INPUT_PER_VERTEX_DATA, 0 },
	};

	// 頂点シェーダー読み込み
	GpuResourceUtils::LoadVertexShader(
		device,
		"Data/Shader/SkyBox_VS.cso",
		layout, 1,
		inputLayout,
		vertexShader
	);

	// ピクセルシェーダー読み込み
	GpuResourceUtils::LoadPixelShader(
		device,
		"Data/Shader/SkyBox_PS.cso",
		pixelShader
	);

	// 定数バッファ生成
	GpuResourceUtils::CreateConstantBuffer(
		device,
		sizeof(CbSkyBox),
		cbSkyBox
	);

	// サンプラーステート生成
	D3D11_SAMPLER_DESC samplerDesc{};
	samplerDesc.Filter = D3D11_FILTER_MIN_MAG_MIP_LINEAR;
	//samplerDesc.AddressU = D3D11_TEXTURE_ADDRESS_WRAP;
	//samplerDesc.AddressV = D3D11_TEXTURE_ADDRESS_WRAP;
	//samplerDesc.AddressW = D3D11_TEXTURE_ADDRESS_WRAP;
	samplerDesc.AddressU = D3D11_TEXTURE_ADDRESS_CLAMP;
	samplerDesc.AddressV = D3D11_TEXTURE_ADDRESS_CLAMP;
	samplerDesc.AddressW = D3D11_TEXTURE_ADDRESS_CLAMP;
	samplerDesc.ComparisonFunc = D3D11_COMPARISON_NEVER;
	samplerDesc.MinLOD = 0;
	samplerDesc.MaxLOD = D3D11_FLOAT32_MAX;
	device->CreateSamplerState(&samplerDesc, samplerState.GetAddressOf());

	// 深度ステンシルステート生成
	D3D11_DEPTH_STENCIL_DESC dsDesc{};
	dsDesc.DepthEnable = TRUE;
	dsDesc.DepthWriteMask = D3D11_DEPTH_WRITE_MASK_ZERO; // 書き込みOFF
	dsDesc.DepthFunc = D3D11_COMPARISON_LESS_EQUAL; // LESS_EQUALで1.0を通す
	device->CreateDepthStencilState(&dsDesc, depthStencilState.GetAddressOf());

	// 単位立方体の頂点データ
	const DirectX::XMFLOAT3 vertices[] =
	{
		{ -1.0f,  1.0f, -1.0f },
		{  1.0f,  1.0f, -1.0f },
		{  1.0f,  1.0f,  1.0f },
		{ -1.0f,  1.0f,  1.0f },
		{ -1.0f, -1.0f, -1.0f },
		{  1.0f, -1.0f, -1.0f },
		{  1.0f, -1.0f,  1.0f },
		{ -1.0f, -1.0f,  1.0f },
	};

	// インデックスデータ
	const UINT indices[] =
	{
		0, 1, 2,  0, 2, 3, // 上面
		4, 6, 5,  4, 7, 6, // 下面
		0, 4, 5,  0, 5, 1, // 前面
		2, 6, 7,  2, 7, 3, // 後面
		0, 3, 7,  0, 7, 4, // 左面
		1, 5, 6,  1, 6, 2, // 右面
	};
	indexCount = ARRAYSIZE(indices);

	// 頂点バッファ生成
	D3D11_BUFFER_DESC vbDesc{};
	vbDesc.ByteWidth = sizeof(vertices);
	vbDesc.Usage = D3D11_USAGE_DEFAULT;
	vbDesc.BindFlags = D3D11_BIND_VERTEX_BUFFER;

	D3D11_SUBRESOURCE_DATA vbData{};
	vbData.pSysMem = vertices;
	device->CreateBuffer(&vbDesc, &vbData, vertexBuffer.GetAddressOf());

	// インデックスバッファ生成
	D3D11_BUFFER_DESC ibDesc{};
	ibDesc.ByteWidth = sizeof(indices);
	ibDesc.Usage = D3D11_USAGE_DEFAULT;
	ibDesc.BindFlags = D3D11_BIND_INDEX_BUFFER;

	D3D11_SUBRESOURCE_DATA ibData{};
	ibData.pSysMem = indices;
	device->CreateBuffer(&ibDesc, &ibData, indexBuffer.GetAddressOf());
}

void SkyBox::Render(
	ID3D11DeviceContext* dc,
	const DirectX::XMFLOAT4X4& view,
	const DirectX::XMFLOAT4X4& projection,
	const DirectX::XMFLOAT3& cameraPosition)
{
	if (!enabled) return;
	if (!skyTexture) return;

	// 定数バッファ更新
	CbSkyBox cbData{};
	cbData.view = view;
	cbData.projection = projection;
	cbData.cameraPosition = { cameraPosition.x, cameraPosition.y,cameraPosition.z, 0.0f };
	dc->UpdateSubresource(cbSkyBox.Get(), 0, nullptr, &cbData, 0, 0);

	// シェーダー設定
	dc->VSSetShader(vertexShader.Get(), nullptr, 0);
	dc->PSSetShader(pixelShader.Get(), nullptr, 0);
	dc->IASetInputLayout(inputLayout.Get());

	// 定数バッファ設定
	dc->VSSetConstantBuffers(0, 1, cbSkyBox.GetAddressOf());

	// テクスチャ設定（t10スロット）
	//dc->PSSetShaderResources(10, 1, skyTexture.GetAddressOf());
	dc->PSSetShaderResources(10, 1, skyBoxTexture.GetAddressOf());
	dc->PSSetSamplers(0, 1, samplerState.GetAddressOf());

	// 深度ステート設定（書き込みOFF）
	dc->OMSetDepthStencilState(depthStencilState.Get(), 0);

	// 頂点・インデックスバッファ設定
	UINT stride = sizeof(DirectX::XMFLOAT3);
	UINT offset = 0;
	dc->IASetVertexBuffers(0, 1, vertexBuffer.GetAddressOf(), &stride, &offset);
	dc->IASetIndexBuffer(indexBuffer.Get(), DXGI_FORMAT_R32_UINT, 0);
	dc->IASetPrimitiveTopology(D3D11_PRIMITIVE_TOPOLOGY_TRIANGLELIST);

	// 描画
	dc->DrawIndexed(indexCount, 0, 0);
}


===== SkyBox.h =====
Path: C:\Users\offri\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Engine\Rendering\SkyBox.h
#pragma once
#include <d3d11.h>
#include <wrl.h>
#include <DirectXMath.h>
#include <string>

class SkyBox
{
public:
    // 初期化
    void Initialize(ID3D11Device* device, const std::string& folderPath);

    // 描画
    void Render(
        ID3D11DeviceContext* dc,
        const DirectX::XMFLOAT4X4& view,
        const DirectX::XMFLOAT4X4& projection,
        const DirectX::XMFLOAT3& cameraPosition
    );

    // SRVゲッター
    ID3D11ShaderResourceView* GetSRV() const { return skyTexture.Get(); }
    ID3D11ShaderResourceView* GetDiffuseIem()  const { return diffuseIem.Get(); }
    ID3D11ShaderResourceView* GetLutGgx()      const { return lutGgx.Get(); }

    // IBL ON/OFF
    bool enabled = true;

private:
    struct CbSkyBox
    {
        DirectX::XMFLOAT4X4 view;
        DirectX::XMFLOAT4X4 projection;
        DirectX::XMFLOAT4   cameraPosition;
        // 16バイトアライメント用パディング
        DirectX::XMFLOAT4   padding;
    };


    // 頂点シェーダー
    Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<ID3D11VertexShader>       vertexShader;
    // ピクセルシェーダー
    Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<ID3D11PixelShader>        pixelShader;
    // 入力レイアウト
    Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<ID3D11InputLayout>        inputLayout;
    // 頂点バッファ
    Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<ID3D11Buffer>             vertexBuffer;
    // インデックスバッファ
    Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<ID3D11Buffer>             indexBuffer;
    // キューブマップテクスチャ
    Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<ID3D11ShaderResourceView> skyTexture;
    // スカイボックス描画用（skybox.ddsがあればこれを使用）
    Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<ID3D11ShaderResourceView> skyBoxTexture;
    // サンプラーステート
    Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<ID3D11SamplerState>       samplerState;
    // 深度ステンシルステート（深度書き込みOFF）
    Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<ID3D11DepthStencilState>  depthStencilState;
    // 定数バッファ
    Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<ID3D11Buffer>             cbSkyBox;

    UINT indexCount = 0;

    // IBL用テクスチャ
    Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<ID3D11ShaderResourceView> diffuseIem;
    Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<ID3D11ShaderResourceView> lutGgx;
};


===== AnimationEditBaker.cpp =====
Path: C:\Users\offri\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Engine\Resource\AnimationEditBaker.cpp
#include "AnimationEditBaker.h"
#include <algorithm>
#include <DirectXMath.h>

namespace
{
	// 指定時刻の値を補間で求める
	DirectX::XMFLOAT3 SampleVector(const std::vector<Model::VectorKeyframe>& keys, float time)
	{
		if (time <= keys.front().seconds) return keys.front().value;

		for (size_t i = 0; i + 1 < keys.size(); i++)
		{
			const auto& a = keys[i];
			const auto& b = keys[i + 1];
			if (time <= b.seconds)
			{
				const float span = b.seconds - a.seconds;
				const float t = (span > 0.0f) ? (time - a.seconds) / span : 0.0f;
				DirectX::XMFLOAT3 result;
				DirectX::XMStoreFloat3(&result, DirectX::XMVectorLerp(DirectX::XMLoadFloat3(&a.value), DirectX::XMLoadFloat3(&b.value), t));
				return result;
			}
		}
		return keys.back().value;
	}

	// 指定時刻の値を補間で求める
	DirectX::XMFLOAT4 SampleQuaternion(const std::vector<Model::QuaternionKeyframe>& keys, float time)
	{
		if (time <= keys.front().seconds) return keys.front().value;

		for (size_t i = 0; i + 1 < keys.size(); i++)
		{
			const auto& a = keys[i];
			const auto& b = keys[i + 1];
			if (time <= b.seconds)
			{
				const float span = b.seconds - a.seconds;
				const float t = (span > 0.0f) ? (time - a.seconds) / span : 0.0f;
				DirectX::XMFLOAT4 result;
				DirectX::XMStoreFloat4(&result, DirectX::XMQuaternionSlerp(DirectX::XMLoadFloat4(&a.value), DirectX::XMLoadFloat4(&b.value), t));
				return result;
			}
		}
		return keys.back().value;
	}

	// キー列を start〜end だけ残して、0秒始まりに詰める
	template<class Key, class Sampler>
	void TrimKeys(std::vector<Key>& keys, float start, float end, Sampler sample)
	{
		if (keys.empty()) return;

		// 1キーだけなら時間に関係なく同じ値なので、時刻だけ0にする
		if (keys.size() == 1)
		{
			keys[0].seconds = 0.0f;
			return;
		}

		std::vector<Key> result;

		// 切り口の値は補間で作る
		result.push_back({ 0.0f, sample(keys, start) });
		for (const auto& key : keys)
		{
			if (key.seconds > start && key.seconds < end)
			{
				result.push_back({ key.seconds - start, key.value });
			}
		}
		result.push_back({ end - start, sample(keys, end) });

		keys = std::move(result);
	}
}


std::vector<Model::Animation> AnimationEditBaker::Bake(
	const std::vector<Model::Animation>& rawAnimations,
	const std::vector<Model::Node>& nodes,
	const AnimationEditData& editData)
{
	const int rootIndex = FindNodeIndex(nodes, editData.rootNodeName);

	std::vector<Model::Animation> result;
	result.reserve(rawAnimations.size());

	for (const auto& raw : rawAnimations)
	{
		result.push_back(BakeClip(raw, nodes, rootIndex, editData.FindClip(raw.name)));
	}

	return result;
}

// 1クリップ分だけ焼く
Model::Animation AnimationEditBaker::BakeClip(
	const Model::Animation& rawAnimation,
	const std::vector<Model::Node>& nodes,
	int rootIndex,
	const AnimationEditClipData* clip)
{
	// 生データをコピーして、そこに加工を加える
	Model::Animation animation = rawAnimation;

	// カット
	if (clip)
	{
		const float length = animation.secondsLength;
		const float start = std::clamp(clip->trimStart, 0.0f, length);
		const float end = (clip->trimEnd < 0.0f) ? length : std::clamp(clip->trimEnd, start, length);

		// 範囲が有効で、実際に切る部分があるときだけ
		if (end - start > 0.0f && (start > 0.0f || end < length))
		{
			TrimAnimation(animation, start, end);
		}
	}

	// ここから先はルートだけを加工する
	if (rootIndex < 0 || rootIndex >= (int)animation.nodeAnims.size()) return animation;
	Model::NodeAnim& rootAnim = animation.nodeAnims[rootIndex];
	const Model::Node& rootNode = nodes[rootIndex];

	// オフセット除去
	RemoveRootOffset(rootAnim, rootNode);

	if (!clip) return animation;

	// 区間の移動量
	if (!clip->sectionsXZ.empty() || !clip->sectionsY.empty())
	{
		ApplySections(animation, rootIndex, clip->sectionsXZ, clip->sectionsY);
	}

	// 向きの補正
	if (clip->yawOffsetDegrees != 0.0f)
	{
		ApplyYawOffset(rootAnim, rootNode, clip->yawOffsetDegrees);
	}

	return animation;
}


// RootNodeの名前を探す
std::string AnimationEditBaker::FindDefaultRootNodeName(const Model& model)
{
	const auto& nodes = model.GetNodes();
	const std::vector<int> bones = CollectBoneIndices(model);

	auto isBone = [&](int index)
		{
			return std::find(bones.begin(), bones.end(), index) != bones.end();
		};

	// 親がいない、または親がボーンではないボーンが一番上
	for (int i : bones)
	{
		const int parent = nodes[i].parentIndex;
		if (parent < 0 || !isBone(parent))
		{
			return nodes[i].name;
		}
	}

	return "";
}

// スキンのボーンになっているノードの番号の一覧
std::vector<int> AnimationEditBaker::CollectBoneIndices(const Model& model)
{
	const auto& nodes = model.GetNodes();

	// どのノードがボーンかを調べる
	std::vector<bool> isBone(nodes.size(), false);
	for (const auto& mesh : model.GetMeshes())
	{
		for (const auto& bone : mesh.bones)
		{
			if (bone.nodeIndex >= 0 && bone.nodeIndex < (int)nodes.size())
			{
				isBone[bone.nodeIndex] = true;
			}
		}
	}

	std::vector<int> result;
	for (int i = 0; i < (int)nodes.size(); i++)
	{
		if (isBone[i]) result.push_back(i);
	}
	return result;
}

// 名前からルートノードの番号を探す
int AnimationEditBaker::FindNodeIndex(const std::vector<Model::Node>& nodes, const std::string& name)
{
	for (int i = 0; i < (int)nodes.size(); i++)
	{
		if (nodes[i].name == name) return i;
	}
	return -1;
}

// 指定範囲だけ残して、時間を0秒始まりに詰める
void AnimationEditBaker::TrimAnimation(Model::Animation& animation, float start, float end)
{
	for (auto& nodeAnim : animation.nodeAnims)
	{
		TrimKeys(nodeAnim.positionKeyframes, start, end, SampleVector);
		TrimKeys(nodeAnim.rotationKeyframes, start, end, SampleQuaternion);
		TrimKeys(nodeAnim.scaleKeyframes, start, end, SampleVector);
	}
	animation.secondsLength = end - start;

	// 時刻の対応表
	animation.bakedTimes = { 0.0f, end - start };
	animation.rawTimes = { start, end };
}

// 0フレーム目のルート位置が、バインドポーズの位置になるようにずらす
void AnimationEditBaker::RemoveRootOffset(Model::NodeAnim& rootAnim, const Model::Node& rootNode)
{
	if (rootAnim.positionKeyframes.empty()) return;

	// 0フレーム目がバインドポーズからどれだけずれているか
	const DirectX::XMFLOAT3& first = rootAnim.positionKeyframes.front().value;
	const DirectX::XMFLOAT3 offset =
	{
		first.x - rootNode.position.x,
		first.y - rootNode.position.y,
		first.z - rootNode.position.z
	};

	// 全キーから同じ量を引く
	for (auto& key : rootAnim.positionKeyframes)
	{
		key.value.x -= offset.x;
		key.value.y -= offset.y;
		key.value.z -= offset.z;
	}
}

// 区間ごとに、ルートの移動量に倍率を掛ける
void AnimationEditBaker::ApplySections(
	Model::Animation& animation,
	int rootIndex,
	const std::vector<AnimationEditSection>& sectionsXZ,
	const std::vector<AnimationEditSection>& sectionsY)
{
	auto& keys = animation.nodeAnims[rootIndex].positionKeyframes;
	if (keys.size() < 2) return;

	// その時刻を含む区間の倍率
	auto findScale = [](const std::vector<AnimationEditSection>& sections, float time)
		{
			for (const auto& s : sections)
			{
				if (time >= s.start && time < s.end) return s.scale;
			}
			return 1.0f;
		};

	// 元の位置を控えておく
	std::vector<DirectX::XMFLOAT3> original(keys.size());
	for (size_t i = 0; i < keys.size(); i++) original[i] = keys[i].value;

	for (size_t i = 1; i < keys.size(); i++)
	{
		// このキー間の真ん中の時刻を、生データの時刻に直す
		const float mid = animation.ToRawTime((keys[i - 1].seconds + keys[i].seconds) * 0.5f);

		const float scaleXZ = findScale(sectionsXZ, mid);
		const float scaleY = findScale(sectionsY, mid);

		// 元の移動量に倍率を掛けて、ひとつ前のキーに積み上げる
		keys[i].value.x = keys[i - 1].value.x + (original[i].x - original[i - 1].x) * scaleXZ;
		keys[i].value.y = keys[i - 1].value.y + (original[i].y - original[i - 1].y) * scaleY;
		keys[i].value.z = keys[i - 1].value.z + (original[i].z - original[i - 1].z) * scaleXZ;
	}
}


// ルートをY軸回りに回す
void AnimationEditBaker::ApplyYawOffset(Model::NodeAnim& rootAnim, const Model::Node& rootNode, float yawDegrees)
{
	const DirectX::XMVECTOR yaw = DirectX::XMQuaternionRotationRollPitchYaw(0.0f, DirectX::XMConvertToRadians(yawDegrees), 0.0f);
	const DirectX::XMVECTOR pivot = DirectX::XMLoadFloat3(&rootNode.position);

	// バインドポーズの位置を中心に回す
	for (auto& key : rootAnim.positionKeyframes)
	{
		DirectX::XMVECTOR p = DirectX::XMVectorSubtract(DirectX::XMLoadFloat3(&key.value), pivot);
		p = DirectX::XMVector3Rotate(p, yaw);
		DirectX::XMStoreFloat3(&key.value, DirectX::XMVectorAdd(p, pivot));
	}

	// 回転キーが無いクリップは、バインドポーズの回転を1キーとして足しておく
	if (rootAnim.rotationKeyframes.empty())
	{
		rootAnim.rotationKeyframes.push_back({ 0.0f, rootNode.rotation });
	}

	// 元の回転の「後に」Yawを掛ける
	for (auto& key : rootAnim.rotationKeyframes)
	{
		DirectX::XMVECTOR q = DirectX::XMLoadFloat4(&key.value);
		q = DirectX::XMQuaternionNormalize(DirectX::XMQuaternionMultiply(q, yaw));
		DirectX::XMStoreFloat4(&key.value, q);
	}
}

===== ComponentRegistry.cpp =====
Path: C:\Users\offri\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Engine\Resource\ComponentRegistry.cpp
#include "ComponentRegistry.h"

std::unordered_map<std::string, ComponentInfo> ComponentRegistry::registry;
std::vector<std::string> ComponentRegistry::typeNames;

void ComponentRegistry::Register(ComponentInfo info)
{
    if (registry.find(info.typeName) != registry.end()) return;
    typeNames.push_back(info.typeName);
    registry[info.typeName] = std::move(info);
}

const std::vector<std::string>& ComponentRegistry::GetTypeNames()
{
    return typeNames;
}

const ComponentInfo* ComponentRegistry::Get(const std::string& typeName)
{
    auto it = registry.find(typeName);
    if (it == registry.end()) return nullptr;
    return &it->second;
}


===== ComponentRegistry.h =====
Path: C:\Users\offri\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Engine\Resource\ComponentRegistry.h
#pragma once
#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <functional>
#include "entt.hpp"

struct ComponentInfo
{
    std::string typeName;    // 識別子(コード上の型名)。保存のキーにも使う
    std::string displayName; // エディタ上の表示名
    std::function<void(entt::registry&, entt::entity)> add;
    std::function<void(entt::registry&, entt::entity)> remove;
    std::function<bool(entt::registry&, entt::entity)> has;
    // エンティティが持つコンポーネントの実態をmeta_anyとして取得する
    std::function<entt::meta_any(entt::registry&, entt::entity)> getMeta;
};

class ComponentRegistry
{
public:
    static void Register(ComponentInfo info);
    static const std::vector<std::string>& GetTypeNames();
    static const ComponentInfo* Get(const std::string& typeName);

    // 型1つ分を、標準の付け外し処理で登録する
    template<typename T>
    static void RegisterType(const std::string& typeName, const std::string& displayName)
    {
        Register({
            typeName,
            displayName,
            [](entt::registry& r, entt::entity e) { if (!r.any_of<T>(e)) r.emplace<T>(e); },
            [](entt::registry& r, entt::entity e) { r.remove<T>(e); },
            [](entt::registry& r, entt::entity e) { return r.any_of<T>(e); },
            [](entt::registry& r, entt::entity e) -> entt::meta_any
            {
                auto* c = r.try_get<T>(e);
                if (!c) return entt::meta_any{};
                return entt::forward_as_meta(*c);
            }
            });
    }

private:
    static std::unordered_map<std::string, ComponentInfo> registry;
    static std::vector<std::string> typeNames;
};

// 型名を、コード上の名前から自動で付けて登録する
#define REGISTER_COMPONENT_TYPE(Type, DisplayName) \
    ComponentRegistry::RegisterType<Type>(#Type, DisplayName)


===== EntitySerializer.cpp =====
Path: C:\Users\offri\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Engine\Resource\EntitySerializer.cpp
#include "EntitySerializer.h"

#include "Engine/Resource/ComponentRegistry.h"

#include "Engine/ECS/Components/PrefabInstanceComponent.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Reflection.h"

#include "Engine/Core/Log.h"
#include "Engine/Core/JsonFileCache.h"

#include <DirectXMath.h>

nlohmann::json EntitySerializer::SerializeEntity(entt::registry& registry, entt::entity e)
{
	nlohmann::json result = nlohmann::json::object();

	for (const auto& name : ComponentRegistry::GetTypeNames()) 
	{
		const ComponentInfo* info = ComponentRegistry::Get(name);
		if (!info || !info->has(registry, e)) continue;
		if (!info->getMeta) continue;

		// instanceをローカル変数に代入すると参照が崩れるため、type()の取得と
		// フィールドの読み取りで、それぞれその場でgetMeta()を呼び直す
		entt::meta_type metaType = info->getMeta(registry, e).type();
		if (!metaType) continue;

		nlohmann::json fields = nlohmann::json::object();

		for (auto&& [id, metaData] : metaType.data())
		{
			// Transientの項目は、実行中にSystemが書き換える状態なので、保存しない
			FieldInfo* fieldInfo = metaData.custom();
			if (fieldInfo && HasFlag(fieldInfo->flags, FieldFlags::Transient)) continue;

			std::string fieldName(metaData.name());
			entt::meta_any value = metaData.get(info->getMeta(registry, e));

			// 保存に未対応の型は、書き出さない
			nlohmann::json serialized = SerializeMetaAny(value);
			if (serialized.is_null()) continue;

			fields[fieldName] = serialized;
		}

		result[name] = fields;
	}

	return result;
}

nlohmann::json EntitySerializer::SerializeMetaAny(entt::meta_any& value)
{
	if (float* f = value.try_cast<float>()) return *f;
	if (int* i = value.try_cast<int>()) return *i;
	if (bool* b = value.try_cast<bool>()) return *b;
	if (std::string* s = value.try_cast<std::string>()) return *s;

	if (DirectX::XMFLOAT2* v = value.try_cast<DirectX::XMFLOAT2>())
		return nlohmann::json::array({ v->x, v->y });
	if (DirectX::XMFLOAT3* v = value.try_cast<DirectX::XMFLOAT3>())
		return nlohmann::json::array({ v->x, v->y, v->z });
	if (DirectX::XMFLOAT4* v = value.try_cast<DirectX::XMFLOAT4>())
		return nlohmann::json::array({ v->x, v->y, v->z, v->w });

	if (value.type().is_enum())
	{
		// enumの値を、REFLECT_ENUM_VALUEで登録した名前(文字列)として保存する
		entt::meta_type enumType = value.type();
		for (auto&& [id, enumData] : enumType.data())
		{
			entt::meta_any constValue = enumData.get({});
			if (constValue == value)
			{
				return std::string(enumData.name());
			}
		}
	}

	return nullptr; // 対応していない型
}

void EntitySerializer::DeserializeEntity(entt::registry& registry, entt::entity e, const nlohmann::json& data)
{
	for (auto& [name, fields] : data.items())
	{
		const ComponentInfo* info = ComponentRegistry::Get(name);
		if (!info)
		{
			// 削除・改名されたコンポーネント。落とさずに飛ばして、ログに残す
			Log("未登録のコンポーネントを無視: %s", name.c_str());
			continue;
		}

		info->add(registry, e); // 無ければ付与
		if (!info->getMeta) continue;

		entt::meta_any instance = info->getMeta(registry, e);
		if (!instance) continue;

		entt::meta_type metaType = instance.type();

		for (auto&& [id, metaData] : metaType.data())
		{
			// Transientの項目は、ファイルに入っていても書き戻さない
			FieldInfo* fieldInfo = metaData.custom();
			if (fieldInfo && HasFlag(fieldInfo->flags, FieldFlags::Transient)) continue;

			std::string fieldName(metaData.name());
			if (!fields.contains(fieldName)) continue; // JSONに無いフィールドは初期値のまま
			DeserializeMetaAny(metaData, instance, fields[fieldName]);
		}
	}
}


namespace
{
	// プレハブファイルの読み書き
	JsonFileCache<nlohmann::json>& PrefabFiles()
	{
		static JsonFileCache<nlohmann::json> cache;
		return cache;
	}
}


// プレハブファイルを読む
const nlohmann::json* EntitySerializer::LoadPrefabFile(const std::string& path)
{
	auto& files = PrefabFiles();
	const nlohmann::json& data = files.GetOrLoad(path,
		[](nlohmann::json& d, const nlohmann::json& j) { d = j; });

	if (files.GetStatus(path) != JsonFileStatus::Loaded) return nullptr;
	return &data;
}
// プレハブファイルに書く
bool EntitySerializer::SavePrefabFile(const std::string& path, const nlohmann::json& data)
{
	return PrefabFiles().Save(path, data,
		[](const nlohmann::json& d) { return nlohmann::ordered_json(d); });
}

// プレハブファイルからEntityを生成する。成功したらそのEntityを、失敗したらentt::nullを返す
entt::entity EntitySerializer::InstantiatePrefab(entt::registry& registry, const std::string& prefabPath)
{
	const nlohmann::json* data = LoadPrefabFile(prefabPath);
	if (!data) return entt::null;

	if (!data->is_object())
	{
		Log("プレハブの形式が違う: %s", LogPath(prefabPath).c_str());
		return entt::null;
	}

	entt::entity e = CreateFromComponents(registry, *data);
	if (e == entt::null)
	{
		Log("プレハブの復元に失敗: %s", LogPath(prefabPath).c_str());
		return entt::null;
	}

	// どのプレハブから生まれたかを記録する
	registry.emplace<PrefabInstanceComponent>(e, prefabPath);
	return e;
}

// コンポーネントの中身(JSON)からEntityを生成する。復元中にエラーが起きたら、作りかけのEntityを破棄してentt::nullを返す
entt::entity EntitySerializer::CreateFromComponents(entt::registry& registry, const nlohmann::json& components)
{
	if (!components.is_object()) return entt::null;

	entt::entity e = registry.create();

	try
	{
		DeserializeEntity(registry, e, components);
	}
	catch (const nlohmann::json::exception&)
	{
		registry.destroy(e);
		return entt::null;
	}

	return e;
}

void EntitySerializer::DeserializeMetaAny(entt::meta_data metaData, entt::meta_any& instance, const nlohmann::json& value)
{
	entt::meta_any current = metaData.get(instance);

	if (current.try_cast<float>() && value.is_number())
	{
		metaData.set(instance, value.get<float>());
	}
	else if (current.try_cast<int>() && value.is_number_integer())
	{
		metaData.set(instance, value.get<int>());
	}
	else if (current.try_cast<bool>() && value.is_boolean())
	{
		metaData.set(instance, value.get<bool>());
	}
	else if (current.try_cast<std::string>() && value.is_string())
	{
		metaData.set(instance, value.get<std::string>());
	}
	else if (current.try_cast<DirectX::XMFLOAT2>() && value.is_array() && value.size() == 2)
	{
		metaData.set(instance, DirectX::XMFLOAT2{ value[0].get<float>(), value[1].get<float>() });
	}
	else if (current.try_cast<DirectX::XMFLOAT3>() && value.is_array() && value.size() == 3)
	{
		metaData.set(instance, DirectX::XMFLOAT3{ value[0].get<float>(), value[1].get<float>(), value[2].get<float>() });
	}
	else if (current.try_cast<DirectX::XMFLOAT4>() && value.is_array() && value.size() == 4)
	{
		metaData.set(instance, DirectX::XMFLOAT4{ value[0].get<float>(), value[1].get<float>(), value[2].get<float>(), value[3].get<float>() });
	}
	else if (current.type().is_enum() && value.is_string())
	{
		// 保存されていた名前から、対応するenum値を探して書き戻す
		entt::meta_type enumType = current.type();
		std::string targetName = value.get<std::string>();

		for (auto&& [id, enumData] : enumType.data())
		{
			if (std::string(enumData.name()) == targetName)
			{
				metaData.set(instance, enumData.get({}));
				break;
			}
		}
	}
}


===== EntitySerializer.h =====
Path: C:\Users\offri\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Engine\Resource\EntitySerializer.h
#pragma once
#include "entt.hpp"
#include <json.hpp>

class EntitySerializer
{
public:
	// Entity1つを、コンポーネント名をキーにしたJSONへ変換する
	static nlohmann::json SerializeEntity(entt::registry& registry, entt::entity e);

	// JSONの内容を、Entityへ反映する
	static void DeserializeEntity(entt::registry& registry, entt::entity e, const nlohmann::json& data);

	// プレハブファイルを読む
	static const nlohmann::json* LoadPrefabFile(const std::string& path);
	// プレハブファイルに書く
	static bool SavePrefabFile(const std::string& path, const nlohmann::json& data);

	// プレハブファイルからEntityを生成する。成功したらそのEntityを、失敗したらentt::nullを返す
	static entt::entity InstantiatePrefab(entt::registry& registry, const std::string& prefabPath);

	// コンポーネントの中身(JSON)からEntityを生成する。復元中にエラーが起きたら、作りかけのEntityを破棄してentt::nullを返す
	static entt::entity CreateFromComponents(entt::registry& registry, const nlohmann::json& components);

private:
	// entt::meta_any 1つ分のフィールド値を、JSONへ変換する
	static nlohmann::json SerializeMetaAny(entt::meta_any& value);
	// JSONの値を、entt::meta経由でフィールドへ書き戻す
	static void DeserializeMetaAny(entt::meta_data metaData, entt::meta_any& instance, const nlohmann::json& value);
};


===== GLTFConverter.cpp =====
Path: C:\Users\offri\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Engine\Resource\GLTFConverter.cpp
#include "GLTFConverter.h"
#include "GpuResourceUtils.h"
#include "Engine/Utility/misc.h"

#include <fstream>
#include <vector>

// GLTFData を受け取り Model を生成して返す
std::shared_ptr<Model> GLTFConverter::Convert(
	ID3D11Device* device,
	const std::shared_ptr<GLTFData>& data)
{
	auto model = std::make_shared<Model>();
	model->hasSkin = data->hasSkin;

	ConvertNodes(*model, *data);
	ConvertMeshes(*model, *data, device);
	ConvertMaterials(*model, *data, device);
	ConvertAnimations(*model, *data);

	return model;
}

// .cereal から復元した後にGPUリソースだけ再生成する
void GLTFConverter::CreateGpuResources(ID3D11Device* device, Model& model)
{
	// メッシュのGPUバッファ再生成
	for (auto& mesh : model.meshes)
	{
		// 頂点バッファ
		{
			D3D11_BUFFER_DESC desc{};
			desc.ByteWidth = static_cast<UINT>(
				sizeof(Model::Vertex) * mesh.vertices.size());
			desc.Usage = D3D11_USAGE_DEFAULT;
			desc.BindFlags = D3D11_BIND_VERTEX_BUFFER;
			D3D11_SUBRESOURCE_DATA sub{};
			sub.pSysMem = mesh.vertices.data();
			HRESULT hr = device->CreateBuffer(
				&desc, &sub, mesh.vertexBuffer.GetAddressOf());
			_ASSERT_EXPR(SUCCEEDED(hr), hr_trace(hr));
		}
		// インデックスバッファ
		{
			D3D11_BUFFER_DESC desc{};
			desc.ByteWidth = static_cast<UINT>(
				sizeof(uint32_t) * mesh.indices.size());
			desc.Usage = D3D11_USAGE_DEFAULT;
			desc.BindFlags = D3D11_BIND_INDEX_BUFFER;
			D3D11_SUBRESOURCE_DATA sub{};
			sub.pSysMem = mesh.indices.data();
			HRESULT hr = device->CreateBuffer(
				&desc, &sub, mesh.indexBuffer.GetAddressOf());
			_ASSERT_EXPR(SUCCEEDED(hr), hr_trace(hr));
		}
	}

	// マテリアルのテクスチャ再生成
	for (auto& mat : model.materials)
	{
		// colorSpace : 色のテクスチャ（Base・Emissive）なら sRGB、データ（Normal など）なら Linear
		auto loadTex = [&](
			const std::string& filepath,
			const std::vector<uint8_t>& pixels,
			Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<ID3D11ShaderResourceView>& srv,
			DWORD dummyColor,
			TextureColorSpace colorSpace)
			{
				if (!filepath.empty())
				{
					// 外部ファイル → DDSキャッシュ優先でロード
					GpuResourceUtils::LoadTextureWithDDSCache(device, filepath, srv, colorSpace);
				}
				else if (!pixels.empty())
				{
					// 埋め込みバイナリ → そのままロード（DDSキャッシュ対象外）
					GpuResourceUtils::LoadTexture(device, pixels.data(), pixels.size(), srv, colorSpace);
				}
				else
				{
					GpuResourceUtils::CreateDummyTexture(device, srv, dummyColor, 1);
				}
			};
		loadTex(mat.baseTextureFileName, mat.baseMapData, mat.baseMap, 0xFFFFFFFF, TextureColorSpace::sRGB);
		loadTex(mat.normalTextureFileName, mat.normalMapData, mat.normalMap, 0xFFFF8080, TextureColorSpace::Linear);
		loadTex(mat.metallicRoughnessTextureFileName, mat.metallicRoughnessMapData, mat.metallicRoughnessMap, 0xFFFFFFFF, TextureColorSpace::Linear);
		loadTex(mat.occlusionTextureFileName, mat.occlusionMapData, mat.occlusionMap, 0xFFFFFFFF, TextureColorSpace::Linear);
		loadTex(mat.emissiveTextureFileName, mat.emissiveMapData, mat.emissiveMap, 0xFFFFFFFF, TextureColorSpace::sRGB); // テクスチャがなければ白（発光 = 係数）
	}

	// material ポインタの再接続
	for (auto& mesh : model.meshes)
	{
		if (mesh.materialIndex >= 0 &&
			mesh.materialIndex < (int)model.materials.size())
		{
			mesh.material = &model.materials[mesh.materialIndex];
		}
	}
}

// ノード変換
void GLTFConverter::ConvertNodes(
	Model& model,
	const GLTFData& data)
{
	model.nodes.reserve(data.nodes.size());

	for (const auto& src : data.nodes)
	{
		Model::Node dst;
		dst.name = src.name;
		dst.parentIndex = src.parentIndex;
		dst.childIndices = src.childIndices;
		dst.position = src.position;
		dst.rotation = src.rotation;
		dst.scale = src.scale;
		dst.localTransform = src.localTransform;
		dst.globalTransform = src.globalTransform;

		model.nodes.push_back(std::move(dst));
	}
}

// メッシュ変換
void GLTFConverter::ConvertMeshes(
	Model& model,
	const GLTFData& data,
	ID3D11Device* device)
{
	model.meshes.reserve(data.meshes.size());

	for (const auto& src : data.meshes)
	{
		Model::Mesh dst;
		dst.nodeIndex = src.nodeIndex;
		dst.materialIndex = src.materialIndex;

		// 頂点データをGLTFData::VertexからModel::Vertexへコピー
		dst.vertices.reserve(src.vertices.size());
		for (const auto& v : src.vertices)
		{
			Model::Vertex mv;
			mv.position = v.position;
			mv.normal = v.normal;
			mv.tangent = v.tangent;
			mv.texcoord = v.texcoord;
			mv.boneWeight = v.boneWeight;
			mv.boneIndex = v.boneIndex;
			dst.vertices.push_back(mv);
		}

		// インデックスデータコピー
		dst.indices = src.indices;

		// ボーンデータコピー
		dst.bones.reserve(src.bones.size());
		for (const auto& b : src.bones)
		{
			Model::Bone mb;
			mb.nodeIndex = b.nodeIndex;
			mb.offsetTransform = b.offsetTransform;
			dst.bones.push_back(mb);
		}

		// 頂点バッファ生成
		{
			D3D11_BUFFER_DESC desc{};
			desc.ByteWidth = static_cast<UINT>(
				sizeof(Model::Vertex) * dst.vertices.size());
			desc.Usage = D3D11_USAGE_DEFAULT;
			desc.BindFlags = D3D11_BIND_VERTEX_BUFFER;

			D3D11_SUBRESOURCE_DATA sub{};
			sub.pSysMem = dst.vertices.data();

			HRESULT hr = device->CreateBuffer(
				&desc, &sub, dst.vertexBuffer.GetAddressOf());
			_ASSERT_EXPR(SUCCEEDED(hr), hr_trace(hr));
		}

		// インデックスバッファ生成
		{
			D3D11_BUFFER_DESC desc{};
			desc.ByteWidth = static_cast<UINT>(
				sizeof(uint32_t) * dst.indices.size());
			desc.Usage = D3D11_USAGE_DEFAULT;
			desc.BindFlags = D3D11_BIND_INDEX_BUFFER;

			D3D11_SUBRESOURCE_DATA sub{};
			sub.pSysMem = dst.indices.data();

			HRESULT hr = device->CreateBuffer(
				&desc, &sub, dst.indexBuffer.GetAddressOf());
			_ASSERT_EXPR(SUCCEEDED(hr), hr_trace(hr));
		}

		model.meshes.push_back(std::move(dst));
	}
	// 全メッシュの頂点からバウンディングボックスを計算
	for (const auto& mesh : model.meshes)
	{
		for (const auto& v : mesh.vertices)
		{
			model.boundingBox.min.x = (std::min)(model.boundingBox.min.x, v.position.x);
			model.boundingBox.min.y = (std::min)(model.boundingBox.min.y, v.position.y);
			model.boundingBox.min.z = (std::min)(model.boundingBox.min.z, v.position.z);

			model.boundingBox.max.x = (std::max)(model.boundingBox.max.x, v.position.x);
			model.boundingBox.max.y = (std::max)(model.boundingBox.max.y, v.position.y);
			model.boundingBox.max.z = (std::max)(model.boundingBox.max.z, v.position.z);
		}
	}
}

// マテリアル変換
void GLTFConverter::ConvertMaterials(
	Model& model,
	const GLTFData& data,
	ID3D11Device* device)
{
	model.materials.reserve(data.materials.size());

	// colorSpace : 色のテクスチャ（Base・Emissive）なら sRGB、データ（Normal など）なら Linear
	auto convertTexture = [&](
		int texIndex,
		Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<ID3D11ShaderResourceView>& srv,
		DWORD dummyColor,
		TextureColorSpace colorSpace)
		{
			if (texIndex < 0 ||
				texIndex >= (int)data.textures.size())
			{
				GpuResourceUtils::CreateDummyTexture(device, srv, dummyColor, 1);
				return;
			}

			const auto& texData = data.textures[texIndex];

			if (!texData.filepath.empty())
			{
				// 外部ファイルから読む
				// DDSキャッシュ優先でロード
				GpuResourceUtils::LoadTextureWithDDSCache(device, texData.filepath, srv, colorSpace);
			}
			else if (!texData.pixels.empty())
			{
				// 埋め込みバイナリから読む(そのままロード(DDSキャッシュ対象外))
				GpuResourceUtils::LoadTexture(device, texData.pixels.data(), texData.pixels.size(), srv, colorSpace);
			}
			else
			{
				GpuResourceUtils::CreateDummyTexture(device, srv, dummyColor, 1);
			}
		};

	for (const auto& src : data.materials)
	{
		Model::Material dst;
		dst.name = src.name;
		dst.baseColor = src.baseColor;
		dst.roughnessFactor = src.roughnessFactor;
		dst.metallicFactor = src.metallicFactor;
		dst.emissiveFactor = src.emissiveFactor;
		dst.alphaMode = static_cast<Model::Material::AlphaMode>(src.alphaMode);
		dst.alphaCutoff = src.alphaCutoff;

		// 外部ファイルならパスを、埋め込みならバイナリデータを保存
		auto saveTexture = [&](
			int texIndex,
			std::string& outPath,
			std::vector<uint8_t>& outPixels)
			{
				if (texIndex < 0 || texIndex >= (int)data.textures.size()) return;
				const auto& texData = data.textures[texIndex];

				if (!texData.filepath.empty())
				{
					// 外部ファイル参照ならパスだけ保存
					outPath = texData.filepath;
				}
				else if (!texData.pixels.empty())
				{
					// 埋め込みならバイナリデータを保存
					outPixels = texData.pixels;
				}
			};

		saveTexture(src.baseMapIndex, dst.baseTextureFileName, dst.baseMapData);
		saveTexture(src.normalMapIndex, dst.normalTextureFileName, dst.normalMapData);
		saveTexture(src.metallicRoughnessMapIndex, dst.metallicRoughnessTextureFileName, dst.metallicRoughnessMapData);
		saveTexture(src.occlusionMapIndex, dst.occlusionTextureFileName, dst.occlusionMapData);
		saveTexture(src.emissiveMapIndex, dst.emissiveTextureFileName, dst.emissiveMapData);

		// テクスチャ生成（初回ロード時）
		convertTexture(src.baseMapIndex, dst.baseMap, 0xFFFFFFFF, TextureColorSpace::sRGB);
		convertTexture(src.normalMapIndex, dst.normalMap, 0xFFFF8080, TextureColorSpace::Linear);
		convertTexture(src.metallicRoughnessMapIndex, dst.metallicRoughnessMap, 0xFFFFFFFF, TextureColorSpace::Linear);
		convertTexture(src.occlusionMapIndex, dst.occlusionMap, 0xFFFFFFFF, TextureColorSpace::Linear);
		convertTexture(src.emissiveMapIndex, dst.emissiveMap, 0xFFFFFFFF, TextureColorSpace::sRGB); // テクスチャがなければ白

		model.materials.push_back(std::move(dst));
	}
	// メッシュとマテリアルを紐付け
	for (auto& mesh : model.meshes)
	{
		if (mesh.materialIndex >= 0 &&
			mesh.materialIndex < (int)model.materials.size())
		{
			mesh.material = &model.materials[mesh.materialIndex];
		}
	}
}

// アニメーション変換
void GLTFConverter::ConvertAnimations(
	Model& model,
	const GLTFData& data)
{
	model.animations.reserve(data.animations.size());

	for (const auto& src : data.animations)
	{
		Model::Animation dst;
		dst.name = src.name;
		dst.secondsLength = src.secondsLength;

		dst.nodeAnims.reserve(src.nodeAnims.size());
		for (const auto& srcNode : src.nodeAnims)
		{
			Model::NodeAnim dstNode;

			// positionキーフレーム
			dstNode.positionKeyframes.reserve(
				srcNode.positionKeyframes.size());
			for (const auto& kf : srcNode.positionKeyframes)
			{
				Model::VectorKeyframe dkf;
				dkf.seconds = kf.seconds;
				dkf.value = kf.value;
				dstNode.positionKeyframes.push_back(dkf);
			}

			// rotationキーフレーム
			dstNode.rotationKeyframes.reserve(
				srcNode.rotationKeyframes.size());
			for (const auto& kf : srcNode.rotationKeyframes)
			{
				Model::QuaternionKeyframe dkf;
				dkf.seconds = kf.seconds;
				dkf.value = kf.value;
				dstNode.rotationKeyframes.push_back(dkf);
			}

			// scaleキーフレーム
			dstNode.scaleKeyframes.reserve(
				srcNode.scaleKeyframes.size());
			for (const auto& kf : srcNode.scaleKeyframes)
			{
				Model::VectorKeyframe dkf;
				dkf.seconds = kf.seconds;
				dkf.value = kf.value;
				dstNode.scaleKeyframes.push_back(dkf);
			}

			dst.nodeAnims.push_back(std::move(dstNode));
		}

		model.animations.push_back(std::move(dst));
	}
}

private:

        Model& model,
