#pragma once
#include "Engine/ECS/Reflect.h"

// スティックの状態
struct StickState
{
	REFLECT_BEGIN(StickState, "Stick State")

		REFLECT_FIELD(float, x, (0.0f), ("X", 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.1f, "", FieldFlags::VisibleOnly | FieldFlags::Transient))
		REFLECT_FIELD(float, y, (0.0f), ("Y", 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.1f, "", FieldFlags::VisibleOnly | FieldFlags::Transient))
		REFLECT_FIELD(bool, hasInput, (false), ("Has Input", 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.1f, "", FieldFlags::VisibleOnly | FieldFlags::Transient)) // 入力があったかどうか

		REFLECT_END()
};

// ボタンの状態
struct ButtonState
{
	REFLECT_BEGIN(ButtonState, "Button State")

		REFLECT_FIELD(bool, pressed, (false), ("Pressed", 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.1f, "", FieldFlags::VisibleOnly | FieldFlags::Transient)) // 入力があったかどうか
		REFLECT_FIELD(bool, held, (false), ("Held", 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.1f, "", FieldFlags::VisibleOnly | FieldFlags::Transient)) // 押しっぱなし中
		REFLECT_FIELD(bool, released, (false), ("Released", 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.1f, "", FieldFlags::VisibleOnly | FieldFlags::Transient)) // 今フレーム離された

		REFLECT_END()

		// 内部用：前フレームの押下状態
		bool wasHeld = false;
};

// トリガーの状態
struct TriggerState
{
	REFLECT_BEGIN(TriggerState, "Trigger State")

		REFLECT_FIELD(float, value, (0.0f), ("Value", 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.1f, "", FieldFlags::VisibleOnly | FieldFlags::Transient)) // 入力値(0.0～1.0)
		REFLECT_FIELD(bool, pressed, (false), ("Pressed", 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.1f, "", FieldFlags::VisibleOnly | FieldFlags::Transient)) // 今フレーム新しく押された
		REFLECT_FIELD(bool, held, (false), ("Held", 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.1f, "", FieldFlags::VisibleOnly | FieldFlags::Transient)) // 押しっぱなし中
		REFLECT_FIELD(bool, released, (false), ("Released", 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.1f, "", FieldFlags::VisibleOnly | FieldFlags::Transient)) // 今フレーム離された

		REFLECT_END()

		// 内部用：前フレームの押下状態
		bool wasHeld = false;
};

struct GameInputComponent
{
	REFLECT_BEGIN(GameInputComponent, "Input")

		// スティック
		REFLECT_FIELD(StickState, moveStick,   (), ("Move Stick"))   // 左スティック
		REFLECT_FIELD(StickState, cameraStick, (), ("Camera Stick")) // 右スティック

		// トリガー
		REFLECT_FIELD(TriggerState, lockOnTrigger, (), ("Lock On Trigger")) // L2
		REFLECT_FIELD(TriggerState, grabTrigger,   (), ("Grab Trigger"))    // R2

		// ボタン
		REFLECT_FIELD(ButtonState, jump,        (), ("Jump"))         // ×
		REFLECT_FIELD(ButtonState, dodge,       (), ("Dodge"))        // 〇
		REFLECT_FIELD(ButtonState, lightAttack, (), ("Light Attack")) // □
		REFLECT_FIELD(ButtonState, heavyAttack, (), ("Heavy Attack")) // △

		REFLECT_FIELD(ButtonState, wire, (), ("Wire")) // R1


	REFLECT_END()
};

#pragma once
#include <DirectXMath.h>
#include "Engine/ECS/Reflect.h"

// 回避の方向
enum class PlayerDodgeDirection
{
	Front,
	Back,
	Left,
	Right,
};

struct PlayerDodgeComponent
{
	REFLECT_BEGIN(PlayerDodgeComponent, "Player Dodge")

		// 設定値
		REFLECT_FIELD(float, dodgeSpeed, (8.0f), ("Dodge Speed", 0.0f, 20.0f)) // 回避中の速さ
		REFLECT_FIELD(float, minTime, (0.15f), ("Min Time", 0.0f, 2.0f))  // R2を短く押した時の長さ
		REFLECT_FIELD(float, maxTime, (0.5f), ("Max Time", 0.0f, 2.0f))  // R2を押し続けた時の長さ
		REFLECT_FIELD(float, accelHalfLife, (0.02f), ("Accel Half Life", 0.0f, 1.0f))  // 回避の速さに達するまでの半減期
		REFLECT_FIELD(float, turnSpeed, (10.0f), ("Turn Speed", 0.0f, 30.0f)) // 回避方向を曲げる速さ

		// 状態
		REFLECT_FIELD(bool, isFront,  (false), ("Is Front", 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.1f, "", FieldFlags::VisibleOnly | FieldFlags::Transient)) // 前へ回避
		REFLECT_FIELD(bool, isBack,   (false), ("Is Back",  0.0f, 0.0f, 0.1f, "", FieldFlags::VisibleOnly | FieldFlags::Transient)) // 後ろへ回避
		REFLECT_FIELD(bool, isLeft,   (false), ("Is Left",  0.0f, 0.0f, 0.1f, "", FieldFlags::VisibleOnly | FieldFlags::Transient)) // 左へ回避
		REFLECT_FIELD(bool, isRight,  (false), ("Is Right", 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.1f, "", FieldFlags::VisibleOnly | FieldFlags::Transient)) // 右へ回避
		REFLECT_FIELD(bool, finished, (false), ("Finished", 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.1f, "", FieldFlags::VisibleOnly | FieldFlags::Transient)) // 回避が終わったか
		REFLECT_FIELD(bool, faceDodgeDir, (false), ("Face Dodge Dir", 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.1f, "", FieldFlags::VisibleOnly | FieldFlags::Transient)) // 回避方向を向いて回避するか(前方3方向のとき)

		// 体から見た回避方向
		REFLECT_FIELD(float, localX,  (0.0f), ("Local X", 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.1f, "", FieldFlags::VisibleOnly | FieldFlags::Transient)) // 右が +
		REFLECT_FIELD(float, localZ,  (0.0f), ("Local Z", 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.1f, "", FieldFlags::VisibleOnly | FieldFlags::Transient)) // 前が +
		REFLECT_FIELD(float, elapsed, (0.0f), ("Elapsed", 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.1f, "", FieldFlags::VisibleOnly | FieldFlags::Transient)) // 回避に入ってからの時間(秒)

		REFLECT_END()

		// 内部実装用
		PlayerDodgeDirection direction = PlayerDodgeDirection::Back; // 入った時に決めた方向
	DirectX::XMFLOAT3    dodgeDir = { 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f };        // 今進んでいる方向
};

#pragma once
#include <DirectXMath.h>
#include "Engine/ECS/Reflect.h"

// ボタン1つぶんの意図
struct ActionIntent
{
	REFLECT_BEGIN(ActionIntent, "Action Intent")

		REFLECT_FIELD(bool,  buffered, (false), ("Buffered",  0.0f, 0.0f, 0.1f, "", FieldFlags::VisibleOnly | FieldFlags::Transient)) // 先行入力として受け付け中
		REFLECT_FIELD(bool,  held,     (false), ("Held",      0.0f, 0.0f, 0.1f, "", FieldFlags::VisibleOnly | FieldFlags::Transient)) // 今押し続けているか
		REFLECT_FIELD(float, heldTime, (0.0f),  ("Held Time", 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.1f, "", FieldFlags::VisibleOnly | FieldFlags::Transient)) // 押し続けている時間

		REFLECT_END()

		// 内部実装用
		float bufferTimer = 0.0f; // 押してからの経過時間

	// ステートが使った時に呼ぶ
	void Consume() { buffered = false; }
};

// プレイヤーの意図
struct PlayerIntentComponent
{
	REFLECT_BEGIN(PlayerIntentComponent, "Player Intent")

		// 設定値
		REFLECT_FIELD(float, moveGraceTime,     (0.1f),  ("Move Grace Time",     0.0f, 0.5f)) // 入力が途切れても wantsMove を保つ時間
		REFLECT_FIELD(float, runEnterThreshold, (0.9f),  ("Run Enter Threshold", 0.0f, 1.0f)) // この倒し具合以上で走りに入る
		REFLECT_FIELD(float, runExitThreshold,  (0.7f),  ("Run Exit Threshold",  0.0f, 1.0f)) // この倒し具合未満で歩きに戻る
		REFLECT_FIELD(float, runGraceTime,      (0.15f), ("Run Grace Time",      0.0f, 0.5f)) // 倒し具合が戻っても wantsRun を保つ時間
		REFLECT_FIELD(float, jumpBufferTime,    (0.15f), ("Jump Buffer Time",    0.0f, 0.5f)) // ジャンプの先行入力を受け付けておく時間
		REFLECT_FIELD(float, dodgeBufferTime,   (0.15f), ("Dodge Buffer Time",   0.0f, 0.5f)) // 回避の先行入力を受け付けておく時間

		// 意図
		REFLECT_FIELD(bool, wantsMove, (false), ("Wants Move", 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.1f, "", FieldFlags::VisibleOnly | FieldFlags::Transient)) // 動きたいか
		REFLECT_FIELD(bool, wantsRun,  (false), ("Wants Run",  0.0f, 0.0f, 0.1f, "", FieldFlags::VisibleOnly | FieldFlags::Transient)) // 走りたいか
		
		REFLECT_FIELD(DirectX::XMFLOAT3, moveDir, (0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f), ("Move Dir", 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.1f, "", FieldFlags::VisibleOnly | FieldFlags::Transient)) // ワールド方向
		
		REFLECT_FIELD(float, moveAmount,  (0.0f),  ("Move Amount",   0.0f, 0.0f, 0.1f, "", FieldFlags::VisibleOnly | FieldFlags::Transient)) // 倒し具合
		REFLECT_FIELD(bool,  hasRawInput, (false), ("Has Raw Input", 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.1f, "", FieldFlags::VisibleOnly | FieldFlags::Transient)) // 今フレームの生の入力があるか

		// ボタンの意図
		REFLECT_FIELD(ActionIntent, jump, (), ("Jump"))
		REFLECT_FIELD(ActionIntent, dodge, (), ("Dodge"))

		REFLECT_END()

		// 内部実装用
		float graceTimer = 0.0f; // 入力が途切れてからの経過時間
		float runGraceTimer = 0.0f; // 倒し具合が runExitThreshold を下回ってからの経過時間
};

===== PlayerLocomotionComponent.h =====
Path: C:\Users\offri\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Game\ECS\Components\PlayerLocomotionComponent.h
#pragma once
#include <DirectXMath.h>
#include "Engine/ECS/Reflect.h"

// 水平移動をどう計算するか
enum class LocomotionMode
{
	Ground,      // 地上：体の向きに沿って進む
	Air,         // 空中：速度のベクトルを保ち入力方向に寄せる
	Directional, // 向きとは別に進む
	External,    // 外部
};

// ステートから Locomotion への指示
struct LocomotionCommand
{
	LocomotionMode    mode = LocomotionMode::Ground;
	DirectX::XMFLOAT3 moveDir = { 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f }; // 向かいたい方向
	float  targetSpeed = 0.0f;    // 目標の速さ
	float  accelHalfLife = 0.05f; // 速さを上げる時の半減期 
	float  decelHalfLife = 0.06f; // 速さを下げる時の半減期
	float  turnSpeed = 0.0f;      // 旋回速度
	float  carryHalfLife = 0.2f;  // 持ち越しの速度が減る半減期
	bool   faceMoveDir = false;   // 体の向きを 向かいたい方向 に一気に向ける
	DirectX::XMFLOAT3 faceDir = { 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f }; // 体を向ける方向
};

// 水平移動の運転手と、体の状態
struct PlayerLocomotionComponent
{
	REFLECT_BEGIN(PlayerLocomotionComponent, "Player Locomotion")

		// 設定値
		REFLECT_FIELD(float, fallGraceTime, (0.1f), ("Fall Grace Time", 0.0f, 0.5f)) // 接地していない状態がこの時間続いたら、宙に浮いたとみなす

		// 体の状態
		REFLECT_FIELD(float, horizontalSpeed, (0.0f), ("Horizontal Speed", 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.1f, "", FieldFlags::VisibleOnly | FieldFlags::Transient)) // 今の水平の速さ(持ち越しを含む)
		REFLECT_FIELD(float, verticalSpeed,   (0.0f), ("Vertical Speed",   0.0f, 0.0f, 0.1f, "", FieldFlags::VisibleOnly | FieldFlags::Transient)) // 今の縦の速さ
		
		REFLECT_FIELD(float, landingHorizontalSpeed, (0.0f), ("Landing Horizontal Speed", 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.1f, "", FieldFlags::VisibleOnly | FieldFlags::Transient)) // 空中にいた最後のフレームの水平の速さ
		REFLECT_FIELD(float, landingVerticalSpeed,   (0.0f), ("Landing Vertical Speed",   0.0f, 0.0f, 0.1f, "", FieldFlags::VisibleOnly | FieldFlags::Transient)) // 空中にいた最後のフレームの縦の速さ
		
		REFLECT_FIELD(float, airTime,   (0.0f),  ("Air Time",    0.0f, 0.0f, 0.1f, "", FieldFlags::VisibleOnly | FieldFlags::Transient)) // 接地していない時間(秒)
		REFLECT_FIELD(bool, isAirborne, (false), ("Is Airborne", 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.1f, "", FieldFlags::VisibleOnly | FieldFlags::Transient)) // 宙に浮いているか(猶予付き)

		// 計算の途中経過
		REFLECT_FIELD(float, currentSpeed,   (0.0f), ("Current Speed", 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.1f, "", FieldFlags::VisibleOnly | FieldFlags::Transient)) // Ground：自分で走っている速さ
		REFLECT_FIELD(float, desiredSpeed,   (0.0f), ("Desired Speed", 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.1f, "", FieldFlags::VisibleOnly | FieldFlags::Transient)) // 向きのずれを考慮した目標の速さ
	
		REFLECT_FIELD(float, facingErrorDeg, (0.0f), ("Facing Error Deg", 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.1f, "", FieldFlags::VisibleOnly | FieldFlags::Transient)) // 体の向きと指示方向のずれ(度)

		REFLECT_END()

	// 内部実装用
	DirectX::XMFLOAT3 carryVelocity = { 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f }; // 持ち越しの速度
	LocomotionMode    lastMode = LocomotionMode::Ground;    // 前フレームのモード
	int               landingCount = 0;                     // 着地した回数

	// ステートからの指示
	LocomotionCommand command;
};

		// 入った直後の加速

		REFLECT_FIELD(float, uphillEndAngleDeg, (10.0f), ("Uphill End Angle Deg", 0.0f, 90.0f)) // 滑る向きに沿って、これより急な上り坂なら滑り終わる(度)
		REFLECT_FIELD(float, accelHalfLife, (0.05f), ("Accel Half Life",  0.0f, 1.0f))  // 滑る速度に寄せる半減期
		REFLECT_FIELD(float, heightScale,    (0.5f), ("Height Scale",     0.1f, 1.0f))  // 滑っている間の当たり判定の高さ

		// 状態
		REFLECT_FIELD(float, slideSpeed, (0.0f), ("Slide Speed", 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.1f, "", FieldFlags::VisibleOnly | FieldFlags::Transient)) // 今の滑る速さ(前へ)
		REFLECT_FIELD(float, elapsed, (0.0f), ("Elapsed", 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.1f, "", FieldFlags::VisibleOnly | FieldFlags::Transient)) // 入ってからの時間
		REFLECT_FIELD(bool,  wantsStand,     (false), ("Wants Stand",     0.0f, 0.0f, 0.1f, "", FieldFlags::VisibleOnly | FieldFlags::Transient)) // 立ちたい
		REFLECT_FIELD(bool,  standRequested, (false), ("Stand Requested", 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.1f, "", FieldFlags::VisibleOnly | FieldFlags::Transient)) // 立ちたい かつ 立てる
		REFLECT_FIELD(bool,  finished,       (false), ("Finished",        0.0f, 0.0f, 0.1f, "", FieldFlags::VisibleOnly | FieldFlags::Transient)) // 滑り終わった

		REFLECT_END()

		// 内部実装用
		DirectX::XMFLOAT3 slideDir = { 0.0f, 0.0f, 1.0f }; // 滑る向き(水平、長さ1)
};

		REFLECT_FIELD(float, kickOutSpeed,     (4.0f), ("Kick Out Speed",     0.0f, 20.0f)) // 壁から離れる向きの速さ

		REFLECT_FIELD(float, kickForwardScale, (1.6f), ("Kick Forward Scale", 0.0f, 3.0f))  // 壁蹴りの前への力
		REFLECT_FIELD(float, kickLockTime,     (0.2f), ("Kick Lock Time",     0.0f, 1.0f))  // 蹴った後、スティックが効かない時間

		REFLECT_FIELD(bool,  isWallRight, (false), ("Is Wall Right", 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.1f, "", FieldFlags::VisibleOnly | FieldFlags::Transient)) // 入った時に決めた左右
		REFLECT_FIELD(float, elapsed,      (0.0f), ("Elapsed",       0.0f, 0.0f, 0.1f, "", FieldFlags::VisibleOnly | FieldFlags::Transient)) // 入ってからの時間
		REFLECT_FIELD(bool,  kicked,      (false), ("Kicked",        0.0f, 0.0f, 0.1f, "", FieldFlags::VisibleOnly | FieldFlags::Transient)) // 壁を蹴った
		REFLECT_FIELD(float, kickTime,     (0.0f), ("Kick Time",     0.0f, 0.0f, 0.1f, "", FieldFlags::VisibleOnly | FieldFlags::Transient)) // 蹴ってからの時間
		REFLECT_FIELD(bool,  finished,    (false), ("Finished",      0.0f, 0.0f, 0.1f, "", FieldFlags::VisibleOnly | FieldFlags::Transient)) // 壁走りが終わった
		REFLECT_FIELD(bool, isClimbing,   (false), ("Is Climbing",   0.0f, 0.0f, 0.1f, "", FieldFlags::VisibleOnly | FieldFlags::Transient)) // ① 駆け上がり中
		REFLECT_FIELD(bool, isFalling,    (false), ("Is Falling",    0.0f, 0.0f, 0.1f, "", FieldFlags::VisibleOnly | FieldFlags::Transient)) // ③ 下がっている(子クリップの条件にも使える)

		REFLECT_FIELD(float, radius, (0.5f), ("Radius", 0.0f, 2.0f)) // 壁走り中の当たり判定の半径

	// 内部実装用
	DirectX::XMFLOAT3 wallNormal = { 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f }; // 今沿っている壁の法線
	DirectX::XMFLOAT3 runDir = { 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f };     // 壁に沿って進む向き
	float             runSpeed = 0.0f;                   // 走る速さ
	float             climbStartY = 0.0f;              // 駆け上がりを始めた高さ
	int               lastLandingCount = -1;           // 前に壁走りへ入った時の着地回数

};

===== CameraEffectSystem.cpp =====
Path: C:\Users\offri\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Game\ECS\Systems\CameraEffectSystem.cpp
#include "CameraEffectSystem.h"

#include <cmath>

#include "Game/ECS/Components/PlayerLocomotionComponent.h"
#include "Game/ECS/Components/PlayerWallRunComponent.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/MovementComponent.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/StateMachineComponent.h"
#include "Game/ECS/Components/PlayerSlideComponent.h"
#include "Game/ECS/Systems/PlayerMovementUtils.h"

void CameraEffectSystem::Update(entt::registry& registry, float elapsedTime)
{
	auto view = registry.view<CameraEffectComponent, GameCameraComponent>();
	view.each([&](CameraEffectComponent& effect, const GameCameraComponent& cam)
		{
			// 出力を初期化
			effect.eyeOffset = { 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f };
			effect.targetOffset = { 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f };
			effect.fovOffset = 0.0f;
			effect.roll = 0.0f;

			// 追従先
			entt::entity player = entt::null;
			if (cam.followTarget != entt::null && registry.valid(cam.followTarget))
			{
				player = cam.followTarget;
			}

			// きっかけを作る
			const CameraEffectTrigger trigger = BuildTrigger(registry, effect, player);

			// 演出ごとの処理
			FovEffect(effect, trigger, elapsedTime);
			RunBobEffect(effect, trigger, cam, elapsedTime);
			KickShakeEffect(effect, trigger, cam, elapsedTime);
			LandShakeEffect(effect, trigger, cam, elapsedTime);
			SlideDropEffect(effect, trigger, elapsedTime);
			WallTiltEffect(effect, trigger, elapsedTime);
		});
}

// プレイヤーを見て、きっかけを作る
CameraEffectTrigger CameraEffectSystem::BuildTrigger(entt::registry& registry, const CameraEffectComponent& effect, entt::entity player)
{
	CameraEffectTrigger trigger;
	if (player == entt::null) return trigger;

	// 速度
	const auto* loco = registry.try_get<PlayerLocomotionComponent>(player);
	if (loco)
	{
		trigger.speed = loco->horizontalSpeed;
	}

	trigger.running = trigger.speed >= effect.runSpeed;

	const auto* stateMachine = registry.try_get<StateMachineComponent>(player);

	// 壁走り・壁蹴り
	const auto* wallRun = registry.try_get<PlayerWallRunComponent>(player);
	if (wallRun)
	{
		trigger.wallKicked = wallRun->kicked;
		trigger.wallRight = wallRun->isWallRight;
		if (stateMachine && stateMachine->IsIn("WallRun") && !wallRun->kicked)
		{
			trigger.wallRunning = true;
		}
	}

	// 落下速度
	const auto* movement = registry.try_get<MovementComponent>(player);
	if (movement)
	{
		trigger.velocityY = movement->velocity.y;
		trigger.isGround = movement->isGrounded;
	}

	// スライディング
	const auto* slide = registry.try_get<PlayerSlideComponent>(player);
	if (stateMachine && slide && stateMachine->IsIn("Slide"))
	{
		trigger.sliding = true;
		trigger.slideSpeed = slide->slideSpeed;
	}

	return trigger;
}

// 速さで Fov を広げる
void CameraEffectSystem::FovEffect(CameraEffectComponent& effect, const CameraEffectTrigger& trigger, float elapsedTime)
{
	// runSpeed～speedFull を 0～1 に
	float t = 0.0f;
	const float range = effect.speedFull - effect.runSpeed;
	if (range > 0.0f)
	{
		t = (trigger.speed - effect.runSpeed) / range;
	}
	if (t < 0.0f)
	{
		t = 0.0f;
	}
	if (t > 1.0f)
	{
		t = 1.0f;
	}
	const float targetAdd = effect.fovAddMax * t;

	// 半減期で寄せる
	effect.fovAdd = PlayerMovementUtils::Damper(effect.fovAdd, targetAdd, effect.fovHalfLife, elapsedTime);

	// 出力に足す
	effect.fovOffset += effect.fovAdd;
}
// 走り出しに左右へ揺らす
void CameraEffectSystem::RunBobEffect(CameraEffectComponent& effect, const CameraEffectTrigger& trigger, const GameCameraComponent& cam, float elapsedTime)
{
	PulseState& pulse = effect.runBob;

	// 速度が一定以下になるまで再度揺らさない
	if (trigger.speed <= effect.runBobRearmSpeed)
	{
		pulse.ready = true;
	}

	// 走り出しに揺らす
	if (pulse.ready && trigger.running)
	{
		pulse.ready = false;
		pulse.active = true;
		pulse.time = 0.0f;
	}
	if (!pulse.active) return;

	// 一定時間で止める
	pulse.time += elapsedTime;
	if (pulse.time >= effect.runBobDuration)
	{
		pulse.active = false;
		return;
	}

	// 揺れの量
	float phase = 0.0f;
	if (effect.runBobStepInterval > 0.0f)
	{
		phase = pulse.time / effect.runBobStepInterval * DirectX::XM_PI;
	}
	const float sway = std::sin(phase);
	const float lift = std::sin(phase * 2.0f); 
	const float remain = 1.0f - pulse.time / effect.runBobDuration;
	const float envelope = remain * remain;
	const float amount = sway * envelope; // +1 で右
	const float liftY = lift * envelope * effect.runBobLift;

	// カメラから見た右へ、eye と target を同じだけずらす
	const float fx = cam.base.currentTarget.x - cam.base.currentEye.x;
	const float fz = cam.base.currentTarget.z - cam.base.currentEye.z;
	const float len = std::sqrt(fx * fx + fz * fz);
	if (len > 0.0001f)
	{
		const float rightX = fz / len;
		const float rightZ = -fx / len;
		const float shift = amount * effect.runBobSway;

		effect.eyeOffset.x += rightX * shift;
		effect.eyeOffset.z += rightZ * shift;
		effect.targetOffset.x += rightX * shift;
		effect.targetOffset.z += rightZ * shift;
	}

	// 上下も eye と target を同じだけ
	effect.eyeOffset.y += liftY;
	effect.targetOffset.y += liftY;

	effect.roll += amount * effect.runBobRollDeg;
}
// 壁蹴りで小さく揺らす
void CameraEffectSystem::KickShakeEffect(CameraEffectComponent& effect, const CameraEffectTrigger& trigger, const GameCameraComponent& cam, float elapsedTime)
{
	PulseState& pulse = effect.kickShake;

	// 壁走りに入るまで再度揺らさない
	if (!trigger.wallKicked)
	{
		pulse.ready = true;
	}

	// 蹴った瞬間に揺らす
	if (pulse.ready && trigger.wallKicked)
	{
		pulse.ready = false;
		pulse.active = true;
		pulse.time = 0.0f;
	}
	if (!pulse.active) return;

	// 一定時間で止める
	pulse.time += elapsedTime;
	if (pulse.time >= effect.kickShakeDuration)
	{
		pulse.active = false;
		return;
	}

	// 揺れの量
	const float phase = pulse.time / effect.kickShakeDuration * DirectX::XM_PI;
	float dir = -1.0f; // 壁が左
	if (trigger.wallRight) 
	{
		dir = 1.0f;    // 壁が右
	}
	const float amount = std::sin(phase) * dir; // +1で右

	// カメラから見た右へ、eye と target を同じだけずらす
	const float fx = cam.base.currentTarget.x - cam.base.currentEye.x;
	const float fz = cam.base.currentTarget.z - cam.base.currentEye.z;
	const float len = std::sqrt(fx * fx + fz * fz);
	if (len > 0.0001f)
	{
		const float rightX = fz / len;
		const float rightZ = -fx / len;
		const float shift = amount * effect.kickShakeSway;

		effect.eyeOffset.x += rightX * shift;
		effect.eyeOffset.z += rightZ * shift;
		effect.targetOffset.x += rightX * shift;
		effect.targetOffset.z += rightZ * shift;
	}

	effect.roll += amount * effect.kickShakeRollDeg;
}
// 着地で揺らす
void CameraEffectSystem::LandShakeEffect(CameraEffectComponent& effect, const CameraEffectTrigger& trigger, const GameCameraComponent& cam, float elapsedTime)
{
	PulseState& pulse = effect.landShake;

	// 素早く落ちるまで再度揺らさない
	if (!trigger.isGround)
	{
		pulse.ready = false;
		if (trigger.velocityY <= -effect.landFallSpeed)
		{
			pulse.ready = true;
		}
	}

	// 着地時に揺らす
	if (pulse.ready && trigger.isGround)
	{
		pulse.ready = false;
		pulse.active = true;
		pulse.time = 0.0f;
	}
	if (!pulse.active) return;

	// 一定時間で止める
	pulse.time += elapsedTime;
	if (pulse.time >= effect.landShakeDuration)
	{
		pulse.active = false;
		return;
	}

	// 揺れの量：２回揺らす
	const float phase = pulse.time / effect.landShakeDuration * DirectX::XM_2PI;
	const float remain = 1.0f - pulse.time / effect.landShakeDuration;
	const float envelope = remain * remain;
	const float amount = std::sin(phase) * envelope; // +1で右

	// カメラから見た右へ、eye と target を同じだけずらす
	const float fx = cam.base.currentTarget.x - cam.base.currentEye.x;
	const float fz = cam.base.currentTarget.z - cam.base.currentEye.z;
	const float len = std::sqrt(fx * fx + fz * fz);
	if (len > 0.0001f)
	{
		const float rightX = fz / len;
		const float rightZ = -fx / len;
		const float shift = amount * effect.landShakeSway;

		effect.eyeOffset.x += rightX * shift;
		effect.eyeOffset.z += rightZ * shift;
		effect.targetOffset.x += rightX * shift;
		effect.targetOffset.z += rightZ * shift;
	}

	effect.roll += amount * effect.landShakeRollDeg;
}
// スライド中は下げ続ける
void CameraEffectSystem::SlideDropEffect(CameraEffectComponent& effect, const CameraEffectTrigger& trigger, float elapsedTime)
{
	// スライド中は 1 へ、それ以外は 0 へ、一定の速さで進める
	float step = 1.0f;
	if (effect.slideDropTime > 0.0f)
	{
		step = elapsedTime / effect.slideDropTime;
	}
	if (trigger.sliding)
	{
		effect.slideDropRate += step;
		if (effect.slideDropRate > 1.0f)
		{
			effect.slideDropRate = 1.0f;
		}
	}
	else
	{
		effect.slideDropRate -= step;
		if (effect.slideDropRate < 0.0f)
		{
			effect.slideDropRate = 0.0f;
		}
	}
	if (effect.slideDropRate <= 0.0f) return;

	// sin の山の前半で下げる
	const float dropY = -std::sin(effect.slideDropRate * DirectX::XM_PIDIV2) * effect.slideDrop;

	// eye と target を同じだけ下げる
	effect.eyeOffset.y += dropY;
	effect.targetOffset.y += dropY;
}
// 壁走り中傾ける
void CameraEffectSystem::WallTiltEffect(CameraEffectComponent& effect, const CameraEffectTrigger& trigger, float elapsedTime)
{
	// 目標と、傾けていく途中かを決める
	float target = 0.0f;
	bool  growing = false;
	if (trigger.wallRunning)
	{
		float dir = -1.0f; // 壁が左
		if (trigger.wallRight)
		{
			dir = 1.0f;    // 壁が右
		}
		target = dir * effect.wallTiltMaxDeg;

		// 反対側に傾いている間は、まず素早く 0 へ
		if (effect.wallTilt * dir >= 0.0f)
		{
			growing = true;
		}
	}

	// 段々弱く傾ける
	if (growing)
	{
		effect.wallTilt = PlayerMovementUtils::Damper(effect.wallTilt, target, effect.wallTiltHalfLife, elapsedTime);
		effect.roll += effect.wallTilt;
		return;
	}

	// 戻す
	const float step = effect.wallTiltReturnSpeed * elapsedTime;
	if (effect.wallTilt < target) 
	{
		effect.wallTilt += step;
		if (effect.wallTilt > target)
		{
			effect.wallTilt = target;
		}
	}
	else
	{
		effect.wallTilt -= step;
		if (effect.wallTilt < target)
		{
			effect.wallTilt = target;
		}
	}

	// 出力に足す
	effect.roll += effect.wallTilt;
}



===== CameraEffectSystem.h =====
Path: C:\Users\offri\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Game\ECS\Systems\CameraEffectSystem.h
#pragma once
#include "entt.hpp"
#include "Game/ECS/Components/CameraEffectComponent.h"
#include "Game/ECS/Components/GameCameraComponent.h"

// 演出のきっかけ
struct CameraEffectTrigger
{
	float speed = 0.0f;    // 水平の速さ
	bool  running = false; // 走っている(runSpeed 以上)

	bool wallRunning = false; // 壁走り中
	bool wallKicked = false;  // 壁を蹴った
	bool wallRight = false;   // 壁が右にあるか

	float velocityY = 0.0f;  // 縦の速さ
	bool  isGround = false;  // 着地している

	bool  sliding = false; // スライド中
	float slideSpeed = 0.0f;  // 滑る速さ
};


class CameraEffectSystem
{
public:
	void Update(entt::registry& registry, float elapsedTime);

private:
	// プレイヤーを見て、きっかけを作る
	CameraEffectTrigger BuildTrigger(entt::registry& registry, const CameraEffectComponent& effect, entt::entity player);

	// 速さで FOV を広げる
	void FovEffect(CameraEffectComponent& effect, const CameraEffectTrigger& trigger, float elapsedTime);
	// 走り出しに左右へ揺らす
	void RunBobEffect(CameraEffectComponent& effect, const CameraEffectTrigger& trigger, const GameCameraComponent& cam, float elapsedTime);
	// 壁蹴りで小さく揺らす
	void KickShakeEffect(CameraEffectComponent& effect, const CameraEffectTrigger& trigger, const GameCameraComponent& cam, float elapsedTime);
	// 着地で揺らす
	void LandShakeEffect(CameraEffectComponent& effect, const CameraEffectTrigger& trigger, const GameCameraComponent& cam, float elapsedTime);
	// スライド中は下げ続ける
	void SlideDropEffect(CameraEffectComponent& effect, const CameraEffectTrigger& trigger, float elapsedTime);
	// 壁走り中傾ける
	void WallTiltEffect(CameraEffectComponent& effect, const CameraEffectTrigger& trigger, float elapsedTime);
};


===== CameraViewSystem.cpp =====
Path: C:\Users\offri\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Game\ECS\Systems\CameraViewSystem.cpp
#include "CameraViewSystem.h"
#include <cmath>

#include "Game/ECS/Components/CameraViewComponent.h"
#include "Game/ECS/Components/CameraEffectComponent.h"
#include "Game/ECS/Components/GameCameraComponent.h"

void CameraViewSystem::Update(entt::registry& registry)
{
	auto view = registry.view<CameraViewComponent, GameCameraComponent>();
	view.each([&](entt::entity entity, CameraViewComponent& camView, const GameCameraComponent& cam)
		{
			// 土台の位置合わせがまだなら、作らない
			camView.valid = false;
			if (!cam.initialized) return;

			// 土台
			camView.eye = cam.base.currentEye;
			camView.target = cam.base.currentTarget;
			camView.fov = cam.fov;
			camView.roll = 0.0f;

			// ＋ 演出
			const auto* effect = registry.try_get<CameraEffectComponent>(entity);
			if (effect)
			{
				camView.eye.x += effect->eyeOffset.x;
				camView.eye.y += effect->eyeOffset.y;
				camView.eye.z += effect->eyeOffset.z;
				camView.target.x += effect->targetOffset.x;
				camView.target.y += effect->targetOffset.y;
				camView.target.z += effect->targetOffset.z;
				camView.fov += effect->fovOffset;
				camView.roll += effect->roll;
			}

			// あたり判定

			camView.valid = true;
		});
}

// Camera に渡す
void CameraViewSystem::ApplyToCamera(entt::registry& registry, Camera& camera)
{
	auto view = registry.view<CameraViewComponent>();
	for (auto entity : view)
	{
		const auto& camView = view.get<CameraViewComponent>(entity);
		if (!camView.valid) continue;

		// 傾き：見る向きを軸に、上の向き(0,1,0)を roll だけ回す
		const DirectX::XMVECTOR eye = DirectX::XMLoadFloat3(&camView.eye);
		const DirectX::XMVECTOR target = DirectX::XMLoadFloat3(&camView.target);
		const DirectX::XMVECTOR forward = DirectX::XMVector3Normalize(DirectX::XMVectorSubtract(target, eye));
		const DirectX::XMMATRIX rollMatrix = DirectX::XMMatrixRotationAxis(forward, DirectX::XMConvertToRadians(camView.roll));
		DirectX::XMFLOAT3 up;
		DirectX::XMStoreFloat3(&up, DirectX::XMVector3TransformNormal(DirectX::XMVectorSet(0.0f, 1.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f), rollMatrix));

		camera.SetLookAt(camView.eye, camView.target, up);
		camera.SetFovDeg(camView.fov);
		break; // カメラは1つだけ
	}
}

===== GameCameraSystem.cpp =====
Path: C:\Users\offri\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Game\ECS\Systems\GameCameraSystem.cpp
#include "GameCameraSystem.h"
#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>

#include "Engine/ECS/EntityFinder.h"
#include "Game/ECS/Components/GameInputComponent.h"

// 更新
void GameCameraSystem::Update(entt::registry& registry, float elapsedTime)
{
	auto view = registry.view<GameCameraComponent>();
	view.each([&](GameCameraComponent& cam)
		{
			// 追従対象が消えていたら、空に戻す
			if (cam.followTarget != entt::null && !registry.valid(cam.followTarget))
			{
				cam.followTarget = entt::null;
			}

			// 空なら Player を探す
			if (cam.followTarget == entt::null)
			{
				cam.followTarget = FindFirstByCategory(registry, EntityCategory::Player);
				cam.initialized = false;
			}
			if (cam.followTarget == entt::null) return;

			const auto* followTransform = registry.try_get<TransformComponent>(cam.followTarget);
			if (!followTransform) return;

			// 右スティックで回す
			const auto* input = registry.try_get<GameInputComponent>(cam.followTarget);
			if (input)
			{
				cam.yaw += input->cameraStick.x * cam.rotateSpeed * elapsedTime;
				cam.pitch += input->cameraStick.y * cam.rotateSpeed * elapsedTime;
				cam.pitch = std::clamp(cam.pitch, cam.pitchMin, cam.pitchMax);
			}

			// 最初・追従対象が変わった時：補間せずに正しい位置へ置く
			if (!cam.initialized)
			{
				cam.base.currentEye = CalcFollowIdealEye(cam, *followTransform);
				cam.base.currentTarget = CalcFollowIdealTarget(cam, *followTransform);
				cam.eyeVelocityY = 0.0f;
				cam.initialized = true;
			}

			// モードごとの処理
			switch (cam.mode)
			{
			case GameCameraMode::Follow:
				UpdateFollow(cam, *followTransform, elapsedTime);
				break;
			}
		});
}

// Camera クラスに結果を反映
void GameCameraSystem::ApplyToCamera(entt::registry& registry, Camera& camera)
{
	auto view = registry.view<GameCameraComponent>();
	for (auto entity : view)
	{
		const auto& cam = view.get<GameCameraComponent>(entity);
		if (!cam.initialized) continue;

		camera.SetLookAt(cam.base.currentEye, cam.base.currentTarget, { 0.0f, 1.0f, 0.0f });
		break;
	}
}

// 追従カメラの処理
void GameCameraSystem::UpdateFollow(GameCameraComponent& cam, const TransformComponent& followTransform, float elapsedTime)
{
	const DirectX::XMFLOAT3 idealEye = CalcFollowIdealEye(cam, followTransform);
	const DirectX::XMFLOAT3 idealTarget = CalcFollowIdealTarget(cam, followTransform);

	// target XYZ lerp 補間
	const float targetT = std::clamp(cam.targetLerpRatio / 100.0f, 0.0f, 1.0f);
	cam.base.currentTarget.x += (idealTarget.x - cam.base.currentTarget.x) * targetT;
	cam.base.currentTarget.y += (idealTarget.y - cam.base.currentTarget.y) * targetT;
	cam.base.currentTarget.z += (idealTarget.z - cam.base.currentTarget.z) * targetT;

	// eye XZ lerp 補間
	const float eyeT = std::clamp(cam.eyeLerpRatio / 100.0f, 0.0f, 1.0f);
	cam.base.currentEye.x += (idealEye.x - cam.base.currentEye.x) * eyeT;
	cam.base.currentEye.z += (idealEye.z - cam.base.currentEye.z) * eyeT;

	// eye ばね 補間
	const float accel = cam.springStiffness * (idealEye.y - cam.base.currentEye.y) - cam.springDamping * cam.eyeVelocityY;
	cam.eyeVelocityY += accel * elapsedTime;
	cam.base.currentEye.y += cam.eyeVelocityY * elapsedTime;
}

// 追従カメラの理想の eye
DirectX::XMFLOAT3 GameCameraSystem::CalcFollowIdealEye(const GameCameraComponent& cam, const TransformComponent& followTransform) const
{
	const DirectX::XMFLOAT3& p = followTransform.position;
	const float sinYaw = sinf(cam.yaw);
	const float cosYaw = cosf(cam.yaw);
	const float sinPitch = sinf(cam.pitch);
	const float cosPitch = cosf(cam.pitch);

	return {
		p.x - sinYaw * cosPitch * cam.followDistance,
		p.y + sinPitch * cam.followDistance + cam.followHeight,
		p.z - cosYaw * cosPitch * cam.followDistance
	};
}
// 追従カメラの理想の target
DirectX::XMFLOAT3 GameCameraSystem::CalcFollowIdealTarget(const GameCameraComponent& cam, const TransformComponent& followTransform) const
{
	const DirectX::XMFLOAT3& p = followTransform.position;
	return { p.x, p.y + cam.followTargetYUp, p.z };
}

===== GameInputSystem.cpp =====
Path: C:\Users\offri\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Game\ECS\Systems\GameInputSystem.cpp
#include "GameInputSystem.h"
#include <cmath>
#include <algorithm>

// 更新
void GameInputSystem::Update(entt::registry& registry, const ControllerSettings& settings)
{
    // ControllerComponent を探す（なければ何もしない）
    ControllerComponent* ctrl = nullptr;
    auto ctrlView = registry.view<ControllerComponent>();
    if (!ctrlView.empty())
    {
        ctrl = &ctrlView.get<ControllerComponent>(*ctrlView.begin());
    }

    // GameInputComponent を持つ Entity を更新
    auto inputView = registry.view<GameInputComponent>();
    inputView.each([&](GameInputComponent& input)
        {
            // リセット
            input.moveStick.x = 0.0f;
            input.moveStick.y = 0.0f;
            input.moveStick.hasInput = false;

            input.cameraStick.x = 0.0f;
            input.cameraStick.y = 0.0f;
            input.cameraStick.hasInput = false;

            input.lockOnTrigger.value = 0.0f;
            input.grabTrigger.value = 0.0f;

            // ControllerComponent がなければスキップ
            if (!ctrl || !ctrl->connected)
            {
                // 接続が切れた場合も、ボタンは押されていない状態にする
                UpdateButtonState(input.lockOnTrigger, false);
                UpdateButtonState(input.grabTrigger, false);
                UpdateButtonState(input.jump, false);
                UpdateButtonState(input.dodge, false);
                UpdateButtonState(input.lightAttack, false);
                UpdateButtonState(input.heavyAttack, false);
                UpdateButtonState(input.wire, false);
                return;
            }

            // スティック（デッドゾーン適用）
            float lx = ApplyStickDeadzone(ctrl->leftStickX, settings.leftStickDeadzone);
            float ly = ApplyStickDeadzone(ctrl->leftStickY, settings.leftStickDeadzone);
            float rx = ApplyStickDeadzone(ctrl->rightStickX, settings.rightStickDeadzone);
            float ry = ApplyStickDeadzone(ctrl->rightStickY, settings.rightStickDeadzone);

            // 移動（左スティック）
            input.moveStick.x = std::clamp(lx, -1.0f, 1.0f);
            input.moveStick.y = std::clamp(ly, -1.0f, 1.0f);
            input.moveStick.hasInput = (input.moveStick.x != 0.0f || input.moveStick.y != 0.0f);

            // カメラ（右スティック）
            input.cameraStick.x = rx;
            input.cameraStick.y = -ry;
            input.cameraStick.hasInput = (input.cameraStick.x != 0.0f || input.cameraStick.y != 0.0f);

            // トリガー（デッドゾーン適用後の値で判定）
            input.lockOnTrigger.value = ApplyTriggerDeadzone(ctrl->leftTrigger, settings.leftTriggerDeadzone);
            input.grabTrigger.value = ApplyTriggerDeadzone(ctrl->rightTrigger, settings.rightTriggerDeadzone);

            // トリガーの押下判定（ヒステリシス込み）
            UpdateButtonState(input.lockOnTrigger, ctrl->IsPressed(ControllerButtons::L2));
            UpdateButtonState(input.grabTrigger, ctrl->IsPressed(ControllerButtons::R2));

            // ボタン判定
            UpdateButtonState(input.jump,        ctrl->IsPressed(ControllerButtons::Cross));
            UpdateButtonState(input.dodge,       ctrl->IsPressed(ControllerButtons::Circle));
            UpdateButtonState(input.lightAttack, ctrl->IsPressed(ControllerButtons::Square));
            UpdateButtonState(input.heavyAttack, ctrl->IsPressed(ControllerButtons::Triangle));
            UpdateButtonState(input.wire,        ctrl->IsPressed(ControllerButtons::R1));
        });
}

// デッドゾーン適用
float GameInputSystem::ApplyStickDeadzone(float value, float deadzone)
{
    if (fabsf(value) < deadzone) return 0.0f;
    float sign = value > 0.0f ? 1.0f : -1.0f;
    return sign * (fabsf(value) - deadzone) / (1.0f - deadzone);
}

// デッドゾーン適用
float GameInputSystem::ApplyTriggerDeadzone(float value, float deadzone)
{
    if (value < deadzone) return 0.0f;
    return (value - deadzone) / (1.0f - deadzone);
}

// ボタン状態の更新
void GameInputSystem::UpdateButtonState(TriggerState& state, bool isHeldNow)
{
    state.pressed = isHeldNow && !state.wasHeld;
    state.released = !isHeldNow && state.wasHeld;
    state.held = isHeldNow;
    state.wasHeld = isHeldNow;
}
void GameInputSystem::UpdateButtonState(ButtonState& state, bool isHeldNow)
{
    state.pressed = isHeldNow && !state.wasHeld;
    state.released = !isHeldNow && state.wasHeld;
    state.held = isHeldNow;
    state.wasHeld = isHeldNow;
}

===== PlayerCrouchSystem.cpp =====
Path: C:\Users\offri\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Game\ECS\Systems\PlayerCrouchSystem.cpp
#include "PlayerCrouchSystem.h"
#include "Game/ECS/Components/PlayerIntentComponent.h"
#include "Game/ECS/Components/PlayerCrouchComponent.h"
#include "Game/ECS/Components/PlayerLocomotionComponent.h"

#include "Engine/ECS/Components/StateMachineComponent.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/MovementComponent.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/CharacterContactComponent.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Systems/StateMachineSystem.h"

PlayerCrouchSystem::PlayerCrouchSystem()
{
	StateMachineSystem::RequireState<PlayerCrouchComponent>("Crouch", "PlayerCrouchSystem");
}

void PlayerCrouchSystem::Update(entt::registry& registry, float elapsedTime, const SceneContext& context)
{
	(void)elapsedTime;
	(void)context;

	auto view = registry.view<PlayerIntentComponent, PlayerCrouchComponent, StateMachineComponent, PlayerLocomotionComponent, MovementComponent, CharacterContactComponent>();
	view.each([&](PlayerIntentComponent& intent, PlayerCrouchComponent& crouch,
		const StateMachineComponent& stateMachine, PlayerLocomotionComponent& loco, MovementComponent& movement,
		const CharacterContactComponent& contact)
		{
			if (!stateMachine.IsIn("Crouch")) return;

			if (stateMachine.enteredThisFrame)
			{
				// 入った瞬間：入ってきた ○ を使い切る(残っていると、立つ判定にも使われてしまう)
				intent.dodge.Consume();
				crouch.wantsStand = false;
			}
			else if (intent.dodge.buffered)
			{
				// しゃがみ中に ○：立ちたい。使い切る(立った後に、また Crouch に入らないように)
				crouch.wantsStand = true;
				intent.dodge.Consume();
			}

			// 立ちたい かつ 頭の上が空いている → 立つ(天井の下なら、出るまで待つ)
			crouch.standRequested = crouch.wantsStand && contact.canStand;

			// 意図を、Locomotion への指示に変える
			LocomotionCommand& cmd = loco.command;
			cmd.mode = LocomotionMode::Ground;
			cmd.moveDir = intent.moveDir;
			cmd.targetSpeed = intent.wantsMove ? crouch.walkSpeed : 0.0f;
			cmd.accelHalfLife = crouch.accelHalfLife;
			cmd.decelHalfLife = crouch.decelHalfLife;
			cmd.turnSpeed = crouch.turnSpeed;

			// 当たり判定を低くする指示
			movement.heightScale = crouch.heightScale;
		});
}

===== PlayerDodgeSystem.cpp =====
Path: C:\Users\offri\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Game\ECS\Systems\PlayerDodgeSystem.cpp
#include "PlayerDodgeSystem.h"
#include <cmath>

#include "Game/ECS/Components/PlayerIntentComponent.h"
#include "Game/ECS/Components/PlayerDodgeComponent.h"
#include "Game/ECS/Components/PlayerLocomotionComponent.h"
#include "Game/ECS/Systems/PlayerMovementUtils.h"

#include "Engine/ECS/Components/StateMachineComponent.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/TransformComponent.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Systems/StateMachineSystem.h"

namespace
{
	// 体の向きから見た角度差から、4方向のどれかを決める
	PlayerDodgeDirection ClassifyDirection(float diff)
	{
		const float quarter = DirectX::XM_PIDIV4; // 45度

		if (diff > -quarter && diff <= quarter)         return PlayerDodgeDirection::Front;
		if (diff > quarter && diff <= quarter * 3.0f)   return PlayerDodgeDirection::Right;
		if (diff > -quarter * 3.0f && diff <= -quarter) return PlayerDodgeDirection::Left;
		return PlayerDodgeDirection::Back;
	}
}

PlayerDodgeSystem::PlayerDodgeSystem()
{
	StateMachineSystem::RequireState<PlayerDodgeComponent>("Dodge", "PlayerDodgeSystem");
}

void PlayerDodgeSystem::Update(entt::registry& registry, float elapsedTime, const SceneContext& context)
{
	(void)context;

	auto view = registry.view<PlayerIntentComponent, PlayerDodgeComponent, StateMachineComponent, PlayerLocomotionComponent, TransformComponent>();

	view.each([&](PlayerIntentComponent& intent, PlayerDodgeComponent& dodge, const StateMachineComponent& stateMachine,
		PlayerLocomotionComponent& loco, const TransformComponent& transform)
		{

			if (!stateMachine.IsIn("Dodge")) return;

			// 入った瞬間 回避方向を決める
			if (stateMachine.enteredThisFrame)
			{
				const float bodyYaw = transform.rotation.y;

				// スティック入力が無ければ、体の後ろ
				float dodgeYaw = bodyYaw + DirectX::XM_PI;
				dodge.faceDodgeDir = false;

				if (intent.wantsMove)
				{
					float inputYaw = PlayerMovementUtils::CalcFacingYaw(intent.moveDir.x, intent.moveDir.z);
					float diff = PlayerMovementUtils::AngleDiff(bodyYaw, inputYaw);

					// 体から見た8方向(-4?4。0 が前、+ が右回り)
					int sector = static_cast<int>(std::round(diff / DirectX::XM_PIDIV4));

					if (sector >= -1 && sector <= 1)
					{
						// 前左・前・前右：45度刻みに丸めた方向を向いて、前回避
						dodgeYaw = bodyYaw + DirectX::XM_PIDIV4 * static_cast<float>(sector);
						dodge.faceDodgeDir = true;
					}
					else
					{
						// 横・後ろ：今までどおり、スティックの方向へ(右後ろ・左後ろは保留)
						dodgeYaw = inputYaw;
					}
				}
				dodge.dodgeDir = PlayerMovementUtils::YawToForward(dodgeYaw);

				// クリップの選択用：入った瞬間だけ決め、回避中は変えない
				dodge.direction = dodge.faceDodgeDir
					? PlayerDodgeDirection::Front
					: ClassifyDirection(PlayerMovementUtils::AngleDiff(bodyYaw, dodgeYaw));
				dodge.isFront = (dodge.direction == PlayerDodgeDirection::Front);
				dodge.isBack = (dodge.direction == PlayerDodgeDirection::Back);
				dodge.isLeft = (dodge.direction == PlayerDodgeDirection::Left);
				dodge.isRight = (dodge.direction == PlayerDodgeDirection::Right);

				dodge.elapsed = 0.0f;
				dodge.finished = false;

				// 入ったステートが入力を消費する
				intent.dodge.Consume();
			}
			// 回避中 スティック入力があれば、回避方向を入力のほうへ曲げる
			else if (intent.wantsMove)
			{
				float dodgeYaw = PlayerMovementUtils::CalcFacingYaw(dodge.dodgeDir.x, dodge.dodgeDir.z);
				float inputYaw = PlayerMovementUtils::CalcFacingYaw(intent.moveDir.x, intent.moveDir.z);
				dodgeYaw = PlayerMovementUtils::RotateTowards(dodgeYaw, inputYaw, dodge.turnSpeed, elapsedTime);
				dodge.dodgeDir = PlayerMovementUtils::YawToForward(dodgeYaw);
			}

			// 毎フレーム 時間を進め、体から見た回避方向を求める
			dodge.elapsed += elapsedTime;

			float localDiff = PlayerMovementUtils::AngleDiff(
				transform.rotation.y,
				PlayerMovementUtils::CalcFacingYaw(dodge.dodgeDir.x, dodge.dodgeDir.z));
			dodge.localX = sinf(localDiff);
			dodge.localZ = cosf(localDiff);

			// 終わりの判定：最低時間を過ぎてR2を離した、または最大時間に達した
			if (!dodge.finished)
			{
				bool released = !intent.dodge.held && dodge.elapsed >= dodge.minTime;
				bool timeUp = dodge.elapsed >= dodge.maxTime;
				dodge.finished = released || timeUp;
			}

			// 毎フレーム：Locomotion への指示
			LocomotionCommand& cmd = loco.command;
			cmd.mode = LocomotionMode::Directional;
			cmd.moveDir = dodge.dodgeDir;
			cmd.targetSpeed = dodge.dodgeSpeed;
			cmd.accelHalfLife = dodge.accelHalfLife;

			// 前方3方向：回避中ずっと、体を回避方向へ向ける
			// それ以外：走り出す時だけ、抜ける前に向きを回避方向へ合わせておく
			cmd.faceMoveDir = dodge.faceDodgeDir || (dodge.finished && intent.wantsMove);
		});
}

===== PlayerIdleSystem.cpp =====

#include "PlayerIdleSystem.h"
#include "Game/ECS/Components/PlayerIdleComponent.h"

{
	StateMachineSystem::RequireState<PlayerIdleComponent>("Idle", "PlayerIdleSystem");
}

void PlayerIdleSystem::Update(entt::registry& registry, float elapsedTime, const SceneContext& context)
{
	(void)elapsedTime;
	(void)context;

	auto view = registry.view<PlayerIdleComponent, StateMachineComponent, PlayerLocomotionComponent>();
	view.each([&](const PlayerIdleComponent& idle, const StateMachineComponent& stateMachine, PlayerLocomotionComponent& loco)
		{
			if (!stateMachine.IsIn("Idle")) return;

			// その場で止まる(向きは変えない)
			LocomotionCommand& cmd = loco.command;
			cmd.mode = LocomotionMode::Ground;
			cmd.moveDir = { 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f };
			cmd.targetSpeed = 0.0f;
			cmd.decelHalfLife = idle.stopHalfLife;
			cmd.turnSpeed = 0.0f;
		});
}

===== PlayerIntentSystem.cpp =====
Path: C:\Users\offri\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Game\ECS\Systems\PlayerIntentSystem.cpp
#include "PlayerIntentSystem.h"
#include <algorithm>

#include "Game/ECS/Components/GameInputComponent.h"
#include "Game/ECS/Components/PlayerIntentComponent.h"
#include "Game/ECS/Systems/PlayerMovementUtils.h"

namespace
{
	// ボタン1つぶんの意図を更新する
	void UpdateAction(ActionIntent& action, bool pressed, bool held, float bufferTime, float elapsedTime)
	{
		// 押し続けている時間
		action.held = held;
		action.heldTime = held ? action.heldTime + elapsedTime : 0.0f;

		// 先行入力：押された瞬間に受け付け、受付時間を過ぎたら取り消す
		if (pressed)
		{
			action.buffered = true;
			action.bufferTimer = 0.0f;
		}
		else if (action.buffered)
		{
			action.bufferTimer += elapsedTime;
			if (action.bufferTimer > bufferTime)
			{
				action.buffered = false;
			}
		}
	}
}

void PlayerIntentSystem::Update(entt::registry& registry, float elapsedTime, const SceneContext& context)
{
	// カメラがない場合は処理しない
	if (!context.camera)return;

	// カメラの前方向と右方向を取得
	const DirectX::XMFLOAT3 front = context.camera->GetFront();
	const DirectX::XMFLOAT3 right = context.camera->GetRight();

	auto view = registry.view<GameInputComponent, PlayerIntentComponent>();
	view.each([&](const GameInputComponent& input, PlayerIntentComponent& intent)
		{
			// 生の入力を、カメラ基準のワールド方向に変換
			auto dir = PlayerMovementUtils::StickToWorldVelocity(input.moveStick.x, input.moveStick.y, front, right, 1.0f);
			intent.hasRawInput = (dir.inputLength > 0.0f);

			// 動きたいか(猶予付き)
			if (intent.hasRawInput)
			{
				// 入力あり：方向と倒し具合を更新して、猶予をリセット
				intent.moveDir = dir.velocity; // 長さ1
				intent.moveAmount = (std::min)(dir.inputLength, 1.0f);
				intent.graceTimer = 0.0f;
				intent.wantsMove = true;
			}
			else
			{
				// 入力なし：猶予の間は、最後の方向と倒し具合を保つ
				intent.graceTimer += elapsedTime;
				if (intent.graceTimer >= intent.moveGraceTime)
				{
					intent.wantsMove = false;
					intent.moveAmount = 0.0f;
				}
			}

			// 走りたいか(ヒステリシス＋猶予)
			if (!intent.wantsMove)
			{
				// 動きたくない：猶予なしで止める
				intent.wantsRun = false;
				intent.runGraceTimer = 0.0f;
			}
			else if (intent.moveAmount >= intent.runEnterThreshold)
			{
				// 深く倒した：走る。猶予を測り直す
				intent.wantsRun = true;
				intent.runGraceTimer = 0.0f;
			}
			else if (intent.moveAmount < intent.runExitThreshold)
			{
				// 浅くなった：猶予の時間だけ待ってから歩きに戻す
				intent.runGraceTimer += elapsedTime;
				if (intent.runGraceTimer >= intent.runGraceTime)
				{
					intent.wantsRun = false;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				// その間：今の状態を保つ
				intent.runGraceTimer = 0.0f;
			}

			// ボタンの意図
			UpdateAction(intent.jump, input.jump.pressed, input.jump.held, intent.jumpBufferTime, elapsedTime);
			UpdateAction(intent.dodge, input.dodge.pressed, input.dodge.held, intent.dodgeBufferTime, elapsedTime);
		});
}

===== PlayerJumpSystem.cpp =====
Path: C:\Users\offri\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Game\ECS\Systems\PlayerJumpSystem.cpp
#include "PlayerJumpSystem.h"
#include <algorithm>

#include "Game/ECS/Components/PlayerIntentComponent.h"
#include "Game/ECS/Components/PlayerJumpComponent.h"
#include "Game/ECS/Components/PlayerLocomotionComponent.h"

#include "Engine/ECS/Components/StateMachineComponent.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/MovementComponent.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Systems/StateMachineSystem.h"

PlayerJumpSystem::PlayerJumpSystem()
{
	StateMachineSystem::RequireState<PlayerJumpComponent>("Jump", "PlayerJumpSystem");
}

void PlayerJumpSystem::Update(entt::registry& registry, float elapsedTime, const SceneContext& context)
{
	(void)elapsedTime;
	(void)context;

	auto view = registry.view<PlayerIntentComponent, PlayerJumpComponent, StateMachineComponent, PlayerLocomotionComponent, MovementComponent>();
	view.each([&](PlayerIntentComponent& intent, PlayerJumpComponent& jump, const StateMachineComponent& stateMachine,
		PlayerLocomotionComponent& loco, MovementComponent& movement)
		{
			if (!stateMachine.IsIn("Jump")) return;

			// 入った瞬間：ジャンプ入力があり、まだ宙に浮いていなければ跳ぶ。そうでなければ落ちるだけ
			if (stateMachine.enteredThisFrame)
			{
				jump.fromGround = intent.jump.buffered && !loco.isAirborne;
				jump.takeoffSpeed = loco.horizontalSpeed;

				if (jump.fromGround)
				{
					// 1回限りの初速は、ステートが直接書いてよい(以降は重力に任せる)
					movement.velocity.y = jump.jumpSpeed;
					intent.jump.Consume();
				}
			}

			// 毎フレーム：空中の指示
			LocomotionCommand& cmd = loco.command;
			cmd.mode = LocomotionMode::Air;
			cmd.moveDir = intent.wantsMove ? intent.moveDir : DirectX::XMFLOAT3{ 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f };
			cmd.targetSpeed = (std::max)(jump.takeoffSpeed, jump.airSpeed); // 踏み切りの勢いを空中で失わないように
			cmd.accelHalfLife = jump.airControlHalfLife;
			cmd.turnSpeed = jump.airTurnSpeed;

			//空中は当たり判定を太くする
			movement.radiusOverride = jump.airRadius;
		});
}

===== PlayerLocomotionSystem.cpp =====
Path: C:\Users\offri\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Game\ECS\Systems\PlayerLocomotionSystem.cpp
#include "PlayerLocomotionSystem.h"
#include <cmath>
#include <algorithm>
#include <DirectXMath.h>

#include "Game/ECS/Components/PlayerLocomotionComponent.h"
#include "Game/ECS/Systems/PlayerMovementUtils.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/MovementComponent.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/TransformComponent.h"

namespace
{
	// 指数的な減衰は0に届かないので、これより遅くなったら0にする
	constexpr float kStopSpeed = 0.05f;

	float PlanarLength(float x, float z) { return sqrtf(x * x + z * z); }

	bool HasDirection(const DirectX::XMFLOAT3& dir) { return PlanarLength(dir.x, dir.z) > 0.0001f; }

	// 指示された方向へ体を回し、回った後の向きのずれ(rad)を返す
	float TurnTowards(const LocomotionCommand& cmd, TransformComponent& transform, float elapsedTime)
	{
		if (!HasDirection(cmd.moveDir)) return 0.0f;

		float targetYaw = PlayerMovementUtils::CalcFacingYaw(cmd.moveDir.x, cmd.moveDir.z);
		transform.rotation.y = PlayerMovementUtils::RotateTowards(transform.rotation.y, targetYaw, cmd.turnSpeed, elapsedTime);
		return PlayerMovementUtils::AngleDiff(transform.rotation.y, targetYaw);
	}

	// Ground に入る時：実際の速度を「体の向きに沿った速さ」と「持ち越しの速度」に分けて取り込む
	void AbsorbIntoGround(PlayerLocomotionComponent& loco, const MovementComponent& movement, const TransformComponent& transform)
	{
		DirectX::XMFLOAT3 forward = PlayerMovementUtils::YawToForward(transform.rotation.y);

		// 体の向きに沿った成分(後ろ向きは0とする)
		float along = movement.velocity.x * forward.x + movement.velocity.z * forward.z;
		loco.currentSpeed = (std::max)(0.0f, along);

		// それ以外(横・後ろ向き)は、持ち越しの速度として残す
		loco.carryVelocity.x = movement.velocity.x - forward.x * loco.currentSpeed;
		loco.carryVelocity.z = movement.velocity.z - forward.z * loco.currentSpeed;
	}

	// 地上：体の向きに沿って進む
	void DriveGround(PlayerLocomotionComponent& loco, const LocomotionCommand& cmd,
		MovementComponent& movement, TransformComponent& transform, float elapsedTime)
	{
		// 向き
		float facingError = TurnTowards(cmd, transform, elapsedTime);

		// 自分で走っている速さ
		float alignment = (std::max)(0.0f, cosf(facingError));
		float desiredSpeed = cmd.targetSpeed * alignment;

		float halfLife = (desiredSpeed > loco.currentSpeed) ? cmd.accelHalfLife : cmd.decelHalfLife;
		loco.currentSpeed = PlayerMovementUtils::Damper(loco.currentSpeed, desiredSpeed, halfLife, elapsedTime);
		if (desiredSpeed <= 0.0f && loco.currentSpeed < kStopSpeed)
		{
			loco.currentSpeed = 0.0f;
		}

		// 持ち越しの速度
		loco.carryVelocity.x = PlayerMovementUtils::Damper(loco.carryVelocity.x, 0.0f, cmd.carryHalfLife, elapsedTime);
		loco.carryVelocity.z = PlayerMovementUtils::Damper(loco.carryVelocity.z, 0.0f, cmd.carryHalfLife, elapsedTime);
		if (PlanarLength(loco.carryVelocity.x, loco.carryVelocity.z) < kStopSpeed)
		{
			loco.carryVelocity = { 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f };
		}

		// 速度 = 体の向き × 速さ ＋ 持ち越し
		DirectX::XMFLOAT3 forward = PlayerMovementUtils::YawToForward(transform.rotation.y);
		movement.velocity.x = forward.x * loco.currentSpeed + loco.carryVelocity.x;
		movement.velocity.z = forward.z * loco.currentSpeed + loco.carryVelocity.z;

		// 確認用
		loco.desiredSpeed = desiredSpeed;
		loco.facingErrorDeg = DirectX::XMConvertToDegrees(facingError);
	}

	// 空中：速度のベクトルを保つ
	void DriveAir(PlayerLocomotionComponent& loco, const LocomotionCommand& cmd,
		MovementComponent& movement, TransformComponent& transform, float elapsedTime)
	{
		// 向き
		float facingError = TurnTowards(cmd, transform, elapsedTime);

		// 速度：入力が無ければ勢いをそのまま保つ
		if (HasDirection(cmd.moveDir))
		{
			float targetX = cmd.moveDir.x * cmd.targetSpeed;
			float targetZ = cmd.moveDir.z * cmd.targetSpeed;
			movement.velocity.x = PlayerMovementUtils::Damper(movement.velocity.x, targetX, cmd.accelHalfLife, elapsedTime);
			movement.velocity.z = PlayerMovementUtils::Damper(movement.velocity.z, targetZ, cmd.accelHalfLife, elapsedTime);
		}

		// 着地した後でも参照できるように
		loco.landingHorizontalSpeed = PlanarLength(movement.velocity.x, movement.velocity.z);
		loco.landingVerticalSpeed = movement.velocity.y;

		// 確認用
		loco.currentSpeed = loco.landingHorizontalSpeed;
		loco.desiredSpeed = cmd.targetSpeed;
		loco.facingErrorDeg = DirectX::XMConvertToDegrees(facingError);
	}

	// 向きとは別の方向に進む：体の向きは変えず、速度だけを指示された方向へ寄せる
	void DriveDirectional(PlayerLocomotionComponent& loco, const LocomotionCommand& cmd,
		MovementComponent& movement, float elapsedTime)
	{
		// 速度：指示された方向 × 速さ へダンパーで近づける
		float targetX = cmd.moveDir.x * cmd.targetSpeed;
		float targetZ = cmd.moveDir.z * cmd.targetSpeed;
		movement.velocity.x = PlayerMovementUtils::Damper(movement.velocity.x, targetX, cmd.accelHalfLife, elapsedTime);
		movement.velocity.z = PlayerMovementUtils::Damper(movement.velocity.z, targetZ, cmd.accelHalfLife, elapsedTime);

		// 確認用
		loco.currentSpeed = PlanarLength(movement.velocity.x, movement.velocity.z);
		loco.desiredSpeed = cmd.targetSpeed;
		loco.facingErrorDeg = 0.0f;
	}
}

void PlayerLocomotionSystem::Update(entt::registry& registry, float elapsedTime)
{
	auto view = registry.view<PlayerLocomotionComponent, MovementComponent, TransformComponent>();
	view.each([&](PlayerLocomotionComponent& loco, MovementComponent& movement, TransformComponent& transform)
		{
			const LocomotionCommand& cmd = loco.command;

			// 体の状態：着地した回数
			if (movement.isGrounded && loco.airTime > 0.0f)
			{
				loco.landingCount++;
			}
			// 体の状態：接地していない時間
			loco.airTime = movement.isGrounded ? 0.0f : loco.airTime + elapsedTime;
			loco.isAirborne = (loco.airTime >= loco.fallGraceTime);

			// 指示があれば、向きを進む方向へ一気に合わせる
			if (HasDirection(cmd.faceDir))
			{
				transform.rotation.y = PlayerMovementUtils::CalcFacingYaw(cmd.faceDir.x, cmd.faceDir.z);
			}
			else if (cmd.faceMoveDir && HasDirection(cmd.moveDir))
			{
				transform.rotation.y = PlayerMovementUtils::CalcFacingYaw(cmd.moveDir.x, cmd.moveDir.z);
			}

			// 他のモードから Ground に切り替わった時だけ、実際の速度を取り込む
			if (cmd.mode == LocomotionMode::Ground && loco.lastMode != LocomotionMode::Ground) 
			{
				AbsorbIntoGround(loco, movement, transform);
			}

			switch (cmd.mode)
			{
			case LocomotionMode::Ground:
				DriveGround(loco, cmd, movement, transform, elapsedTime);
				break;

			case LocomotionMode::Air:
				DriveAir(loco, cmd, movement, transform, elapsedTime);
				break;

			case LocomotionMode::Directional:
				DriveDirectional(loco, cmd, movement, elapsedTime);
				break;

			case LocomotionMode::External:
				// velocity.xz は外部が書く
				loco.desiredSpeed = 0.0f;
				loco.facingErrorDeg = 0.0f;
				break;
			}

			// 体の状態：今の勢い
			loco.horizontalSpeed = PlanarLength(movement.velocity.x, movement.velocity.z);
			loco.verticalSpeed = movement.velocity.y;

			// 指示は1フレーム限り
			loco.lastMode = cmd.mode;
			loco.command = LocomotionCommand{};
		});
}

===== PlayerMovementUtils.cpp =====
Path: C:\Users\offri\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Game\ECS\Systems\PlayerMovementUtils.cpp
#include "PlayerMovementUtils.h"
#include <cmath>

namespace PlayerMovementUtils
{
	WorldVelocityResult StickToWorldVelocity(
		float inputX, float inputZ,
		const DirectX::XMFLOAT3& cameraFront,
		const DirectX::XMFLOAT3& cameraRight,
		float maxSpeed)
	{
		WorldVelocityResult result;

		float frontLen = sqrtf(cameraFront.x * cameraFront.x + cameraFront.z * cameraFront.z);
		float rightLen = sqrtf(cameraRight.x * cameraRight.x + cameraRight.z * cameraRight.z);

		float frontX = (frontLen > 0.0f) ? cameraFront.x / frontLen : 0.0f;
		float frontZ = (frontLen > 0.0f) ? cameraFront.z / frontLen : 0.0f;
		float rightX = (rightLen > 0.0f) ? cameraRight.x / rightLen : 0.0f;
		float rightZ = (rightLen > 0.0f) ? cameraRight.z / rightLen : 0.0f;

		float dirX = frontX * inputZ + rightX * inputX;
		float dirZ = frontZ * inputZ + rightZ * inputX;
		float len = sqrtf(dirX * dirX + dirZ * dirZ);

		result.inputLength = len;
		
		if (len > 0.0f)
		{
			result.velocity.x = (dirX / len) * maxSpeed;
			result.velocity.z = (dirZ / len) * maxSpeed;
		}

		return result;
	}

	WorldVelocityResult ResolvePlanarVelocity(
		float inputX, float inputZ,
		const DirectX::XMFLOAT3& cameraFront,
		const DirectX::XMFLOAT3& cameraRight,
		float targetSpeed,
		const DirectX::XMFLOAT3& currentVelocity,
		float elapsedTime,
		float acceleration)
	{
		// 目標(方向は入力方向、大きさは targetSpeed)
		WorldVelocityResult result = StickToWorldVelocity(inputX, inputZ, cameraFront, cameraRight, targetSpeed);

		// 負なら従来どおり、即座に目標速度
		if (acceleration < 0.0f) return result;
		if (targetSpeed <= 0.0f) return result;

		// 現在の水平方向の速さ
		float currentSpeed = sqrtf(currentVelocity.x * currentVelocity.x + currentVelocity.z * currentVelocity.z);

		// 速さだけを、1フレームあたり最大 acceleration * elapsedTime ずつ目標へ近づける
		float maxStep = acceleration * elapsedTime;
		float diff = targetSpeed - currentSpeed;
		float newSpeed = (fabsf(diff) <= maxStep)
			? targetSpeed
			: currentSpeed + (diff > 0.0f ? maxStep : -maxStep);

		// 方向は入力方向のまま、大きさだけ差し替える
		float scale = newSpeed / targetSpeed;
		result.velocity.x *= scale;
		result.velocity.z *= scale;

		return result;
	}

	float CalcFacingYaw(float worldDirX, float worldDirZ)
	{
		return atan2f(-worldDirX, -worldDirZ);
	}
	float RotateTowards(float currentYaw, float targetYaw, float turnSpeed, float elapsedTime)
	{
		float diff = targetYaw - currentYaw;
		while (diff > DirectX::XM_PI)  diff -= DirectX::XM_2PI;
		while (diff < -DirectX::XM_PI) diff += DirectX::XM_2PI;

		float maxStep = turnSpeed * elapsedTime;
		if (diff > maxStep)       diff = maxStep;
		else if (diff < -maxStep) diff = -maxStep;

		float result = currentYaw + diff;

		while (result > DirectX::XM_PI)  result -= DirectX::XM_2PI;
		while (result < -DirectX::XM_PI) result += DirectX::XM_2PI;

		return result;
	}

	float AngleDiff(float fromYaw, float toYaw)
	{
		float diff = toYaw - fromYaw;
		while (diff > DirectX::XM_PI)  diff -= DirectX::XM_2PI;
		while (diff < -DirectX::XM_PI) diff += DirectX::XM_2PI;
		return diff;
	}

	DirectX::XMFLOAT3 YawToForward(float yaw)
	{
		// CalcFacingYaw が atan2(-x, -z) なので、その逆
		return { -sinf(yaw), 0.0f, -cosf(yaw) };
	}

	float MoveTowards(float current, float target, float maxStep)
	{
		float diff = target - current;
		if (fabsf(diff) <= maxStep) return target;
		return current + (diff > 0.0f ? maxStep : -maxStep);
	}

	float Damper(float current, float target, float halfLife, float elapsedTime)
	{
		if (halfLife <= 0.0f) return target;
		// 経過時間が halfLife 1つ分なら、差は 2^-1 = 半分になる
		return target + (current - target) * exp2f(-elapsedTime / halfLife);
	}
}


===== PlayerMovementUtils.h =====
Path: C:\Users\offri\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Game\ECS\Systems\PlayerMovementUtils.h
#pragma once
#include <DirectXMath.h>

// プレイヤーの移動計算
namespace PlayerMovementUtils
{
	// スティック入力とカメラの前方/右方向から、ワールド座標系の速度ベクトルと、
	// スティックの倒し具合を求める
	struct WorldVelocityResult
	{
		DirectX::XMFLOAT3 velocity{ 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f };
		float inputLength = 0.0f; // スティックの倒し具合
	};

	WorldVelocityResult StickToWorldVelocity(
		float inputX, float inputZ,
		const DirectX::XMFLOAT3& cameraFront,
		const DirectX::XMFLOAT3& cameraRight,
		float speed);

	WorldVelocityResult ResolvePlanarVelocity(
		float inputX, float inputZ,
		const DirectX::XMFLOAT3& cameraFront,
		const DirectX::XMFLOAT3& cameraRight,
		float targetSpeed,
		const DirectX::XMFLOAT3& currentVelocity,
		float elapsedTime,
		float acceleration = -1.0f);

	// ワールド座標系の移動方向から、Y軸回転角を求める
	float CalcFacingYaw(float worldDirX, float worldDirZ);

	// 滑らかに回転させる
	float RotateTowards(float currentYaw, float targetYaw, float turnSpeed, float elapsedTime);

	// fromYaw から toYaw への角度差
	float AngleDiff(float fromYaw, float toYaw);

	// Yaw から水平方向の前方ベクトルを求める
	DirectX::XMFLOAT3 YawToForward(float yaw);

	// current を target へ、最大 maxStep だけ近づける
	float MoveTowards(float current, float target, float maxStep);

	// current を target へ、指数的に近づける(halfLife 秒で差が半分になる。フレームレート非依存)
	float Damper(float current, float target, float halfLife, float elapsedTime);
}


===== PlayerMoveSystem.cpp =====
Path: C:\Users\offri\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Game\ECS\Systems\PlayerMoveSystem.cpp
#include "PlayerMoveSystem.h"
#include "Game/ECS/Components/PlayerIntentComponent.h"
#include "Game/ECS/Components/PlayerMoveComponent.h"
#include "Game/ECS/Components/PlayerLocomotionComponent.h"

#include "Engine/ECS/Components/StateMachineComponent.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Systems/StateMachineSystem.h"

PlayerMoveSystem::PlayerMoveSystem()
{
	StateMachineSystem::RequireState<PlayerMoveComponent>("Move", "PlayerMoveSystem");
}

void PlayerMoveSystem::Update(entt::registry& registry, float elapsedTime, const SceneContext& context)
{
	(void)context;

	auto view = registry.view<PlayerIntentComponent, PlayerMoveComponent, StateMachineComponent, PlayerLocomotionComponent>();
	view.each([&](const PlayerIntentComponent& intent, PlayerMoveComponent& move,
		const StateMachineComponent& stateMachine, PlayerLocomotionComponent& loco)
		{
			if (!stateMachine.IsIn("Move")) return;

			// 入った瞬間に計測をやり直す
			if (stateMachine.enteredThisFrame)
			{
				move.runTime = 0.0f;
			}

			// Run の速さに入っている間だけ時間を進める
			const bool atRunSpeed = loco.currentSpeed >= move.runSpeed * move.runReachRatio;
			move.runTime = atRunSpeed ? move.runTime + elapsedTime : 0.0f;

			// 意図を、Locomotion への指示に変える
			LocomotionCommand& cmd = loco.command;
			cmd.mode = LocomotionMode::Ground;
			cmd.moveDir = intent.moveDir;
			cmd.targetSpeed = intent.wantsRun ? move.runSpeed : move.walkSpeed;
			cmd.accelHalfLife = move.accelHalfLife;
			cmd.decelHalfLife = move.decelHalfLife;
			cmd.turnSpeed = move.turnSpeed;
		});
}

===== PlayerSlideSystem.cpp =====
Path: C:\Users\offri\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Game\ECS\Systems\PlayerSlideSystem.cpp
#include "PlayerSlideSystem.h"
#include <cmath>

#include "Game/ECS/Components/PlayerIntentComponent.h"
#include "Game/ECS/Components/PlayerSlideComponent.h"
#include "Game/ECS/Components/PlayerLocomotionComponent.h"
#include "Game/ECS/Systems/PlayerMovementUtils.h"

#include "Engine/ECS/Components/StateMachineComponent.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/MovementComponent.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/TransformComponent.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/CharacterContactComponent.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Systems/StateMachineSystem.h"

PlayerSlideSystem::PlayerSlideSystem()
{
	StateMachineSystem::RequireState<PlayerSlideComponent>("Slide", "PlayerSlideSystem");
}

void PlayerSlideSystem::Update(entt::registry& registry, float elapsedTime, const SceneContext& context)
{
	(void)context;

	auto view = registry.view<PlayerIntentComponent, PlayerSlideComponent, StateMachineComponent,
		PlayerLocomotionComponent, MovementComponent, TransformComponent, CharacterContactComponent>();

	view.each([&](PlayerIntentComponent& intent, PlayerSlideComponent& slide, const StateMachineComponent& stateMachine,
		PlayerLocomotionComponent& loco, MovementComponent& movement, const TransformComponent& transform,
		const CharacterContactComponent& contact)
		{
			if (!stateMachine.IsIn("Slide")) return;

			if (stateMachine.enteredThisFrame)
			{
				// 入った瞬間：入ってきた ○ を使い切る
				intent.dodge.Consume();
				slide.wantsStand = false;
				slide.standRequested = false;
				slide.finished = false;
				slide.elapsed = 0.0f;

				// 滑る向きと速さ：今の速度を引き継ぐ
				const float vx = movement.velocity.x;
				const float vz = movement.velocity.z;
				const float speed = sqrtf(vx * vx + vz * vz);
				if (speed > 1e-4f)
				{
					slide.slideDir = { vx / speed, 0.0f, vz / speed };
				}
				else
				{
					// 止まっている：体の向きへ滑る
					slide.slideDir = PlayerMovementUtils::YawToForward(transform.rotation.y);
				}
				slide.slideSpeed = speed;
			}
			else if (intent.dodge.buffered)
			{
				// 滑っている途中で ○：立ちたい。使い切る
				slide.wantsStand = true;
				intent.dodge.Consume();
			}

			slide.elapsed += elapsedTime;

			// 坂：滑る向きを、坂の下る向きへ少しずつ回す(急な坂ほど速く回る)
			{
				const DirectX::XMFLOAT3& n = contact.groundNormal;
				const float steepness = sqrtf(n.x * n.x + n.z * n.z); // 坂の急さ(平地は 0)
				if (steepness > 0.05f)
				{
					// 坂の下る向き(水平、長さ1)
					const float downX = n.x / steepness;
					const float downZ = n.z / steepness;

					// 今の滑る向きから見て、下る向きが何度ずれているか(右が +)
					const float rightX = slide.slideDir.z;
					const float rightZ = -slide.slideDir.x;
					const float front = downX * slide.slideDir.x + downZ * slide.slideDir.z;
					const float side = downX * rightX + downZ * rightZ;
					const float angleToDown = atan2f(side, front);

					// 下る向きが後ろ側にある(上り坂を登っている)時は回さない(振り向かない)
					float maxStep = 0.0f;
					if (front > 0.0f)
					{
						// このフレームで回れる角度(行き過ぎない)
						maxStep = slide.downhillTurnSpeed * steepness * elapsedTime;
					}
					float step = angleToDown;
					if (step > maxStep)
					{
						step = maxStep;
					}
					if (step < -maxStep)
					{
						step = -maxStep;
					}

					// 滑る向きを step だけ右へ回す(マイナスなら左)
					const float c = cosf(step);
					const float s = sinf(step);
					float newX = slide.slideDir.x * c + rightX * s;
					float newZ = slide.slideDir.z * c + rightZ * s;
					const float len = sqrtf(newX * newX + newZ * newZ);
					if (len > 1e-4f)
					{
						slide.slideDir = { newX / len, 0.0f, newZ / len };
					}
				}
			}

			// 前への速さ：入った直後は少し加速、その後は摩擦で減速
			if (slide.elapsed <= slide.boostTime)
			{
				if (slide.boostTime > 0.0f)
				{
					slide.slideSpeed += slide.boostSpeed / slide.boostTime * elapsedTime;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				slide.slideSpeed -= slide.friction * elapsedTime;
			}

			// 坂：重力のうち、滑る向きに沿った分だけ加速(上り坂なら減速)
			// 地面に当たっていない時は法線が真上なので、0 になる
			const DirectX::XMFLOAT3& n = contact.groundNormal;
			const float gravity = fabsf(movement.gravity);
			const float alongSlide = n.x * slide.slideDir.x + n.z * slide.slideDir.z;
			slide.slideSpeed += gravity * n.y * alongSlide * slide.slopeGravityScale * elapsedTime;

			// 速さを 0 〜 maxSpeed に収める
			if (slide.slideSpeed < 0.0f)
			{
				slide.slideSpeed = 0.0f;
			}
			if (slide.slideSpeed > slide.maxSpeed)
			{
				slide.slideSpeed = slide.maxSpeed;
			}

			// 終わり：加速が終わった後、遅くなったら滑り終わる(→ しゃがみ)
			slide.finished = false;
			if (slide.elapsed > slide.boostTime && slide.slideSpeed <= slide.endSpeed)
			{
				slide.finished = true;
			}

			// 終わり：急な上り坂に滑り込んだら、すぐ滑り終わる(→ しゃがみ)
			if (contact.hitGround && alongSlide < -sinf(DirectX::XMConvertToRadians(slide.uphillEndAngleDeg)))
			{
				slide.finished = true;
			}

			// 左右の操作：滑る向きは変えず、横へずらす(前後に倒した分は使わない)
			// 滑る向きの右(前が +Z のとき、右は +X)
			const float rightX = slide.slideDir.z;
			const float rightZ = -slide.slideDir.x;
			float side = 0.0f;
			if (intent.wantsMove)
			{
				// 横の成分(右が +、左が -)
				side = intent.moveDir.x * rightX + intent.moveDir.z * rightZ;
			}

			// 立つ：立ちたい かつ 頭の上が空いている(低い隙間の中なら、抜けるまで待つ)
			slide.standRequested = false;
			if (slide.wantsStand && contact.canStand)
			{
				slide.standRequested = true;
			}

			// 毎フレーム：Locomotion への指示(前へ滑る ＋ 横へずれる)
			float vx = slide.slideDir.x * slide.slideSpeed + rightX * side * slide.strafeSpeed;
			float vz = slide.slideDir.z * slide.slideSpeed + rightZ * side * slide.strafeSpeed;
			const float targetSpeed = sqrtf(vx * vx + vz * vz);
			if (targetSpeed > 1e-4f)
			{
				vx /= targetSpeed;
				vz /= targetSpeed;
			}

			LocomotionCommand& cmd = loco.command;
			cmd.mode = LocomotionMode::Directional;
			cmd.moveDir = { vx, 0.0f, vz };
			cmd.targetSpeed = targetSpeed;
			cmd.accelHalfLife = slide.accelHalfLife;
			cmd.faceMoveDir = false;
			cmd.faceDir = slide.slideDir; // 体は滑る向きへ(横へずれる分は入れない)

			// 毎フレーム：当たり判定を低くする
			movement.heightScale = slide.heightScale;
		});
}

===== PlayerSprintSystem.cpp =====

===== PlayerWallRunSystem.cpp =====
Path: C:\Users\offri\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Game\ECS\Systems\PlayerWallRunSystem.cpp
#include "PlayerWallRunSystem.h"
#include <cmath>
#include <algorithm>

#include "Game/ECS/Components/PlayerWallRunComponent.h"
#include "Game/ECS/Components/PlayerWallSenseComponent.h"
#include "Game/ECS/Components/PlayerLocomotionComponent.h"
#include "Game/ECS/Components/PlayerIntentComponent.h"

#include "Game/ECS/Systems/PlayerMovementUtils.h"

#include "Engine/ECS/Components/CharacterContactComponent.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/MovementComponent.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/TransformComponent.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/StateMachineComponent.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Systems/StateMachineSystem.h"

namespace
{
	// 水平の向きを長さ１にする
	bool NormalizeXZ(float& x, float& z)
	{
		const float len = sqrtf(x * x + z * z);
		if (len < 1e-4f) return false;
		x /= len;
		z /= len;
		return true;
	}

	// 向きから「壁へ向かう成分」を取り除き、壁の面に沿った向きにする
	bool ProjectOnWall(float& dx, float& dz, float nx, float nz)
	{
		const float d = dx * nx + dz * nz;
		dx -= d * nx;
		dz -= d * nz;
		return NormalizeXZ(dx, dz);
	}
}

PlayerWallRunSystem::PlayerWallRunSystem()
{
	StateMachineSystem::RequireState<PlayerWallRunComponent>("WallRun", "PlayerWallRunSystem");
}

void PlayerWallRunSystem::Update(entt::registry& registry, float elapsedTime, const SceneContext& context)
{
	(void)context;

	auto view = registry.view<PlayerWallRunComponent, PlayerWallSenseComponent, CharacterContactComponent,
		StateMachineComponent, PlayerLocomotionComponent, MovementComponent, TransformComponent, PlayerIntentComponent>();

	view.each([&](PlayerWallRunComponent& wallRun, const PlayerWallSenseComponent& sense, const CharacterContactComponent& contact,
		const StateMachineComponent& stateMachine, PlayerLocomotionComponent& loco, MovementComponent& movement, const TransformComponent& transform,
		PlayerIntentComponent& intent)
		{
			// 現在がWallRunでなければ何もしない
			if (!stateMachine.IsIn("WallRun")) return;

			// 壁に当たっていれば、法線を取り直す
			float nx = contact.sideNormal.x;
			float nz = contact.sideNormal.z;
			if (contact.hitSide && NormalizeXZ(nx, nz))
			{
				wallRun.wallNormal = { nx, 0.0f, nz };
			}
			const float wnx = wallRun.wallNormal.x;
			const float wnz = wallRun.wallNormal.z;

			// WallRun 開始
			if (stateMachine.enteredThisFrame)
			{
				// 走る向き：現在の速度を壁の面に沿わせる
				float dx = movement.velocity.x;
				float dz = movement.velocity.z;

				if (!ProjectOnWall(dx, dz, wnx, wnz))
				{
					// 速度から決まらない場合は体の向きを使う
					const DirectX::XMFLOAT3 forward = PlayerMovementUtils::YawToForward(transform.rotation.y);
					dx = forward.x;
					dz = forward.z;
					ProjectOnWall(dx, dz, wnx, wnz);
				}
				wallRun.runDir = { dx, 0.0f, dz };

				// 走る速さ：入ってきた勢いを保つ
				const float planarSpeed = sqrtf(movement.velocity.x * movement.velocity.x + movement.velocity.z * movement.velocity.z);
				wallRun.runSpeed = (std::max)(planarSpeed, wallRun.minRunSpeed);

				// 壁が左右どちらにあるかを決める
				wallRun.isWallRight = sense.isWallRight;
				
				// 状態を初期化
				wallRun.elapsed = 0.0f;
				wallRun.kicked = false;
				wallRun.kickTime = 0.0f;
				wallRun.finished = false;

				// 前回の壁走りから着地を挟んでいるか
				const bool chained = (loco.landingCount == wallRun.lastLandingCount);
				wallRun.lastLandingCount = loco.landingCount;

				// 始まる区間と、縦の速さを決める
				wallRun.isClimbing = false;
				wallRun.isFalling = false;
				if (!chained)
				{
					// 最初の壁走り：駆け上がらせる
					wallRun.isClimbing = true;
					wallRun.climbStartY = transform.position.y;
					movement.velocity.y = wallRun.climbSpeed;
				}
				else
				{
					// 連続の壁走り：駆け下がらせる
					wallRun.isFalling = true;
					movement.velocity.y = (std::clamp)(movement.velocity.y, wallRun.minEnterVerticalSpeed, wallRun.maxEnterVerticalSpeed);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				// 走っている向きを、今の壁の面に沿わせ直す
				float dx = wallRun.runDir.x;
				float dz = wallRun.runDir.z;
				if (ProjectOnWall(dx, dz, wnx, wnz))
				{
					wallRun.runDir = { dx, 0.0f, dz };
				}
			}

			wallRun.elapsed += elapsedTime;

			const float gravity = fabsf(movement.gravity);


			// 駆け上がりの終わりを判断する
			if (wallRun.isClimbing)
			{
				float arcHeight = 0.0f;
				const float riseGravity = gravity * wallRun.riseGravityScale;
				if (riseGravity > 0.0f)
				{
					arcHeight = wallRun.climbSpeed * wallRun.climbSpeed / (2.0f * riseGravity);
				}
				float straightHeight = wallRun.climbHeight - arcHeight;
				if (straightHeight < 0.0f)
				{
					straightHeight = 0.0f;
				}

				if (transform.position.y - wallRun.climbStartY >= straightHeight)
				{
					wallRun.isClimbing = false;
				}
			}

			// 駆け下がり中は、推進力を少しずつ下げる
			if (!wallRun.isClimbing && !wallRun.isFalling && movement.velocity.y <= 0.0f)
			{
				wallRun.isFalling = true;
			}

			// 推進力を下げ始める
			if (wallRun.isFalling)
			{
				wallRun.runSpeed *= powf(0.5f, elapsedTime / wallRun.fallSpeedHalfLife);
			}



			// 壁を蹴る
			if (!wallRun.kicked && intent.jump.buffered)
			{
				const float forward = wallRun.runSpeed * wallRun.kickForwardScale;
				movement.velocity.x = wallRun.runDir.x * forward + wnx * wallRun.kickOutSpeed;
				movement.velocity.z = wallRun.runDir.z * forward + wnz * wallRun.kickOutSpeed;
				movement.velocity.y = wallRun.kickUpSpeed;

				intent.jump.Consume();
				wallRun.kicked = true;
			}
			movement.radiusOverride = wallRun.radius;

			// 蹴った後：ロックの時間を数える
			if (wallRun.kicked)
			{
				wallRun.kickTime += elapsedTime;
			}

			// 壁蹴り後、ロック時間が過ぎた
			const bool kickDone = wallRun.kicked && wallRun.kickTime >= wallRun.kickLockTime;
			// 壁が無くなった
			const bool wallLost = !wallRun.kicked && !sense.nearWall;
			// 走る速さが一定以下になった
			const bool speedLost = !wallRun.kicked && wallRun.runSpeed < wallRun.enterMinSpeed; 
			// 上記のどれか or 地面についたら WallRun を終了する
			wallRun.finished = kickDone || wallLost || speedLost || movement.isGrounded;

			// 蹴った直後は Locomotion に触らない
			if (wallRun.kicked)
			{
				loco.command.mode = LocomotionMode::External;
				return;
			}

			// 走る指示を Locomotion に指示する
			const float stick = sense.touchingWall ? wallRun.stickSpeed : 0.0f;
			float mx = wallRun.runDir.x * wallRun.runSpeed - wnx * stick;
			float mz = wallRun.runDir.z * wallRun.runSpeed - wnz * stick;
			const float targetSpeed = sqrtf(mx * mx + mz * mz);
			NormalizeXZ(mx, mz);

			LocomotionCommand& cmd = loco.command;
			cmd.mode = LocomotionMode::Directional;
			cmd.moveDir = { mx, 0.0f, mz };
			cmd.targetSpeed = targetSpeed;
			cmd.accelHalfLife = wallRun.accelHalfLife;
			cmd.faceMoveDir = true;

			// 重力を Movement へ指示する
			if (wallRun.isClimbing)
			{
				// 駆け上がり：重力 0
				movement.gravityScale = 0.0f; 
			}
			else if (wallRun.isFalling)
			{
				// 駆け下がり：失速しながら下がる
				movement.gravityScale = wallRun.fallGravityScale;
			}
			else
			{
				// 頂上付近：上昇が弱まる
				movement.gravityScale = wallRun.riseGravityScale;
			}
		});
}

===== PlayerWallSenseSystem.cpp =====
Path: C:\Users\offri\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Game\ECS\Systems\PlayerWallSenseSystem.cpp
#include "PlayerWallSenseSystem.h"
#include <cmath>
#include <algorithm>
#include <DirectXMath.h>

#include "Game/ECS/Components/PlayerWallSenseComponent.h"

#include "Engine/ECS/Components/CharacterContactComponent.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/MovementComponent.h"

void PlayerWallSenseSystem::Update(entt::registry& registry, float elapsedTime, const SceneContext& context)
{
	(void)elapsedTime;
	(void)context;

	auto view = registry.view<PlayerWallSenseComponent, CharacterContactComponent, MovementComponent>();
	view.each([&](PlayerWallSenseComponent& sense, const CharacterContactComponent& contact, const MovementComponent& movement)
		{
			// フラグリセット
			sense.touchingWall = false;
			sense.approachAngleDeg = 0.0f;
			sense.isWallRight = false;

			// 今のフレームの壁(途中で抜けても、下の猶予の計算は必ず通る)
			do
			{
				if (!contact.hitSide) break;

				// 壁らしさ：ほぼ垂直な面だけ
				const DirectX::XMFLOAT3& n = contact.sideNormal;
				if (fabsf(n.y) > sense.maxWallNormalY) break;

				// 水平成分だけで考える
				const float nLen = sqrtf(n.x * n.x + n.z * n.z);
				if (nLen < 1e-4f) break;
				const float nx = n.x / nLen;
				const float nz = n.z / nLen;

				sense.touchingWall = true;

				const float vLen = sqrtf(movement.velocity.x * movement.velocity.x + movement.velocity.z * movement.velocity.z);
				if (vLen < 1e-4f)
				{
					// 水平に動いていない：向きが決まらないので、正面扱いにしておく
					sense.approachAngleDeg = 90.0f;
					break;
				}
				const float vx = movement.velocity.x / vLen;
				const float vz = movement.velocity.z / vLen;

				// 壁へ向かう成分(1=正面、0=平行、マイナス=離れていく)
				const float into = -(vx * nx + vz * nz);
				sense.approachAngleDeg = DirectX::XMConvertToDegrees(asinf((std::clamp)(into, -1.0f, 1.0f)));

				// 進む向きの右(前が +Z のとき、右は +X)
				const float rightX = vz;
				const float rightZ = -vx;

				// 法線は壁から外向き
				sense.isWallRight = (nx * rightX + nz * rightZ) < 0.0f;
			} while (false);

			// 猶予付き：壁から離れても、少しの間は「壁にいる」とみなす(地面の isAirborne と対)
			sense.wallLostTime = sense.touchingWall ? 0.0f : sense.wallLostTime + elapsedTime;
			sense.nearWall = sense.touchingWall || (sense.wallLostTime < sense.wallGraceTime);
		});
}
