===== StudioTimelineWidget.cpp =====
Path: C:\Users\offri\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Tool\Studio\StudioTimelineWidget.cpp
#include "StudioTimelineWidget.h"
#include "StudioContext.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/AnimatorComponent.h"
#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>

namespace
{
	const ImU32 RulerBgColor = IM_COL32(40, 42, 46, 255);
	const ImU32 TickColor = IM_COL32(120, 120, 120, 255);
	const ImU32 LabelColor = IM_COL32(200, 200, 200, 255);
	const ImU32 PinColor = IM_COL32(255, 82, 82, 255);
	const ImU32 RangeHandleColor = IM_COL32(255, 255, 255, 255);
	const ImU32 RangeHandleActiveColor = IM_COL32(255, 213, 79, 255);
}

// 目盛りを描いて、ピンのドラッグを処理する
bool StudioTimelineWidget::Begin(const char* id, float clipLength, float frameStep, float& inOutTime)
{
	ImDrawList* drawList = ImGui::GetWindowDrawList();
	const ImVec2 cursor = ImGui::GetCursorScreenPos();
	const float availWidth = ImGui::GetContentRegionAvail().x;

	left = cursor.x + LeftPadding;
	width = (std::max)(availWidth - LeftPadding - RightPadding, 1.0f);
	top = cursor.y;
	length = (std::max)(clipLength, 0.0001f);

	// 目盛りの領域をつかめるようにする
	ImGui::InvisibleButton(id, ImVec2(availWidth, PinHeadHeight + RulerHeight));

	bool changed = false;
	if (ImGui::IsItemActive())
	{
		const float t = SnapToFrame(XToTime(ImGui::GetIO().MousePos.x), frameStep, length);
		if (t != inOutTime)
		{
			inOutTime = t;
			changed = true;
		}
	}
	time = inOutTime;

	DrawRuler(drawList, frameStep);
	return changed;
}

// ドラッグしたら animator を止めてポーズだけ更新する
bool StudioTimelineWidget::BeginWithAnimator(const char* id, float clipLength, float frameStep, AnimatorComponent& animator, StudioContext& context)
{
	if (!Begin(id, clipLength, frameStep, animator.animationTime)) return false;

	// スクラブ中は止めて、ポーズだけ更新する
	animator.isPlaying = false;
	animator.isEnd = false;
	context.requestPoseUpdate = true;
	return true;
}
// animator を再生するボタン
void StudioTimelineWidget::DrawAnimatorToolbar(AnimatorComponent& animator, float clipLength, float frameStep)
{
	if (!DrawToolbar(animator.isPlaying, animator.animationTime, frameStep)) return;

	if (animator.isPlaying && animator.animationTime >= clipLength)
	{
		animator.animationTime = 0.0f;
	}
	animator.isEnd = false;
}

// トラックを描き終えたら呼ぶ
void StudioTimelineWidget::End()
{
	ImDrawList* drawList = ImGui::GetWindowDrawList();
	const float bottom = ImGui::GetCursorScreenPos().y;
	const float x = TimeToX(time);

	// 細い線：頭の下から、トラックの下端まで
	drawList->AddLine(ImVec2(x, top + PinHeadHeight), ImVec2(x, bottom), PinColor, 1.0f);

	// 頭は最後に描いて、一番手前に出す
	DrawPinHead(drawList);
}
// 再生ボタンと現在時刻
bool StudioTimelineWidget::DrawToolbar(bool& isPlaying, float time, float frameStep)
{
	bool changed = false;

	if (ImGui::Button(isPlaying ? "■" : "▶", ImVec2(28.0f, 0.0f)))
	{
		isPlaying = !isPlaying;
		changed = true;
	}

	ImGui::SameLine();
	ImGui::Text("%.3f s / F%d", time, ToFrame(time, frameStep));

	return changed;
}

// フレームに吸い付かせる
float StudioTimelineWidget::SnapToFrame(float t, float frameStep, float clipLength)
{
	if (frameStep <= 0.0f) return std::clamp(t, 0.0f, clipLength);
	return std::clamp(std::round(t / frameStep) * frameStep, 0.0f, clipLength);
}
// 時刻からフレームの番号
int StudioTimelineWidget::ToFrame(float t, float frameStep)
{
	return (frameStep > 0.0f) ? (int)std::round(t / frameStep) : 0;
}

// 重ならない範囲の並びで、index 番目が動ける所
void StudioTimelineWidget::GetSpanLimits(const std::vector<TimeSpan>& spans, int index, float clipLength, float& outLower, float& outUpper)
{
	outLower = 0.0f;
	outUpper = clipLength;
	if (index < 0 || index >= (int)spans.size()) return;

	const float start = spans[index].start;
	const float end = spans[index].end;

	for (int i = 0; i < (int)spans.size(); i++)
	{
		if (i == index) continue;
		if (spans[i].end <= start + 0.0001f) outLower = (std::max)(outLower, spans[i].end);   // 前にある範囲
		if (spans[i].start >= end - 0.0001f) outUpper = (std::min)(outUpper, spans[i].start); // 後ろにある範囲
	}
}
// pinTime に新しい範囲を追加できるか
bool StudioTimelineWidget::FindAddEnd(const std::vector<TimeSpan>& spans, float pinTime, float clipLength, float minLength, float& outEnd)
{
	const float tolerance = minLength * 0.5f; // 小数の誤差として許す量

	float end = clipLength;
	for (const auto& s : spans)
	{
		if (s.end - s.start < tolerance) continue; // カットされて長さが無くなった範囲は、無いものとして扱う

		if (pinTime >= s.start - tolerance && pinTime < s.end - tolerance) return false; // 既存の範囲の中
		if (s.start >= pinTime - tolerance) end = (std::min)(end, s.start);              // 次の範囲の始まりまで
	}

	outEnd = end;
	return (end - pinTime) >= (minLength - tolerance);
}

// 範囲の端のピン
bool StudioTimelineWidget::DrawRangeHandles(const char* id, float handleY, float frameStep, float minLength, float& inOutStart, float& inOutEnd,
	float lowerLimit, float upperLimit, float lineTopY) 
{
	ImDrawList* drawList = ImGui::GetWindowDrawList();

	// 動ける範囲と、線の上端
	const float lower = lowerLimit;
	const float upper = (upperLimit < 0.0f) ? length : upperLimit;
	const float lineTop = (lineTopY < 0.0f) ? (top + PinHeadHeight) : lineTopY;

	// 頭の位置につかめる領域を置くため、カーソルを一時的に動かす
	const ImVec2 savedCursor = ImGui::GetCursorScreenPos();
	bool changed = false;

	ImGui::PushID(id);
	for (int i = 0; i < 2; i++)
	{
		const bool isStart = (i == 0);
		float& value = isStart ? inOutStart : inOutEnd;

		ImGui::PushID(i);
		ImGui::SetCursorScreenPos(ImVec2(TimeToX(value) - RangeHandleHalfWidth, handleY));
		ImGui::InvisibleButton("##RangeHandle", ImVec2(RangeHandleHalfWidth * 2.0f, RangeHandleHeight));
		const bool active = ImGui::IsItemActive();
		const bool hovered = ImGui::IsItemHovered();
		ImGui::PopID();

		// ドラッグで動かす
		if (active && ImGui::IsMouseDragging(ImGuiMouseButton_Left))
		{
			float t = SnapToFrame(XToTime(ImGui::GetIO().MousePos.x), frameStep, length);

			// もう一方の端と、最低 minLength は空ける
			if (isStart) t = (std::min)(t, inOutEnd - minLength);
			else         t = (std::max)(t, inOutStart + minLength);
			t = std::clamp(t, lower, upper);

			if (t != value)
			{
				value = t;
				changed = true;
			}
		}

		DrawRangeHandle(drawList, TimeToX(value), handleY, lineTop, active || hovered);
	}
	ImGui::PopID();

	ImGui::SetCursorScreenPos(savedCursor);
	return changed;
}

// ピンを1本描く
void StudioTimelineWidget::DrawPin(ImDrawList* drawList, float x, float tipY, float headHeight, float radius, ImU32 color, PinDirection direction)
{
	// 頭の丸の中心と、三角形の底辺の位置
	const float sign = (direction == PinDirection::Down) ? -1.0f : 1.0f;
	const float headY = tipY + sign * (headHeight - radius);
	const float baseY = headY - sign * 2.0f;

	drawList->AddTriangleFilled(
		ImVec2(x - radius * 0.8f, baseY),
		ImVec2(x + radius * 0.8f, baseY),
		ImVec2(x, tipY),
		color);
	drawList->AddCircleFilled(ImVec2(x, headY), radius, color);
}

// 時刻とX座標の変換
float StudioTimelineWidget::TimeToX(float t) const
{
	return left + (t / length) * width;
}
float StudioTimelineWidget::XToTime(float x) const
{
	return std::clamp((x - left) / width * length, 0.0f, length);
}


// 目盛り
void StudioTimelineWidget::DrawRuler(ImDrawList* drawList, float frameStep) const
{
	const float rulerTop = top + PinHeadHeight;
	const float bottom = rulerTop + RulerHeight;
	drawList->AddRectFilled(
		ImVec2(left - LeftPadding, rulerTop),
		ImVec2(left + width + RightPadding, bottom),
		RulerBgColor);

	// フレームごとの細かい目盛り
	if (frameStep > 0.0f && width * frameStep / length >= 4.0f)
	{
		const int frameCount = (int)std::round(length / frameStep);
		for (int i = 0; i <= frameCount; i++)
		{
			const float x = TimeToX(i * frameStep);
			drawList->AddLine(ImVec2(x, bottom - 4.0f), ImVec2(x, bottom), TickColor);
		}
	}

	// 秒の目盛り
	const float candidates[] = { 0.1f, 0.2f, 0.5f, 1.0f, 2.0f, 5.0f, 10.0f };
	float major = candidates[6];
	for (float c : candidates)
	{
		if (width * c / length >= 60.0f)
		{
			major = c;
			break;
		}
	}

	const int majorCount = (int)std::floor(length / major + 0.0001f);
	for (int i = 0; i <= majorCount; i++)
	{
		const float t = i * major;
		const float x = TimeToX(t);
		drawList->AddLine(ImVec2(x, bottom - 12.0f), ImVec2(x, bottom), TickColor);

		char label[16];
		snprintf(label, sizeof(label), "%.1f", t);
		drawList->AddText(ImVec2(x + 3.0f, rulerTop + 2.0f), LabelColor, label);
	}
}

// ピンの頭
void StudioTimelineWidget::DrawPinHead(ImDrawList* drawList) const
{
	// 再生位置のピン：下向き、とがった先が目盛りの帯の上端
	DrawPin(drawList, TimeToX(time), top + PinHeadHeight, PinHeadHeight, 6.0f, PinColor, PinDirection::Down);
}
void StudioTimelineWidget::DrawRangeHandle(ImDrawList* drawList, float x, float handleY, float lineTopY, bool highlighted) const
{
	const ImU32 color = highlighted ? RangeHandleActiveColor : RangeHandleColor;

	// 線：頭のとがった先から、指定された上端まで
	drawList->AddLine(ImVec2(x, handleY), ImVec2(x, lineTopY), color, 1.5f);

	// 頭：上向き、とがった先が帯の下端
	DrawPin(drawList, x, handleY, RangeHandleHeight, 6.0f, color, PinDirection::Up);
}



===== StudioTimelineWidget.h =====
Path: C:\Users\offri\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Tool\Studio\StudioTimelineWidget.h
#pragma once
#include <imgui.h>
#include <vector>

struct AnimatorComponent;
struct StudioContext;

class StudioTimelineWidget
{
public:
	// 目盛りを描いて、ピンのドラッグを処理する
	bool Begin(const char* id, float clipLength, float frameStep, float& inOutTime);
	
	// ドラッグしたら animator を止めてポーズだけ更新する
	bool BeginWithAnimator(const char* id, float clipLength, float frameStep, AnimatorComponent& animator, StudioContext& context);
	// animator を再生するボタン
	static void DrawAnimatorToolbar(AnimatorComponent& animator, float clipLength, float frameStep);

	// トラックを描き終えたら呼ぶ
	void End();

	// 再生ボタンと現在時刻
	static bool DrawToolbar(bool& isPlaying, float time, float frameStep);
	// フレームに吸い付かせる
	static float SnapToFrame(float t, float frameStep, float clipLength);

	// 範囲の最低の長さ
	static float MinLength(float frameStep) { return (frameStep > 0.0f) ? frameStep : 0.001f; }
	// 時刻からフレームの番号
	static int ToFrame(float t, float frameStep);

	// タイムライン上の範囲
	struct TimeSpan
	{
		float start = 0.0f;
		float end = 0.0f;
	};

	// 重ならない範囲の並びで、index 番目が動ける所
	static void GetSpanLimits(const std::vector<TimeSpan>& spans, int index, float clipLength, float& outLower, float& outUpper);
	// pinTime に新しい範囲を追加できるか
	static bool FindAddEnd(const std::vector<TimeSpan>& spans, float pinTime, float clipLength, float minLength, float& outEnd);

	// 範囲の端のピン
	bool DrawRangeHandles(const char* id, float handleY, float frameStep, float minLength, float& inOutStart, float& inOutEnd,
		float lowerLimit = 0.0f, float upperLimit = -1.0f, float lineTopY = -1.0f);

	// ピンの向き
	enum class PinDirection
	{
		Down, // 頭が上、とがった先が下
		Up,   // 頭が下、とがった先が上
	};

	// ピンを1本描く
	static void DrawPin(ImDrawList* drawList, float x, float tipY, float headHeight, float radius, ImU32 color, PinDirection direction);

	static constexpr float RangeHandleHeight = 16.0f; // 範囲の端のピンの頭の高さ

	// 時刻とX座標の変換
	float TimeToX(float t) const;
	float XToTime(float x) const;

	// トラックを描ける左右の端
	float GetLeft() const { return left; }
	float GetRight() const { return left + width; }

private:
	static constexpr float PinHeadHeight = 16.0f; // 目盛りの上に、ピンの頭を置く余白
	static constexpr float RulerHeight = 24.0f;   // 目盛りの帯の高さ
	static constexpr float LeftPadding = 12.0f;   // 左の余白
	static constexpr float RightPadding = 28.0f;  // 右の余白

	float left = 0.0f;   // 0秒の位置
	float width = 1.0f;  // 0秒から最後までの幅
	float top = 0.0f;    // 目盛りの上端
	float length = 1.0f; // クリップの長さ
	float time = 0.0f;   // ピンの時刻

	// 目盛り
	void DrawRuler(ImDrawList* drawList, float frameStep) const;
	// ピンの頭
	void DrawPinHead(ImDrawList* drawList) const;
	void DrawRangeHandle(ImDrawList* drawList, float x, float handleY, float lineTopY, bool highlighted) const;

	// 範囲の端のピンをつかめる幅の半分
	static constexpr float RangeHandleHalfWidth = 7.0f;
};


===== StudioWindow.cpp =====
Path: C:\Users\offri\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Tool\Studio\StudioWindow.cpp
#include "StudioWindow.h"
#include <imgui.h>
#include <imgui_internal.h>
#include <cassert>
#include <cmath>

namespace
{
	// 最大化したときの広げる先を求める
	void GetMaximizeArea(ImVec2 windowPos, ImVec2 windowSize, ImVec2& outPos, ImVec2& outSize)
	{
		// アプリの中にいる
		ImGuiViewport* mainViewport = ImGui::GetMainViewport();
		if (ImGui::GetWindowViewport() == mainViewport)
		{
			outPos = mainViewport->WorkPos;
			outSize = mainViewport->WorkSize;
			return;
		}

		// アプリの外：ウィンドウの中心があるモニターを探す
		ImVec2 center(windowPos.x + windowSize.x * 0.5f, windowPos.y + windowSize.y * 0.5f);
		const ImGuiPlatformIO& platformIO = ImGui::GetPlatformIO();
		for (const ImGuiPlatformMonitor& monitor : platformIO.Monitors)
		{
			bool inside =
				center.x >= monitor.MainPos.x && center.x < monitor.MainPos.x + monitor.MainSize.x &&
				center.y >= monitor.MainPos.y && center.y < monitor.MainPos.y + monitor.MainSize.y;
			if (inside)
			{
				outPos = monitor.WorkPos;
				outSize = monitor.WorkSize;
				return;
			}
		}

		// 見つからなければ最初のモニター
		if (!platformIO.Monitors.empty())
		{
			outPos = platformIO.Monitors[0].WorkPos;
			outSize = platformIO.Monitors[0].WorkSize;
			return;
		}

		// モニターの情報が無い場合は、アプリのウィンドウ
		outPos = mainViewport->WorkPos;
		outSize = mainViewport->WorkSize;
	}

	// 2つの値がほぼ同じか
	bool NearlyEqual(ImVec2 a, ImVec2 b)
	{
		return std::fabs(a.x - b.x) < 1.0f && std::fabs(a.y - b.y) < 1.0f;
	}
}

void StudioWindow::Initialize(ID3D11Device* device, StudioSharedContext* shared, UINT width, UINT height)
{
	renderWidth = width;
	renderHeight = height;
	context.device = device;
	this->shared = shared;

	renderSystem.Initialize(device);
	postProcess.Initialize(device);
	lightManager.Initialize(device);

	// IBLテクスチャセット
	renderSystem.SetIBLTextures(
		shared->skyBox.GetDiffuseIem(),
		shared->skyBox.GetSRV(),
		shared->skyBox.GetLutGgx()
	);

	stadoiModelViewer.Initialize(device, &shared->resourceManager);
	stateGraph.Initialize();

	ResizeRenderTarget();
}

// 更新
void StudioWindow::Update(float elapsedTime)
{
	// カメラ更新
	camera.Update(viewportHovered, elapsedTime);

	// モデルビュワー更新
	stadoiModelViewer.Update(elapsedTime, context);

	// アニメーション速度更新
	animationSpeedSystem.Update(studioRegistry);

	if (context.requestPoseUpdate)
	{
		// スクラブ操作時は時間を進めずポーズだけ更新
		animationSystem.EvaluatePose(studioRegistry);
		context.requestPoseUpdate = false;
	}
	else
	{
		animationSystem.Update(studioRegistry, elapsedTime);
	}
}

// 描画
void StudioWindow::Render()
{
	// ホバー中は毎フレーム、離れたら1/10
	if (!isHovered)
	{
		renderSkipCount++;
		if (renderSkipCount < 10) return;
		renderSkipCount = 0;
	}
	else
	{
		renderSkipCount = 0;
	}

	ID3D11DeviceContext* dc = context.deviceContext;
	if (!dc) return;

	// 描画先を自前RTVに切り替え
	Begin();

	// CbScene更新
	CbScene cbSceneData{};
	cbSceneData.view = camera.GetView();
	cbSceneData.projection = camera.GetProjection();
	const auto& eye = camera.GetEye();
	cbSceneData.cameraPosition = { eye.x, eye.y, eye.z, 0.0f };

	dc->UpdateSubresource(renderSystem.GetCbScene(), 0, nullptr, &cbSceneData, 0, 0);
	dc->VSSetConstantBuffers(0, 1, renderSystem.GetCbSceneAddress());
	dc->PSSetConstantBuffers(0, 1, renderSystem.GetCbSceneAddress());

	// ライトをGPUに転送
	lightManager.Bind(dc);

	// フラスタム更新
	renderSystem.SetFrustum(camera.GetView(), camera.GetProjection());

	// スカイボックス描画
	shared->skyBox.Render(
		dc,
		camera.GetView(),
		camera.GetProjection(),
		camera.GetEye()
	);

	// モデルがある時だけRenderSystemを走らせる
	if (!studioRegistry.storage<TransformComponent>().empty())
	{
		// 描画
		renderSystem.Render(
			studioRegistry,
			dc,
			rtv.Get(),
			dsv.Get(),
			context.device,
			renderWidth,
			renderHeight
		);
	}

	// 最終パス：シーン（HDR）を読んで、表示用へ書き出す
	static const PostProcessSettings kDefaultSettings{};
	const PostProcessSettings& settings = (shared && shared->postProcessSettings) ? *shared->postProcessSettings : kDefaultSettings;
	postProcess.Render(dc, srv.Get(), outputRtv.Get(), renderWidth, renderHeight, settings);

	// 描画先をメインに戻す
	End();
}

// ImGui描画
void StudioWindow::DrawWindow(const char* windowName)
{
	// 最大化・元に戻すの予約があれば、Beginの前に位置と大きさを伝える
	if (applyWindowRect)
	{
		ImGui::SetNextWindowPos(applyPos, ImGuiCond_Always);
		ImGui::SetNextWindowSize(applySize, ImGuiCond_Always);
		applyWindowRect = false;
	}


	bool open = true;
	ImGui::Begin(windowName, &open, ImGuiWindowFlags_MenuBar);
	if (!open) closeRequested = true;

	// Studioがいるビューポート
	context.studioViewportId = ImGui::GetWindowViewport()->ID;

	// 最大化中に、ドラッグで動かした・大きさを変えた・ドッキングしたら、最大化を外す
	if (maximized)
	{
		bool changed = !NearlyEqual(ImGui::GetWindowPos(), applyPos) || !NearlyEqual(ImGui::GetWindowSize(), applySize);
		if (changed || ImGui::IsWindowDocked()) maximized = false;
	}

	// 右端：最大化ボタン
	DrawMaximizeButton();

	// Studioウィンドウ全体でホバー判定
	isHovered = ImGui::IsWindowHovered(ImGuiHoveredFlags_ChildWindows | ImGuiHoveredFlags_DockHierarchy);

	// メニューバー
	if (ImGui::BeginMenuBar())
	{
		if (ImGui::BeginMenu("Window"))
		{
			ImGui::MenuItem("Animator", nullptr, &context.showAnimator);
			ImGui::MenuItem("Timeline", nullptr, &context.showTimeline);
			ImGui::MenuItem("Sequencer", nullptr, &context.showSequencer);
			ImGui::MenuItem("State Graph", nullptr, &context.showStateGraph);
			ImGui::MenuItem("AnimationSpeed", nullptr, &context.showAnimationSpeed);
			ImGui::MenuItem("Animation Edit", nullptr, &context.showAnimationEdit);

			ImGui::EndMenu();
		}
		ImGui::EndMenuBar();
	}

	// このStudio専用のDockSpace
	std::string dockSpaceName = "StudioDockSpace##" + std::to_string(windowId);
	ImGuiID dockSpaceId = ImGui::GetID(dockSpaceName.c_str());
	ImGui::DockSpace(dockSpaceId, ImVec2(0.0f, 0.0f), ImGuiDockNodeFlags_None);

	// 初回フレームだけデフォルトレイアウトを組む
	if (firstDockSetup)
	{
		firstDockSetup = false;

		ImGui::DockBuilderRemoveNode(dockSpaceId);
		ImGui::DockBuilderAddNode(dockSpaceId, ImGuiDockNodeFlags_None);
		ImGui::DockBuilderSetNodeSize(dockSpaceId, ImVec2(1280, 720));

		ImGuiID dockMain = dockSpaceId;
		ImGuiID dockLeft = ImGui::DockBuilderSplitNode(
			dockMain, ImGuiDir_Left, 0.2f, nullptr, &dockMain);
		ImGuiID dockRight = ImGui::DockBuilderSplitNode(
			dockMain, ImGuiDir_Right, 0.7f, nullptr, &dockMain);
		ImGuiID dockBottom = ImGui::DockBuilderSplitNode(
			dockMain, ImGuiDir_Down, 0.2f, nullptr, &dockMain);
		ImGuiID dockSequencer = ImGui::DockBuilderSplitNode(
			dockBottom, ImGuiDir_Down, 0.5f, nullptr, &dockBottom);

		std::string viewportName = "Viewport##" + std::to_string(windowId);
		std::string animatorName = "Animator##" + std::to_string(windowId);
		std::string timelineName = "Timeline##" + std::to_string(windowId);
		std::string sequencerName = "Sequencer##" + std::to_string(windowId);
		std::string stateGraphName = "StateGraph##" + std::to_string(windowId);
		std::string animationSpeedName = "Animation Speed##" + std::to_string(windowId);

		ImGui::DockBuilderDockWindow(viewportName.c_str(), dockMain);
		ImGui::DockBuilderDockWindow(animatorName.c_str(), dockLeft);
		ImGui::DockBuilderDockWindow(timelineName.c_str(), dockBottom);
		ImGui::DockBuilderDockWindow(sequencerName.c_str(), dockSequencer);
		ImGui::DockBuilderDockWindow(stateGraphName.c_str(), dockRight);
		ImGui::DockBuilderDockWindow(animationSpeedName.c_str(), dockSequencer);

		ImGui::DockBuilderFinish(dockSpaceId);
	}

	// Viewportパネル
	std::string viewportName = "Viewport##" + std::to_string(windowId);
	ImGui::Begin(viewportName.c_str());

	// 画質確認
	// ImGui::Text("Render Resolution: %ux%u", renderWidth, renderHeight);

	ImVec2 region = ImGui::GetContentRegionAvail();
	if (region.x > 1 && region.y > 1)
	{
		camera.UpdateAspect(region.x, region.y);
	}

	ImGui::Image(reinterpret_cast<ImTextureID>(outputSrv.Get()), region);

	// モデルのドロップを受け取る
	if (ImGui::BeginDragDropTarget())
	{
		if (const ImGuiPayload* payload = ImGui::AcceptDragDropPayload("MODEL_PATH"))
		{
			std::string droppedModelPath(static_cast<const char*>(payload->Data));
			LoadAsset(droppedModelPath);
		}
		ImGui::EndDragDropTarget();
	}

	// カメラ操作の開始判定はViewport単体のホバーだけを見る
	viewportHovered = ImGui::IsWindowHovered();

	ImGui::End(); // Viewport

	// Animatorパネル
	if (context.showAnimator)
	{
		studioAnimator.DrawGUI(studioRegistry, context);
	}

	// Timelineパネル
	if (context.showTimeline)
	{
		studioTimeline.DrawGUI(studioRegistry, context);
	}

	// Sequencerパネル
	if (context.showSequencer)
	{
		studioSequencer.DrawGUI(studioRegistry, context);
	}

	// State Graphパネル
	if (context.showStateGraph)
	{
		stateGraph.DrawGUI(studioRegistry, context);
	}

	// AnimationSpeedパネル
	if (context.showAnimationSpeed)
	{
		studioAnimationSpeed.DrawGUI(studioRegistry, context);
	}
	
	// Animation Editパネル
	if (context.showAnimationEdit)
	{
		studioAnimationEdit.DrawGUI(studioRegistry, context);
	}
	else
	{
		studioAnimationEdit.OnHidden(studioRegistry, context);
	}

	stadoiModelViewer.DrawGUI(studioRegistry, context);

	ImGui::End(); // Studio
}

// アセット読み込み
void StudioWindow::LoadAsset(const std::string& assetPath)
{
	stadoiModelViewer.LoadModel(studioRegistry, assetPath);
	context.currentModelPath = assetPath;

	// モデルが変わったことを伝える
	stateGraph.OnModelChanged(context);
}

void StudioWindow::Begin()
{
	ID3D11DeviceContext* dc = context.deviceContext;
	if (!dc) return;

	// 現在のビューポートを保存
	UINT numVP = 1;
	dc->RSGetViewports(&numVP, &savedViewport);

	// 自前のビューポートをセット
	D3D11_VIEWPORT vp{};
	vp.Width = static_cast<float>(renderWidth);
	vp.Height = static_cast<float>(renderHeight);
	vp.MinDepth = 0.0f;
	vp.MaxDepth = 1.0f;
	dc->RSSetViewports(1, &vp);

	// 描画先を自前RTVに切り替え
	dc->OMSetRenderTargets(1, rtv.GetAddressOf(), dsv.Get());

	// クリア
	float clear[4] = { 0.15f, 0.15f, 0.15f, 1.0f };
	dc->ClearRenderTargetView(rtv.Get(), clear);
	dc->ClearDepthStencilView(dsv.Get(), D3D11_CLEAR_DEPTH | D3D11_CLEAR_STENCIL, 1.0f, 0);
}
void StudioWindow::End()
{
	ID3D11DeviceContext* dc = context.deviceContext;
	if (!dc) return;

	// ビューポートを元に戻す
	dc->RSSetViewports(1, &savedViewport);

	// 描画先をメインに戻す
	ID3D11RenderTargetView* nullRTV = nullptr;
	dc->OMSetRenderTargets(1, &nullRTV, nullptr);
}
void StudioWindow::ResizeRenderTarget()
{
	ID3D11Device* device = context.device;
	if (!device) return;
	if (renderWidth == 0 || renderHeight == 0) return;

	// 古いリソースを解放
	srv.Reset();
	rtv.Reset();
	renderTexture.Reset();
	dsv.Reset();
	depthTexture.Reset();
	outputSrv.Reset();
	outputRtv.Reset();
	outputTexture.Reset();

	HRESULT hr;

	// カラーテクスチャ再生成
	{
		D3D11_TEXTURE2D_DESC desc{};
		desc.Width = renderWidth;
		desc.Height = renderHeight;
		desc.MipLevels = 1;
		desc.ArraySize = 1;
		desc.Format = DXGI_FORMAT_R16G16B16A16_FLOAT;
		desc.SampleDesc.Count = 1;
		desc.Usage = D3D11_USAGE_DEFAULT;
		desc.BindFlags = D3D11_BIND_RENDER_TARGET | D3D11_BIND_SHADER_RESOURCE;

		hr = device->CreateTexture2D(&desc, nullptr, renderTexture.GetAddressOf());
		assert(SUCCEEDED(hr));
		hr = device->CreateRenderTargetView(renderTexture.Get(), nullptr, rtv.GetAddressOf());
		assert(SUCCEEDED(hr));
		hr = device->CreateShaderResourceView(renderTexture.Get(), nullptr, srv.GetAddressOf());
		assert(SUCCEEDED(hr));
	}

	// 深度テクスチャ再生成
	{
		D3D11_TEXTURE2D_DESC desc{};
		desc.Width = renderWidth;
		desc.Height = renderHeight;
		desc.MipLevels = 1;
		desc.ArraySize = 1;
		desc.Format = DXGI_FORMAT_D24_UNORM_S8_UINT;
		desc.SampleDesc.Count = 1;
		desc.Usage = D3D11_USAGE_DEFAULT;
		desc.BindFlags = D3D11_BIND_DEPTH_STENCIL;

		hr = device->CreateTexture2D(&desc, nullptr, depthTexture.GetAddressOf());
		assert(SUCCEEDED(hr));
		hr = device->CreateDepthStencilView(depthTexture.Get(), nullptr, dsv.GetAddressOf());
		assert(SUCCEEDED(hr));
	}

	// 表示用テクスチャ再生成
	{
		D3D11_TEXTURE2D_DESC desc{};
		desc.Width = renderWidth;
		desc.Height = renderHeight;
		desc.MipLevels = 1;
		desc.ArraySize = 1;
		desc.Format = DXGI_FORMAT_R8G8B8A8_UNORM;
		desc.SampleDesc.Count = 1;
		desc.Usage = D3D11_USAGE_DEFAULT;
		desc.BindFlags = D3D11_BIND_RENDER_TARGET | D3D11_BIND_SHADER_RESOURCE;

		hr = device->CreateTexture2D(&desc, nullptr, outputTexture.GetAddressOf());
		assert(SUCCEEDED(hr));
		hr = device->CreateRenderTargetView(outputTexture.Get(), nullptr, outputRtv.GetAddressOf());
		assert(SUCCEEDED(hr));
		hr = device->CreateShaderResourceView(outputTexture.Get(), nullptr, outputSrv.GetAddressOf());
		assert(SUCCEEDED(hr));
	}
}

// 最大化ボタン
void StudioWindow::DrawMaximizeButton()
{
	ImGuiWindow* window = ImGui::GetCurrentWindow();

	// ドッキング中はタイトルバーがタブになるので出さない
	if (window->DockIsActive || (window->Flags & ImGuiWindowFlags_NoTitleBar)) return;

	ImGuiStyle& style = ImGui::GetStyle();
	ImRect titleBar = window->TitleBarRect();

	// ×ボタンと同じ大きさで、×の左に並べる(ImGuiが×を置く位置の計算に合わせる)
	float buttonSize = ImGui::GetFontSize();
	float closeX = titleBar.Max.x - window->WindowBorderSize - style.FramePadding.x - buttonSize;
	ImVec2 pos(closeX - style.ItemInnerSpacing.x - buttonSize, titleBar.Min.y + style.FramePadding.y);
	ImRect bb(pos, ImVec2(pos.x + buttonSize, pos.y + buttonSize));

	// タイトルバーは中身の描画範囲の外なので、ボタンを作る間だけ範囲を広げる
	ImGui::PushClipRect(titleBar.Min, titleBar.Max, false);

	// ボタンの判定(×ボタンと同じ作り方)
	ImGuiID id = window->GetID("#MAXIMIZE");
	ImGui::ItemAdd(bb, id);
	bool hovered = false;
	bool held = false;
	bool pressed = ImGui::ButtonBehavior(bb, id, &hovered, &held);

	// マウスが乗ったら、×ボタンと同じく丸い背景を出す
	ImDrawList* drawList = window->DrawList;
	ImVec2 center = bb.GetCenter();
	if (hovered)
	{
		ImU32 bg = ImGui::GetColorU32(held ? ImGuiCol_ButtonActive : ImGuiCol_ButtonHovered);
		drawList->AddCircleFilled(center, ImMax(2.0f, buttonSize * 0.5f + 1.0f), bg);
	}

	// 印：通常は四角1つ(最大化)、最大化中は四角2つを重ねる(元に戻す)
	ImU32 color = ImGui::GetColorU32(ImGuiCol_Text);
	float half = buttonSize * 0.25f;
	if (!maximized)
	{
		drawList->AddRect(ImVec2(center.x - half, center.y - half), ImVec2(center.x + half, center.y + half), color);
	}
	else
	{
		float offset = half * 0.4f;
		drawList->AddRect(ImVec2(center.x - half + offset, center.y - half - offset), ImVec2(center.x + half + offset, center.y + half - offset), color);
		drawList->AddRect(ImVec2(center.x - half - offset, center.y - half + offset), ImVec2(center.x + half - offset, center.y + half + offset), color);
	}

	ImGui::PopClipRect();

	// 説明
	if (hovered)
	{
		ImGui::SetTooltip("%s", maximized ? "Restore" : "Maximize");
	}

	if (!pressed) return;

	if (!maximized)
	{
		// 今の位置と大きさを覚えて、広げる先を予約する
		restorePos = ImGui::GetWindowPos();
		restoreSize = ImGui::GetWindowSize();
		GetMaximizeArea(restorePos, restoreSize, applyPos, applySize);
		maximized = true;
	}
	else
	{
		// 覚えておいた位置と大きさに戻す
		applyPos = restorePos;
		applySize = restoreSize;
		maximized = false;
	}

	// 位置と大きさは、次のフレームのBeginの前に反映する
	applyWindowRect = true;
}

===== AdaptiveTriggerToolWindow.cpp =====
Path: C:\Users\offri\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Tool\AdaptiveTriggerToolWindow.cpp
#include "AdaptiveTriggerToolWindow.h"
#include <imgui.h>
#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>

namespace
{
    constexpr ImU32 AccentColor = IM_COL32(66, 150, 250, 255);   // ImGuiらしい青
    constexpr ImU32 IndicatorColor = IM_COL32(255, 70, 70, 255); // 現在地の赤線
}

void AdaptiveTriggerToolWindow::Draw(entt::registry& registry)
{
    if (!isOpen) return;

    if (!ImGui::Begin("Adaptive Trigger Tool", &isOpen))
    {
        ImGui::End();
        return;
    }

    // 最初に見つかったコントローラーを編集対象にする
    ControllerComponent* found = nullptr;
    auto view = registry.view<ControllerComponent>();
    for (auto entity : view)
    {
        found = &view.get<ControllerComponent>(entity);
        break;
    }

    if (!found)
    {
        ImGui::TextDisabled("ControllerComponent を持つエンティティがありません");
        ImGui::End();
        return;
    }
    ControllerComponent& ctrl = *found;

    if (ImGui::BeginTable("##layout", 2, ImGuiTableFlags_None))
    {
        ImGui::TableSetupColumn("main", ImGuiTableColumnFlags_WidthStretch);
        ImGui::TableSetupColumn("panel", ImGuiTableColumnFlags_WidthFixed, 160.0f);
        ImGui::TableNextRow();

        ImGui::TableSetColumnIndex(0);
        DrawTriggerSection("L2", ctrl.leftTriggerEffect, leftZone, leftRangeDrag, ctrl.leftTrigger);
        ImGui::Spacing();
        DrawTriggerSection("R2", ctrl.rightTriggerEffect, rightZone, rightRangeDrag, ctrl.rightTrigger);

        ImGui::TableSetColumnIndex(1);
        if (leftZone.selectedIndex >= 0)
        {
            DrawSelectedPointPanel(leftZone.localPoints, leftZone.selectedIndex);
        }
        else if (rightZone.selectedIndex >= 0)
        {
            DrawSelectedPointPanel(rightZone.localPoints, rightZone.selectedIndex);
        }
        else
        {
            ImGui::TextDisabled("ポイントを選択してください");
        }

        ImGui::EndTable();
    }

    ImGui::End();
}

void AdaptiveTriggerToolWindow::DrawTriggerSection(const char* label, AdaptiveTrigger::TriggerEffect& effect, ZoneState& zone, RangeDragState& rangeDrag, float currentValue)
{
    ImGui::PushID(label);

    ImGui::Text(label);

    if (!zone.exists)
    {
        ImGui::SameLine(ImGui::GetContentRegionAvail().x - 80.0f + ImGui::GetCursorPosX());
        if (ImGui::Button("+ ゾーン追加"))
        {
            zone.exists = true;
            zone.start = 0.0f;
            zone.end = 1.0f;
            zone.localPoints = { {0.0f, 0.5f}, {1.0f, 0.5f} };
        }
    }
    else
    {
        ImGui::SameLine(ImGui::GetContentRegionAvail().x - 60.0f + ImGui::GetCursorPosX());
        if (ImGui::Button("削除"))
        {
            zone.exists = false;
            zone.selectedIndex = -1;
        }
    }

    float width = ImGui::GetContentRegionAvail().x;

    DrawRangeBar(zone, width, rangeDrag, currentValue);

    ImGui::Spacing();

    if (zone.exists)
    {
        bool withinZone = (currentValue >= zone.start && currentValue <= zone.end && zone.end > zone.start);
        float localCurrent = withinZone ? (currentValue - zone.start) / (zone.end - zone.start) : 0.0f;

        int dummyOther = -1;
        DrawGraph(zone.localPoints, zone.selectedIndex, dummyOther, localCurrent, withinZone);
    }

    auto& points = effect.forcePoints;
    points.clear();
    if (zone.exists)
    {
        for (const auto& lp : zone.localPoints)
        {
            float absPos = zone.start + lp.position * (zone.end - zone.start);
            points.push_back({ absPos, lp.value });
        }
    }

    ImGui::PopID();
}

void AdaptiveTriggerToolWindow::DrawRangeBar(ZoneState& zone, float width, RangeDragState& rangeDrag, float currentValue)
{
    ImVec2 pos = ImGui::GetCursorScreenPos();
    const float height = 24.0f;
    ImDrawList* drawList = ImGui::GetWindowDrawList();

    drawList->AddRectFilled(pos, ImVec2(pos.x + width, pos.y + height), IM_COL32(60, 60, 60, 255), 4.0f);

    ImGui::InvisibleButton("##rangeBar", ImVec2(width, height));
    bool barClicked = ImGui::IsItemClicked();
    bool barActive = ImGui::IsItemActive();
    ImVec2 mousePos = ImGui::GetMousePos();

    if (zone.exists)
    {
        float x1 = pos.x + zone.start * width;
        float x2 = pos.x + zone.end * width;

        drawList->AddRectFilled(ImVec2(x1, pos.y), ImVec2(x2, pos.y + height), AccentColor);

        const float handleHalf = 3.0f;
        drawList->AddRectFilled(ImVec2(x1 - handleHalf, pos.y - 3.0f), ImVec2(x1 + handleHalf, pos.y + height + 3.0f), IM_COL32(255, 255, 255, 255));
        drawList->AddRectFilled(ImVec2(x2 - handleHalf, pos.y - 3.0f), ImVec2(x2 + handleHalf, pos.y + height + 3.0f), IM_COL32(255, 255, 255, 255));

        const float pickRange = 8.0f;

        if (barClicked)
        {
            if (std::abs(mousePos.x - x1) <= pickRange)
            {
                rangeDrag.mode = RangeDragState::Mode::Left;
            }
            else if (std::abs(mousePos.x - x2) <= pickRange)
            {
                rangeDrag.mode = RangeDragState::Mode::Right;
            }
            else if (mousePos.x >= x1 && mousePos.x <= x2)
            {
                rangeDrag.mode = RangeDragState::Mode::Move;
                rangeDrag.startMouseX = mousePos.x;
                rangeDrag.startZoneStart = zone.start;
                rangeDrag.startZoneEnd = zone.end;
            }
            else
            {
                rangeDrag.mode = RangeDragState::Mode::None;
            }
        }

        if (barActive)
        {
            float mouseNormX = std::clamp((mousePos.x - pos.x) / width, 0.0f, 1.0f);

            if (rangeDrag.mode == RangeDragState::Mode::Left)
            {
                zone.start = std::clamp(mouseNormX, 0.0f, zone.end - 0.01f);
            }
            else if (rangeDrag.mode == RangeDragState::Mode::Right)
            {
                zone.end = std::clamp(mouseNormX, zone.start + 0.01f, 1.0f);
            }
            else if (rangeDrag.mode == RangeDragState::Mode::Move)
            {
                float rawDelta = (mousePos.x - rangeDrag.startMouseX) / width;
                float zoneWidth = rangeDrag.startZoneEnd - rangeDrag.startZoneStart;
                float clampedDelta = std::clamp(rawDelta, -rangeDrag.startZoneStart, 1.0f - rangeDrag.startZoneEnd);
                zone.start = rangeDrag.startZoneStart + clampedDelta;
                zone.end = rangeDrag.startZoneStart + clampedDelta + zoneWidth;
            }
        }

        if (!ImGui::IsMouseDown(ImGuiMouseButton_Left))
        {
            rangeDrag.mode = RangeDragState::Mode::None;
        }
    }

    // 現在のトリガー押し込み位置（赤線）
    float curX = pos.x + std::clamp(currentValue, 0.0f, 1.0f) * width;
    drawList->AddLine(ImVec2(curX, pos.y - 4.0f), ImVec2(curX, pos.y + height + 4.0f), IndicatorColor, 2.0f);

    ImGui::Dummy(ImVec2(width, height));

    ImGui::TextDisabled("0");
    ImGui::SameLine(width - 24.0f);
    ImGui::TextDisabled("255");
}

void AdaptiveTriggerToolWindow::DrawGraph(std::vector<AdaptiveTrigger::CurvePoint>& points, int& selectedIndex, int& otherSelectedIndex, float localCurrentValue, bool showIndicator)
{
    const float margin = 8.0f;

    ImVec2 graphSize(ImGui::GetContentRegionAvail().x, 150.0f);
    ImVec2 graphPos = ImGui::GetCursorScreenPos();

    ImGui::InvisibleButton("##graphBg", graphSize);
    bool bgClicked = ImGui::IsItemClicked();
    bool bgActive = ImGui::IsItemActive();
    ImVec2 mousePos = ImGui::GetMousePos();

    ImDrawList* drawList = ImGui::GetWindowDrawList();
    drawList->AddRect(graphPos, ImVec2(graphPos.x + graphSize.x, graphPos.y + graphSize.y), IM_COL32(150, 150, 150, 255));

    float innerX = graphPos.x + margin;
    float innerWidth = graphSize.x - margin * 2.0f;
    float innerY = graphPos.y + margin;
    float innerHeight = graphSize.y - margin * 2.0f;

    auto ToScreen = [&](const AdaptiveTrigger::CurvePoint& p) -> ImVec2
        {
            return ImVec2(innerX + p.position * innerWidth, innerY + (1.0f - p.value) * innerHeight);
        };
    auto FromScreen = [&](ImVec2 screen) -> AdaptiveTrigger::CurvePoint
        {
            AdaptiveTrigger::CurvePoint p;
            p.position = std::clamp((screen.x - innerX) / innerWidth, 0.0f, 1.0f);
            p.value = std::clamp(1.0f - (screen.y - innerY) / innerHeight, 0.0f, 1.0f);
            return p;
        };

    for (size_t i = 0; i + 1 < points.size(); ++i)
    {
        drawList->AddLine(ToScreen(points[i]), ToScreen(points[i + 1]), AccentColor, 2.0f);
    }

    for (size_t i = 0; i < points.size(); ++i)
    {
        ImVec2 p = ToScreen(points[i]);
        bool isSelected = (static_cast<int>(i) == selectedIndex);
        float radius = isSelected ? 7.0f : 5.0f;
        drawList->AddCircleFilled(p, radius, AccentColor);
        if (isSelected)
        {
            drawList->AddCircle(p, radius + 2.0f, IM_COL32(255, 255, 255, 255), 0, 2.0f);
        }
    }

    // 現在地の赤線（ゾーン内にいる時だけ表示）
    if (showIndicator)
    {
        float curX = innerX + std::clamp(localCurrentValue, 0.0f, 1.0f) * innerWidth;
        drawList->AddLine(ImVec2(curX, graphPos.y), ImVec2(curX, graphPos.y + graphSize.y), IndicatorColor, 2.0f);
    }

    const float pickRadius = 8.0f;
    const int lastIndex = static_cast<int>(points.size()) - 1;

    if (bgClicked)
    {
        int picked = -1;
        for (size_t i = 0; i < points.size(); ++i)
        {
            ImVec2 p = ToScreen(points[i]);
            float dx = mousePos.x - p.x;
            float dy = mousePos.y - p.y;
            if ((dx * dx + dy * dy) <= (pickRadius * pickRadius))
            {
                picked = static_cast<int>(i);
                break;
            }
        }

        if (picked == -1)
        {
            AdaptiveTrigger::CurvePoint newPoint = FromScreen(mousePos);

            // 末尾の境界点(100%)の手前に挿入してから、中間部分だけ並び替える
            points.insert(points.end() - 1, newPoint);
            if (points.size() > 2)
            {
                std::sort(points.begin() + 1, points.end() - 1,
                    [](const AdaptiveTrigger::CurvePoint& a, const AdaptiveTrigger::CurvePoint& b)
                    {
                        return a.position < b.position;
                    });
            }

            // 挿入したポイントの新しいindexを探す
            for (size_t i = 0; i < points.size(); ++i)
            {
                if (points[i].position == newPoint.position && points[i].value == newPoint.value)
                {
                    picked = static_cast<int>(i);
                    break;
                }
            }
        }

        selectedIndex = picked;
        otherSelectedIndex = -1;
    }

    if (bgActive && selectedIndex >= 0 && selectedIndex < static_cast<int>(points.size()))
    {
        // 先頭・末尾（ゾーンの境界）はX固定、Yだけ動かす
        if (selectedIndex == 0)
        {
            points[selectedIndex].value = FromScreen(mousePos).value;
            points[selectedIndex].position = 0.0f;
        }
        else if (selectedIndex == lastIndex)
        {
            points[selectedIndex].value = FromScreen(mousePos).value;
            points[selectedIndex].position = 1.0f;
        }
        else
        {
            points[selectedIndex] = FromScreen(mousePos);
        }
    }

    if (!ImGui::IsMouseDown(ImGuiMouseButton_Left) &&
        selectedIndex > 0 && selectedIndex < lastIndex) // 境界点は並び替え不要
    {
        AdaptiveTrigger::CurvePoint selected = points[selectedIndex];
        std::sort(points.begin() + 1, points.end() - 1,
            [](const AdaptiveTrigger::CurvePoint& a, const AdaptiveTrigger::CurvePoint& b)
            {
                return a.position < b.position;
            });
        for (size_t i = 1; i + 1 < points.size(); ++i)
        {
            if (points[i].position == selected.position && points[i].value == selected.value)
            {
                selectedIndex = static_cast<int>(i);
                break;
            }
        }
    }
}

void AdaptiveTriggerToolWindow::DrawSelectedPointPanel(std::vector<AdaptiveTrigger::CurvePoint>& points, int& selectedIndex)
{
    if (selectedIndex < 0 || selectedIndex >= static_cast<int>(points.size()))
    {
        ImGui::TextDisabled("ポイントを選択してください");
        return;
    }

    const int lastIndex = static_cast<int>(points.size()) - 1;
    const bool isBoundary = (selectedIndex == 0 || selectedIndex == lastIndex);

    ImGui::PushID(selectedIndex);
    ImGui::TextDisabled("選択中のポイント");
    ImGui::Spacing();

    AdaptiveTrigger::CurvePoint& p = points[selectedIndex];

    ImGui::Text("X (ゾーン内位置)");
    ImGui::SetNextItemWidth(-1);
    if (isBoundary)
    {
        ImGui::BeginDisabled();
        float fixedX = p.position;
        ImGui::DragFloat("##posX", &fixedX, 0.0f, 0.0f, 1.0f, "%.3f");
        ImGui::EndDisabled();
    }
    else
    {
        ImGui::DragFloat("##posX", &p.position, 0.005f, 0.0f, 1.0f, "%.3f");
    }

    ImGui::Spacing();

    ImGui::Text("Y (強さ)");
    ImGui::SetNextItemWidth(-1);
    ImGui::DragFloat("##posY", &p.value, 0.005f, 0.0f, 1.0f, "%.3f");

    ImGui::Spacing();

    if (isBoundary)
    {
        ImGui::BeginDisabled();
        ImGui::Button("削除（境界のため不可）", ImVec2(-1, 0));
        ImGui::EndDisabled();
    }
    else if (ImGui::Button("削除", ImVec2(-1, 0)))
    {
        points.erase(points.begin() + selectedIndex);
        selectedIndex = -1;
        ImGui::PopID();
        return;
    }

    ImGui::PopID();
}


===== AdaptiveTriggerToolWindow.h =====
Path: C:\Users\offri\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Tool\AdaptiveTriggerToolWindow.h
#pragma once
#include <vector>
#include "entt.hpp"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/ControllerComponent.h"

class AdaptiveTriggerToolWindow
{
public:
    // registry から ControllerComponent を探して表示する
    void Draw(entt::registry& registry);

    bool isOpen = false; // メニューから開閉する

private:
    // ゾーンの状態
    struct ZoneState
    {
        bool  exists = false;
        float start = 0.0f;
        float end = 1.0f;
        std::vector<AdaptiveTrigger::CurvePoint> localPoints;
        int   selectedIndex = -1;
    };

    struct RangeDragState
    {
        enum class Mode { None, Left, Right, Move };
        Mode mode = Mode::None;
        float startMouseX = 0.0f;
        float startZoneStart = 0.0f;
        float startZoneEnd = 0.0f;
    };

    void DrawTriggerSection(const char* label, AdaptiveTrigger::TriggerEffect& effect, ZoneState& zone, RangeDragState& rangeDrag, float currentValue);
    void DrawRangeBar(ZoneState& zone, float width, RangeDragState& rangeDrag, float currentValue);
    void DrawGraph(std::vector<AdaptiveTrigger::CurvePoint>& points, int& selectedIndex, int& otherSelectedIndex, float localCurrentValue, bool showIndicator);
    void DrawSelectedPointPanel(std::vector<AdaptiveTrigger::CurvePoint>& points, int& selectedIndex);

    ZoneState leftZone;
    ZoneState rightZone;
    RangeDragState leftRangeDrag;
    RangeDragState rightRangeDrag;
};


===== main.cpp =====
Path: C:\Users\offri\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\main.cpp
#include <time.h>

// 起動するApp
// RUN_GAME_APP は GameApp のプロジェクト設定(プリプロセッサの定義)で定義される
// このファイルの中では定義しないこと

#ifdef RUN_GAME_APP
#include "App/GameApp.h"
using AppType = GameApp;
#else
#include "App/EditorApp.h"
using AppType = EditorApp;
#endif


#define APPLICATION_NAME L"X3DGP"

// ウィンドウサイズ
#define SCREEN_WIDTH 1280
#define SCREEN_HEIGHT 720

LRESULT CALLBACK window_procedure(HWND hwnd, UINT msg, WPARAM wparam, LPARAM lparam)
{
	Application* p{ reinterpret_cast<Application*>(GetWindowLongPtr(hwnd, GWLP_USERDATA)) };
	return p ? p->HandleMessage(hwnd, msg, wparam, lparam) : DefWindowProc(hwnd, msg, wparam, lparam);
}

int WINAPI WinMain(_In_ HINSTANCE instance, _In_opt_  HINSTANCE prev_instance, _In_ LPSTR cmd_line, _In_ int cmd_show)
{
	srand(static_cast<unsigned int>(time(nullptr)));

#if defined(DEBUG) | defined(_DEBUG)
	_CrtSetDbgFlag(_CRTDBG_ALLOC_MEM_DF | _CRTDBG_LEAK_CHECK_DF);
	//_CrtSetBreakAlloc(####);
#endif

	WNDCLASSEXW wcex{};
	wcex.cbSize = sizeof(WNDCLASSEX);
	wcex.style = CS_HREDRAW | CS_VREDRAW;
	wcex.lpfnWndProc = window_procedure;
	wcex.cbClsExtra = 0;
	wcex.cbWndExtra = 0;
	wcex.hInstance = instance;
	wcex.hIcon = 0;
	wcex.hCursor = LoadCursor(NULL, IDC_ARROW);
	wcex.hbrBackground = (HBRUSH)(COLOR_WINDOW + 1);
	wcex.lpszMenuName = NULL;
	wcex.lpszClassName = APPLICATION_NAME;
	wcex.hIconSm = 0;
	RegisterClassExW(&wcex);

	RECT rc{ 0, 0, SCREEN_WIDTH, SCREEN_HEIGHT };
	AdjustWindowRect(&rc, WS_OVERLAPPEDWINDOW, FALSE);

	//HWND hwnd = CreateWindowExW(0, APPLICATION_NAME, L"",
	//	WS_OVERLAPPEDWINDOW ^ WS_MAXIMIZEBOX ^ WS_THICKFRAME | WS_VISIBLE,
	//	CW_USEDEFAULT, CW_USEDEFAULT,
	//	rc.right - rc.left, rc.bottom - rc.top, NULL, NULL, instance, NULL);

	HWND hwnd = CreateWindowExW(0, APPLICATION_NAME, L"",
		WS_OVERLAPPEDWINDOW | WS_VISIBLE,
		CW_USEDEFAULT, CW_USEDEFAULT,
		rc.right - rc.left, rc.bottom - rc.top, NULL, NULL, instance, NULL);

	ShowWindow(hwnd, cmd_show);

	AppType app(hwnd);
	app.Initialize();
	SetWindowLongPtrW(hwnd, GWLP_USERDATA, reinterpret_cast<LONG_PTR>(&app));
	return app.Run();
}
